# Environment variable reference

Every environment variable that the Ace Combat Zero recompilation runtime (`build/zero/ac5.exe`, built from `runtime/`) and the launcher scripts at the repository root read, collected from the source on 2026-11-06. Line numbers point at the place where each variable is read and will drift as the code changes.

## How the variables are used

Every switch below is read from the process environment, almost always once (on first use) and then cached, so a change needs a restart. Export the variables in Git Bash before starting `build/zero/ac5.exe`, put them in front of a single command, or let `play_zero_vr.sh` set the common ones from its mode words. `play_zero_vr.sh` keeps a value you exported for the variables it writes as `${VAR:-default}` (`PS2_UPSCALE` outside the sharp mode, `PS2_XR_RUNTIME`, `PS2_FIELD_HZ`, `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MODEL`). It always overwrites `PS2_XR`, `PS2_XR_MODE`, `ACZ_VR_HEADLOOK` (stick mode) and `PS2_XR_MULTIVIEW` (sharp mode). Anything else you export passes straight through to the game.

**The ENHANCEMENTS settings (bug #64, 2026-10-23).** The player-facing improvements are also settings: the pause menu's ENHANCEMENTS page (View + Menu > X in the menus between missions, F4 > Enhancements on the window) saves them to `build/zero/ac5_settings.ini` (`[enhancements]`, plus the existing `[immersion]`, `[graphics] realistic_terrain` and `[input] pad_layout`). With `ACZ_MENU_SETTINGS=1`, which `play_zero_vr.sh` exports, the runtime sets each item's switch from the file at start-up (before anything reads it) unless the switch is already in the environment: a variable set by hand or by a launcher word wins for that session and the page shows it locked. Test scripts (`run_test.sh` & co.) do not set `ACZ_MENU_SETTINGS`, so their switches keep the built-in defaults listed below, whatever the user's file says. The rows, their keys, switches, defaults and when a change takes effect, the three layers (the launcher before the game, ACZ_LAUNCHER; the page; the expert keys of the file, listed by F4 > Enhancements > Expert (ini)) and the migration of the old keys: `docs/SETTINGS.md`; the table is `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c` (`enh_rows`, `enh_combo_tab`). A page row can stand for several switches (K_COMBO: EDGE SMOOTHING, TERRAIN LOOK, BUILDINGS ...): the switches below stay as they are; only the file has one key for them.

```bash
cd <the repository>

# through the launcher (VR, mission 1 straight away, native renderer, in-between frames)
./play_zero_vr.sh sharp m1 native smooth

# one-off extra variables for a single launcher run
PS2_SPEED_LOG=1 PS2_XR_DUMP_EYES=1200 ./play_zero_vr.sh m1

# by hand, without the launcher
export PS2_XR=1 PS2_XR_MODE=stereo PS2_UPSCALE=3 ACZ_BOOT_MISSION=1
ISO=<your disc image>
build/zero/ac5.exe --data gen_zero --disc "$(cygpath -w "$ISO")" --watchdog 0 > zero_play.log 2>&1

# exported variables stay set in that shell: clear them all before an unrelated test
unset $(compgen -v | grep -E '^(PS2|ACZ)_')
```

### How values are parsed

- **Presence flags** (written as `any` in the Values column): the variable only has to exist. `getenv(...) != NULL` checks and the `PS2_ENV()` macro (`runtime/include/ps2_runtime.h`) treat `VAR=0` as on too, so unset these to turn them off.
- **Default-on switches** (`0` in the Values column): the feature is on unless the value starts with `0`.
- **Opt-in switches** (`1`): only a value starting with `1` turns them on (some accept any value that does not start with `0`; those rows say "non-0").
- Numbers go through `atoi`/`atof`/`strtol(..., 0)`, so `0x` hex works where the code uses `strtol`/`strtoul` with base 0. The float parsers in `zero_vr.c` and `ps2_xr.c` (`env_float`, `envf`) accept `.` or `,` as the decimal separator.
- Tags at the start of Meaning: **[debug]** diagnostic or inspection aid, **[experimental]** opt-in feature still being evaluated, **[AC5]** only does something on Ace Combat 5 (SLUS-20851); on Zero the code checks fail and the runtime logs that it is off.

### Most useful day to day

| Variable | Typical value | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `PS2_XR` + `PS2_XR_MODE` | `1` + `stereo` | VR on, synchronous stereo (`mono`, `cinema` also exist) |
| `PS2_XR_RUNTIME` | Meta `oculus_openxr_64.json` | Pick the OpenXR runtime (the launcher uses Meta's when it is installed, else the system's active one) |
| `ACZ_DISC` | your disc image or folder | The disc for the launcher and the scripted runs |
| `PS2_UPSCALE` | `2`-`4` | Internal resolution: the main sharpness/GPU cost knob |
| `ACZ_BOOT_MISSION` | `1`..`18` | Boot straight into a campaign mission, flying ~15 s after launch |
| `PS2_RN_VP` | `1` | Native renderer for models and terrain (needed by the cockpit lighting and the smooth mode's object motion) |
| `PS2_XR_RERENDER` | `1` | Draw in-between frames at the headset rate (launcher word `smooth`) |
| `PS2_XR_MULTIVIEW` | `1` | Both eyes in one pass (launcher mode `sharp`) |
| `ACZ_VR_HEADLOOK` | `stick` | Head turns drive the game's look camera outside the cockpit |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MODEL` | `cockpits/f5e_t38.cpit` | 3D stereo cockpit in the R3 view (launcher word `cockpit`) |
| `ACZ_VR_HUD_SCALE` | `1.0`-`1.3` | HUD size (1 = original angular size) |
| `ACZ_VR_STEREO_SCALE` | `1.0` | Strength of the stereo depth |
| `PS2_FIELD_HZ` | `60` | 60.00 Hz fields so frames line up with a 120 Hz headset |
| `PS2_SPEED_LOG` | `1` | CPU/GPU timing windows every 5 s in the log |
| `PS2_SHOT_EVERY` / `_FROM` / `_MAX` | `600` / `1200` / `2` | Periodic screenshots to `out/shot_*.ppm` |
| `PS2_XR_DUMP_EYES` | field number | Write the exact left/right images the headset gets |
| `PS2_PAD_SCRIPT` | `"400:START 900:CROSS"` | Scripted button presses for unattended runs |
| `PS2_XR_TEST_HEAD` | `30,10` | Fake head turn (yaw, pitch) when testing without moving the headset |

## Launcher and boot

The debug boot (`ACZ_BOOT_*`) lives in `runtime/src/zero_vr.c` and only works on Ace Combat Zero NTSC-U (SLUS-21346): it checks the game code first and logs `zero-boot: code mismatch` otherwise. Campaign progress after a debug boot is not what a real save would hold.

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ACZ_BOOT_MISSION` | unset (normal boot) | `1`..`18` | [debug] Boot straight into campaign mission n, skipping logos, title, menus, movie and briefing | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5295` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_BRANCH` | `0` | `0`..`2` | Branch of a mission with a choice (04/08/14 operation, 03/10/16 ace variant) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5297` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_DIFF` | `2` (Normal) | `0`..`5` | Difficulty the debug boot writes into the save slot | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5298` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_STAGE` | from the mission table (mission 1 = stage 0) | stage id (hex ok) | Override the stage id written at GameControl::enter | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5299` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_AIRCRAFT` | `15` (F-5E) | aircraft id `0`..`35` | Player aircraft for the debug boot (ignored with `ACZ_BOOT_HANGAR=1`) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5300` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_WINGMAN` | `5` (Pixy's F-15C) | aircraft id (`10` = PJ's F-16C) | Wingman aircraft for the debug boot | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5301` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_HANGAR` | off | `1` | Stop at the hangar (aircraft select) instead of going straight to flight | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5302` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_SKIP` | `774` | fields; `0` keeps the intro | Press Start this many fields after the mission starts loading, skipping the in-engine intro flyby -- only while the game offers a skip then (bug #99, 2026-10-23): the intro plays (the pad player's update 0x231808 has not run), a mission-start cutscene holds the camera (player flag 0x2000) or the take-off runs (player flag 0x1000, "PRESS START BUTTON TO SKIP"); with the player flying already (M4: from field 154) or a menu up nothing is pressed (the press opened the pause menu over M4's flight at field 805); logged `zero-boot: intro skip due at field ...` | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5304` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_SKIP_CHECK` | on | `0` | ACZ_BOOT_SKIP presses Start only when the game offers a skip (an intro, a mission-start cutscene, the take-off); `0` = at the field whatever plays (before 2026-10-23) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5305` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_MOVIES` | off | `1` | [debug] With ACZ_BOOT_MISSION: keep the mission's sequence -- its story movies, mission title and briefing play as in the campaign (then the briefing waits for the pad). Mission 11, a fresh save: MOVIEUS 3, 9, 14, 15, 20, BRIEF 10 (the 2026-10-28 campaign played 3, 11, 12, 17, 18) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5303` |
| `ACZ_BOOT_SAVE_POKE` | off | `off:val[:bytes],...` (hex ok; bytes 1, 2 or 4, default 4) | [debug] (fx_mpbm 2026-10-31) Values written into the save slot together with the debug boot's aircraft (the hangar's choices the boot skips): `0x25C:1` with `ACZ_BOOT_AIRCRAFT=35` gives the Morgan its second special weapon, MPBM (8 rounds; the weapon block shows `MPBM`), instead of TLS. 0x28A548 hands the hangar record +0x254, +0x25C, +0x260 and three bytes per aircraft at +0x1E0 + 3 x id to the player's set-up; logged `zero-boot: save slot +0x...` | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:4064` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SCENES` | off | any | [debug] Log every scene change (factory, caller, depth, field) and the mission fields at flight start | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5294` |
| `PS2_SAVE_DIR` | `saves` | directory | Where the virtual memory cards live (`cardN.ps2mc`) | `runtime/src/ps2_memcard.c:94` |
| `PS2_SETTINGS_FILE` | `<exe dir>/ac5_settings.ini` | path | Settings file of the F4 menu and the ENHANCEMENTS page (gsreplay only uses a file when this is set) | `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:376`, `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:382` |
| `PS2_UI_OPEN` | closed | tab `0`..`8` | Open the F4 settings menu at start on that tab (0 Quick, 1 Display, 2 Rendering, 3 Post, 4 Keyboard, 5 Controller, 6 Analog, 7 Clouds A/B, 8 Enhancements) | `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:1252` |
| `PS2_UI_TEST` | off | words: `expert`, `tall`, `scroll=N`, `set=KEY:CHOICE`, `reset_ask` | [test] (menu_1105a, for window dumps with PS2_XR_DUMP_SCREEN: `scr_*_win.ppm` has the F4 menu) F4 > Enhancements > Expert (ini) open; the menu as tall as the window; the open tab scrolled to N pixels; a row set as a mouse would when the tab first shows (menu_1105b); F4 > Enhancements' RESET ALL opens its question, nothing is reset (2026-11-05) | `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:49` |
| `PS2_MOD_DIR` | `mods` | directory | Root folder of the mod layer | `runtime/src/ps2_mod.c:468` |
| `PS2_NO_MODS` | off | any | Turn the mod layer off | `runtime/src/ps2_mod.c:473` |
| `PS2_MOD_NAMES` | `config/pac_names.txt`, then `pac_names.txt`, `../config/pac_names.txt` | path | Name list for the members of DATA.PAC (lets mods address files by name) | `runtime/src/ps2_vfs.c:667` |
| `PS2_SHADER_DIR` | first of `shaders`, `build/gcc/shaders`, `build/shaders`, `runtime/shaders`, else the exe folder | directory | Where the compiled SPIR-V shaders (`gs.vert.spv`, ...) and the cockpit shaders are loaded from | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:5513`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:24433`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:35136` |

### Variables only the scripts read

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PS2_UPSCALE_SHARP` | unset (the ENHANCEMENTS RESOLUTION, 4X) | `1`..`8` | `play_zero_vr.sh sharp`: the `PS2_UPSCALE` used together with multiview for this session, over the menu's RESOLUTION | `play_zero_vr.sh:119` |
| `REPEAT` | `4` | count | `bench_flat.sh`: how often each field is drawn, passed on as `PS2_BENCH_REPEAT` | `bench_flat.sh:19` |
| `EXTRA_PAD` | empty | `"field:BUTTON ..."` | `run_zero_mission.sh` / `run_zero_soak.sh`: extra entries appended to their `PS2_PAD_SCRIPT` | `run_zero_mission.sh:23` |
| `ACZ_EXE` | `build/zero/ac5.exe` | path | `run_zero_mission.sh` / `run_zero_soak.sh`: the executable to run | `run_zero_mission.sh:28` |

`sweep_missions.sh` and `bench_flat.sh` read no variables of their own; they set runtime variables per run (`sweep_missions.sh`: `ACZ_BOOT_MISSION`, `PS2_RN_VP=1`, `PS2_UPSCALE=2`, `PS2_SPEED_LOG=1`, `PS2_SHOT_EVERY=600`, `PS2_SHOT_FROM=1200`, `PS2_SHOT_MAX=2`; `bench_flat.sh`: `ACZ_BOOT_MISSION=1`, `PS2_UPSCALE`, `PS2_SPEED_LOG=1`, `PS2_BENCH_REPEAT`, and it unsets `PS2_XR`, `PS2_XR_MODE` and `PS2_RN_VP` first). Extra `VAR=value` arguments to `bench_flat.sh`, `run_zero_mission.sh` and `run_zero_soak.sh` are exported before the run.

### What `play_zero_vr.sh` sets

| Launcher word | Variables it sets |
|---|---|
| (always) | `ACZ_LAUNCHER=${ACZ_LAUNCHER:-0}` (the launcher window stays off in this script; the public tree's copy says 1), `ACZ_MENU_SETTINGS=${ACZ_MENU_SETTINGS:-1}` (the ENHANCEMENTS settings apply; among their defaults the HUD scale 1.3, edge AA, LOD bias 2, terrain detail 2, smooth motion, 4x in the sharp mode, the modern pad layout), `SDL_JOYSTICK_ALLOW_BACKGROUND_EVENTS=1`; `PS2_UPSCALE=${PS2_UPSCALE:-3}` in every mode but sharp |
| `stick` (default mode) | `ACZ_VR_HEADLOOK=stick`, then as `stereo` |
| `stereo`, `mono`, `cinema` | `PS2_XR=1`, `PS2_XR_MODE=<mode>`, `PS2_XR_RUNTIME=${PS2_XR_RUNTIME:-<Meta runtime json>}`, `PS2_FIELD_HZ=${PS2_FIELD_HZ:-60}` |
| `sharp` | `PS2_XR_MULTIVIEW=1`, `PS2_UPSCALE=$PS2_UPSCALE_SHARP` only when that is set (else the menu's RESOLUTION, 4X), then as `stick` |
| `flat` | no XR variables |
| `m1` .. `m18` | `ACZ_BOOT_MISSION=<n>` |
| `native` | `PS2_RN_VP=1` |
| `smooth` | `PS2_XR_RERENDER=1` (without the word: the menu's SMOOTH MOTION, on) |
| `modern`, `classic` | `ACZ_PAD_LAYOUT=<word>` (without either: the menu's CONTROLLER LAYOUT, modern) |
| `cockpit` | `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MODEL=${ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MODEL:-cockpits/f5e_t38.cpit}` and the per-aircraft `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MODELS` (the menu's 3D COCKPIT OFF drops both at start-up) |

`play_zero_desktop.sh` (the desktop shortcuts) runs `play_zero_vr.sh sharp native cockpit` with `PS2_TEX_STATS=600 PS2_SPEED_LOG=1`; its words: `nosmooth` = `PS2_XR_RERENDER=0`, `clouds3d` = `ACZ_VR_CLOUDS=all` (both over the menu for that session), `record` (OBS recording, scene by `ACZ_REC_SCENE`; tools/playtest/README.md), `clean` (= `record` with `ACZ_REC_CLEAN=1` and `PS2_PASS_MASK=copy` unless set: the recording without the HUD, collection and profile "ACZ VR clean"; wish #236), `cine` (= `record` with `ACZ_REC_CINE=1 ACZ_REC_CINE_WINDOW=2560x1440` unless set: the window's own steady 16:9 view for a trailer, collection and profile "ACZ VR cine"; wish #245; since 2026-11-02 with the frame-exact master by the game itself: `ACZ_REC_EXACT=1` into `~/Videos/ACZ_playtest/acz_cine_<date>_<time>.mkv`, `ACZ_REC_CINE_WHEN=game` unless set, OBS with the light profile "ACZ VR cine ref" (voice, game, 960x540 reference, `acz_cineobs_*`) whose Voice track `tools/playtest/recmux.py` copies into the master; bug #264), `noexact` with `cine` (OBS records the picture as before, `ACZ_REC_EXACT=0`), `1080` / `1440` / `4k` with `cine` (= `ACZ_REC_CINE_SCALE` of that height unless set: the view drawn natively at that density; default `1`, the eyes'), `timeline` (= `ACZ_PLAY_TIMELINE=1`: the per-frame timeline CSVs beside the log), `72hz` / `80hz` / `90hz` / `120hz` (= `PS2_XR_REFRESH_HZ`: ask the runtime for that rate; weaker PCs set it in the Link / Virtual Desktop app, README), `m1`..`m18`. Until 2026-10-23 it also exported `PS2_EDGE_AA=1 ACZ_LOD_BIAS=2 ACZ_TERRAIN_DETAIL=2` and the words `smooth modern`: these are now the ENHANCEMENTS defaults (the same environment reaches the game: bug #64).

## VR / XR core

OpenXR session, stereo rendering and head tracking (`runtime/src/ps2_xr.c`, the stereo parts of `runtime/src/ps2_video.c`, and the Zero camera hooks in `runtime/src/zero_vr.c`). Head tracking only engages on the 640x480 flight camera and needs `PS2_XR=1` with `PS2_XR_MODE=mono` or a `stereo` mode.

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PS2_XR` | off | `1` | Turn OpenXR on (only the value `1` counts) | `runtime/src/ps2_hle_iop.c:937`, `runtime/src/ps2_launcher.cpp:758`, `runtime/src/ps2_spatial.c:38` (+18 more) |
| `PS2_XR_MODE` | `cinema` | `stereo`, `stereo-alt`, `mono`, `cinema` (anything else = cinema) | stereo: game renders once, the renderer draws each eye with parallax; stereo-alt: eyes alternate per game frame; mono: tracked, no stereo; cinema: virtual screen | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:395`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:662`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:5168` (+6 more) |
| `PS2_XR_RUNTIME` | system default OpenXR runtime | path to a runtime `.json` | Copied into `XR_RUNTIME_JSON` before the OpenXR instance is created | `runtime/src/ps2_profile.c:228`, `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:605` |
| `PS2_XR_MULTIVIEW` | off | `1` (needs `PS2_XR=1` and a stereo mode) | [experimental] Both eyes in one pass with Vulkan multiview (2-layer render targets) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:395` |
| `PS2_XR_RERENDER` | off (ENHANCEMENTS SMOOTH MOTION: on) | `1` | [experimental] When the headset wants a frame and the game has none, redraw the last one from the extrapolated camera | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:20010`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25963`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32155` (+1 more) |
| `PS2_XR_REPROJ_NEAR` | `200` | game units; `0` = no limit | In-between frames: GS-path draws with a vertex nearer than this (~33 m) are not extrapolated | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:37789`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:38489` |
| `PS2_XR_REPROJ_GS` | on | `0` | In-between frames: `0` stops reprojecting GS-path geometry | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:38507` |
| `PS2_XR_REPROJ_UNMATCHED` | on | `0` | In-between frames: `0` stops extrapolating native mesh objects with no match in the previous game frame | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:38792` |
| `PS2_XR_FROM_RT` | on | `0` | Head-tracked frames go to the headset straight from the render target; `0` uses the window image | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:33093` |
| `PS2_XR_SCREEN_FROM_RT` | on | `0` | The cinema screen (menus, briefings, story movies, the static screen over the flight, the intro flyby; bug #107, 2026-10-24) is copied from the render target's display region at internal resolution into its own swapchain (0) instead of the window image: at 4x 1792 lines over the screen's 48.5 deg (37 px/deg) instead of the window's 1009 (20.8 px/deg, 56 %); the same region (the window's crop, the movies' own letterbox) on the same 4:3 quad. Same-frame headset simulation (25 px/deg, one bilinear sample, ev in a private script): PSNR to the ideal +2.6..+8.1 dB, vertical edge contrast x1.05-1.10 menus, x1.01-1.10 story movie, x1.05-1.28 briefing (upscaled BRIEF.PAC), x1.22-1.24 pause menu over the flight. The window image stays for an overlay composite, a blank display, a display taller than 512 lines (one field at a movie start) and while the F4 menu is open; not carried over (as in flight): the window's present filter / [postfx] and the F4 FPS counter. GPU (in-run PS2_XR_SCREEN_FROM_RT_AB=30, 4x, 2 runs): the copy +0.008 ms (2048x1792, 14 MB) / +0.013..+0.015 ms (2560x1792, 17.5 MB) per screen frame; swapchain 0 2560x2048 x 3 images (60 MB). `0` = the window image (before 2026-10-24) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:1940`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:34745` |
| `PS2_XR_SCREEN_FROM_RT_AB` | off | N frames | [test] Screen frames alternate N-frame blocks between the render target and the window image; `xr-screen-ab:` GPU ms of the frame's tail (window present + headset copy + prompt layer) and of the whole frame per source every 40 x N screen frames (the first 2 frames of a block not counted) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:1903` |
| `PS2_XR_EYE_CROP` | on | `0` | Submit each eye as the crop of the symmetric image that covers its own asymmetric FOV; `0` submits the full image | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:1662`, `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:2251`, `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:2466` |
| `PS2_XR_VIS_MASK` | on (when the runtime offers it) | `0` | XR_KHR_visibility_mask (ov_passes 2026-10-25): the runtime's per-eye hidden-area and visible-area meshes (the lens corners no eye sees) are queried after the session starts and after its change event, checked against the eye's FOV (a mesh whose vertices are not tangents within 1.25 x the FOV is refused and logged), and used by PS2_PASS_MASK. The Meta PC runtime's DLL names the extension; the OpenXR Simulator does not offer it (log `xr: XR_KHR_visibility_mask ...`). `0` = not enabled | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:631` |
| `PS2_XR_REFRESH_EXT` | on (when the runtime offers it) | `0` | XR_FB_display_refresh_rate (perf6_cadence 2026-11-03): enabled on the instance when the runtime offers it (`xr: XR_FB_display_refresh_rate enabled` / `not offered by the runtime`); the session then logs the rates the runtime offers and the one it runs at (`xr: XR_FB_display_refresh_rate: the runtime offers 72.00 / 80.00 / 90.00 / 120.00 Hz; it runs at 120.00 Hz`) and every DISPLAY_REFRESH_RATE_CHANGED_FB event (`xr: the runtime changed the display refresh rate A -> B Hz`; the next new display period then counts at once). Needed for PS2_XR_REFRESH_HZ. Nothing else depends on it: the cadence (PS2_XR_SMOOTH_CLOCK=auto) follows every frame's display period. The OpenXR Simulator does not offer it; the Meta PC runtime's DLL names it (whether Link enumerates it: the first headset log). `0` = never enabled | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:640` |
| `PS2_XR_REFRESH_HZ` | off (the rate set in the headset's PC app) | Hz (`72`, `80`, `90`, `120`); `play_zero_desktop.sh` words `72hz` / `80hz` / `90hz` / `120hz` | Asks the runtime for this display refresh rate once the session runs (xrRequestDisplayRefreshRateFB, XR_FB_display_refresh_rate; perf6_cadence 2026-11-03): logs the call's result (`xr: PS2_XR_REFRESH_HZ: xrRequestDisplayRefreshRateFB(90.00) -> XR_SUCCESS`) and, 2 s later, the rate the runtime reports and the one the display period shows. Not relied on: Virtual Desktop's VDXR answers success without changing anything, and whether Quest Link honours it is untested (the log tells); without the extension a line says so. The way that always works is the rate in the Meta Quest Link app (Devices > the headset > Graphics Preferences > Refresh Rate) or in Virtual Desktop (Settings > Streaming > Frame rate); the game follows whatever rate the display runs at (PS2_XR_SMOOTH_CLOCK=auto; weaker PCs: 90 or 72 Hz, README) | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:209` |
| `PS2_XR_MONO_BELOW` | `0.3` | PS2 pixels; `0` = always stereo | Fields whose largest parallax is below this are drawn once for both eyes (nothing near) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32763` |
| `PS2_XR_GPU_BUDGET_MS` | `14` | ms; `0` = off | Stereo GPU governor: over budget draw once for both eyes, back to stereo under 85% of it | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32769` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOVERNOR` | on (VR only; built-in); `0` = the ENHANCEMENTS default since 2026-10-26 (FRAME-TIME GOVERNOR = OFF, the user's rule: the full picture always, optimise technically) | `0` | Headset frame-time governor (bugs #23, #59): from the GPU timestamps of the frames just completed it sheds cost that is hard to see while the GPU time stays over the budget, level by level (the ladder, ACZ_VR_GOV_LADDER; since 2026-10-25 today's extras first, ACZ_VR_GOV_EXTRAS): 1 canopy glints / glare / reflection, heat haze + wing vapour, flash lights, contrails (faded out over ACZ_VR_GOV_FADE_MS) and our far clouds re-marched with looser limits (ACZ_VR_GOV_FAR_MODE: every tile's error limit x ACZ_VR_GOV_FAR_ERR, the refresh half as often, ACZ_VR_GOV_FAR_CAP tiles a frame only outside the view; only with our far clouds), 2 cockpit MSAA 4x -> 2x (2-sample twins of the cockpit pipelines and a 2-sample target, ~117 MB at 4x; ACZ_VR_GOV_MSAA=0 removes it) and edge AA off (PS2_EDGE_AA), 3 shader water and near-ground materials faded out (the painted 2006 water and ground photo, exactly) and the target boxes as shapes. In-run A/B of each lever alone, M4 MiG-29A 40 m over the port / sea and the south base, our clouds, sim 4x, user settings, 2 runs (ACZ_VR_GOV_LEVER_AB=24): water 0.13-0.36, materials 0.02 (over sea) .. 0.22 (desert base), edge AA 0.13-0.16, cockpit MSAA 2x 0.09-0.11, glass 0.07-0.08, target box shapes 0.04-0.08, far clouds 0.04-0.07, haze 0.01-0.07, flash lights 0-0.03 (a launch every 7 s), contrails 0 (low), terrain detail ring 0.10-0.16 (+-0.1) ms per stereo frame; the old levels with ACZ_VR_GOV_EXTRAS=0 (1 far clouds, 2 edge AA, 3 cockpit MSAA); a level with nothing to shed is skipped. ACZ_VR_GOV_MODE picks the logic; changes logged as `gov: level a -> b`, frames per level, ups, hold factor and measured savings in the 5-s `gov:` line | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:30938` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_MODE` | `2` | `1` | Governor logic. `2` (bug #59): up when ACZ_VR_GOV_SHARE of the last ACZ_VR_GOV_WIN_MS of frames are over the budget (a few slow frames do not count), one level per step; down not before ACZ_VR_GOV_HOLD_MS after the last step up (doubled, up to 6x, when a level is taken again within 30 s of giving it back) and when the window's 90th percentile plus the level's saving stays under 0.85 x the budget for ACZ_VR_GOV_DOWN_MS; each step's saving measured (median 1 s before / after), a level whose median over 3+ steps is under ACZ_VR_GOV_MIN_SAVE_MS is dropped. `1`: the first version (EMA + 2 mean deviations, 4 frames up, 1 s down, at least 0.3 s between changes, samples clipped at 1.5 x the budget, no action in the first 2 s; oscillated on the headset: 335 changes in 27 min) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31416` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_BUDGET_MS` | 0.8 x the headset display period (6.67 at 120 Hz) | ms of our GPU time per frame | Governor budget (mode 2: the frames over it are counted; mode 1: the high-water GPU time, EMA + 2 x mean deviation, is compared with it). On the simulator (60-90 Hz) it rarely triggers: set it for tests | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31479`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31515` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_SHARE` | `0.10` | share of frames | Governor mode 2: a level is added when this share of the window's frames are over the budget (0.10 = 12 frames a second at 120 Hz) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31264` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_WIN_MS` | `1000` | ms (`250`-`2000`) | Governor mode 2: the window of recent frames (since the last change + 150 ms; at least 60 frames) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31265` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_HOLD_MS` | `15000` | ms | Governor mode 2: a level is kept at least this long after the last step up (x2 up to x6 when a level is taken again within 30 s of giving it back; x1 after a minute without a step up) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31267` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_UP` | `4` | frames | Governor mode 1: consecutive frames with the high-water time over the budget before a level is added | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31551` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_DOWN_MS` | `3000` (mode 2), `1000` (mode 1) | ms | Governor: how long the predicted time without the level (mode 2: window p90 + the level's measured saving; mode 1: high-water + saving) must stay under 0.85 x the budget before the level is given back | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31268`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31552` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_MIN_SAVE_MS` | `0.10` | ms | Governor mode 2: a level whose measured saving (median of its last 8 steps, 3 at least) is below this is not shed any more this session (logged) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31269` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_STEP_MAX` | `0.3` | x the budget (`0` = every step counts) | Governor mode 2 (bug #65, 2026-10-23): a level step whose measured saving is larger than this either way (2.0 ms at 120 Hz) measured a scene change, not the level, and is not counted (logged `a scene change -- not counted`); on the headset the cockpit vanishing and coming back gave level 3 steps of +0.82 / -3.48 / -1.15 ms and the median then dropped MSAA | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31292` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_MSAA` | on | `0` | The governor's cockpit MSAA lever 4x -> 2x (level 2 of the default ladder, 3 of the old). `0`: no such lever -- no 2-sample cockpit target or pipelines (~117 MB less GPU memory at 4x). | `ACZ_VR_GOV_FORCE_SEQ` | off | `"field:level field:level ..."` | [test] From each field on the governor holds that level, switched when a frame is recorded (also inside eye-dump sequences, where the GPU-timed path pauses): level switches within PS2_XR_DUMP_SEQ sequences, whose log lines give the cockpit model's sample count per frame | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31165` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_EXTRAS` | on | `0` | Governor ladder (2026-10-25): the default ACZ_VR_GOV_LADDER sheds today's extras before the old levers; `0` = the levels of before (`far,eaa,msaa`: 1 far clouds, 2 edge AA off, 3 cockpit MSAA 2x) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31009` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_LADDER` | `glass+haze+flash+trails+far+gcloud,msaa+eaa,water+tmat+marker+offrt` | comma list of levels (at most 8), each a `+` list of levers: `glass` (canopy scratches / glare / reflection, ACZ_VR_CANOPY_*), `haze` (heat haze + wing vapour), `trails` (contrails), `flash` (flash lights in the cockpit and the clouds), `far` (our far clouds' looser refresh, ACZ_VR_GOV_FAR_MODE), `gcloud` (the game's clouds of ACZ_VR_CLOUD_BASE sliced half as finely -- bands and slices across -- when centred more than 40 deg off the head's axis; the limit sweeps in from 90 deg as it fades; nx_gov 2026-10-25: saves 0.25 +- 0.10 ms in the M1 overcast, ACZ_VR_GOV_LEVER_AB, exclusive 4x, 19 pairs), `msaa` (cockpit MSAA 4x -> 2x), `eaa` (edge AA off), `water` (shader water), `wlite` (only the water's sun glitter and our clouds' reflection in it), `tmat` (near-ground materials), `marker` (target boxes as shapes, ACZ_VR_MARKER_SHARP 3 -> 2), `offrt` (the game's off-screen targets that reach the picture only through a filter drawn at ACZ_VR_GOV_OFFRT_SCALE instead of PS2_UPSCALE, see ACZ_VR_GOV_OFFRT; switched, not faded; nx_offscreen 2026-10-25: saves 0.17-0.23 ms per stereo frame in M4 low over the port and the sea, where the game's speed-blur / glow chain runs (ACZ_VR_GOV_LEVER_AB, exclusive 4x, 4 runs of 38 pairs: 0.232 +- 0.048, 0.198 +- 0.042, 0.183 +- 0.035 with our clouds, 0.170 +- 0.043 with the game's), nothing measurable in M12 / M14 (-0.03 +- 0.04 / -0.04 +- 0.07)), `texpack` (the T5 pack), `tdetail` (the terrain detail ring back to the PS2's); a fading lever (`glass` `haze` `trails` `flash` `water` `wlite` `tmat` `gcloud`) or `tdetail` may keep a share (`tmat:0.5` = half strength; `tdetail:0.5` = ring 1 + (ACZ_TERRAIN_DETAIL - 1) x 0.5) | [tuning] The governor's levels, cumulative, cheapest-to-lose first; logged at start (`gov: level N: ...`). Default order by the M4 in-run A/B (ACZ_VR_GOV_LEVER_AB, see ACZ_VR_GOVERNOR) and how visible each loss is: level 1 nearly invisible, 2 edge quality at 4x supersampling, 3 the 2006 water and ground; in-run level A/B (ACZ_VR_GOV_AB=48, M4 as ACZ_VR_GOVERNOR, sim 4x) 0.10-0.12 / 0.30-0.32 / 0.30 ms, together 0.70-0.72 of 5.3. Not in the default: `tdetail` (0.10-0.16 +-0.1 ms, re-streams the terrain when it switches: a governor step measured -0.39 ms; the mid-distance mesh visibly steps), `wlite` (0.03-0.05 ms), `texpack` (+0.02 ms, off by default) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31009` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_INTRO` | on | `0` | Governor (nx_gov 2026-10-25): only frames drawn with the flight camera count. Frames recorded while the pad player does not fly -- a mission event holds the camera (flag 0x2000 of player +0x1A68: mission 2's take-off clearance, cutscenes) or the player's update has not run for 8 fields (an intro flyby, a replay) -- are left out of the window: the governor holds its level, neither up nor down (menus, briefings, the pause and the cinema screen were never counted). At the first flight frame after a mission start, a RETRY or a jump of the aircraft (the canopy reset's count) it starts afresh at level 0 (fresh window, hold x1, 2 s without action; measured savings kept). Before: M2's take-off clearance (head-tracked stereo, no cockpit; 9.6 ms GPU at 4x in ov_hitch's census, 4.0 ms on the 2026-10-25 build, so on the headset's 6.67-ms budget at x1.2-1.75) raised it to level 3, and the 15-s hold carried the painted sea, no edge AA and the 2-sample cockpit into the first 15 s of flight (simulator with ACZ_VR_GOV_LOAD_X=3.2: level 3 at the flight start, level 0 only 26 s into it; now level 0 throughout; a forced x5.5 load in flight still climbs to 3 in 4.4 s). `0` = every timed frame counts (before) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:31429`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:4338`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:4348` |
| `ACZ_VR_GOV_OFFRT` | `auto` | `auto`, `0`, comma list of target slots | Governor lever `offrt` (nx_offscreen 2026-10-25, level 3 of the default ACZ_VR_GOV_LADDER): which of the game's off-screen render targets are drawn at ACZ_VR_GOV_OFFRT_SCALE instead of PS2_UPSCALE while the lever is shed (at level 0 every target as before: the same images). `auto`: every slot that qualifies in the frame's draw list -- not the scene, the displayed target or the cockpit screens' target; no DATE draw into it, no colour clamp pass, AC5 native pass or cockpit / HUD copy; no depth buffer shared with another slot and its depth never sampled (the sun-occlusion probe reads one target's depth: left alone); no draw lays it about 1:1 (0.75-1.5 texels a pixel) onto the scene or the displayed target; its drawn part at least 1/32 of the target; 8 frames in a row. In the missions measured these are the glow / speed-blur chain (a half-size copy of the scene, its quarter and eighth reductions, laid back over the scene 2x magnified), the sun glow overlay (128 x 128 stretched over the screen), the sea's reflection (256 x 256, sampled in perspective by the sea), the game's cloud sprite textures and targets nothing samples. A list (`7,9,10`) takes only those slots (slot numbers follow the order a session first draws its targets: check `rt-census:` / `offrt:` lines; the 1:1 rule is then not applied, the others are); `0` = the lever has nothing to shed. Each shed slot keeps a second colour + depth pair at the lower scale (made one a frame, ~0.8-1.4 ms each at 4x, once the slot has qualified for 2 s while the governor runs: `offrt:` lines), swapped in at a frame start with its picture blitted across: a switch allocates nothing and keeps the content. Measured (exclusive 4x, user settings, in-run A/B): what the off-screen targets cost at all (PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=rtskip, governor off) M4 0.66-0.69 ms of 4.4 (game and in-between frames alike), M14 sea 0.28 +- 0.05, M12 overcast 0.18 +- 0.05; the lever M4 -0.17..-0.23 ms, M12 / M14 0 +- 0.04-0.07 (there the cost is the 128 x 128 object / cloud sprite textures, laid onto the scene near 1:1 by near sprites, and the sun-occlusion probe chain: left alone). Same frame (PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=offrt, M4): > 8 levels on 0.001-0.08 % of the pixels, at most 24 levels, only at the edges of the speed-blur overlay over the ground (noise floor 0 % / 5 levels); M12 / M14 at their noise floor. A switch costs one frame +0.3-0.4 ms of GPU (all slots' copies in two barrier calls, only of slots something samples, one layer for one-view targets; no late frame in the traces); the lower versions are made with the full ones in the mission's loading frames (~0.9 ms and ~15 MB each at 4x) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25036` |

| `ACZ_VR_GAZE_TARGET` | on, 20 degrees | `0`, `1`, degrees `3`..`60` | The target button (Y / Triangle: the game's switch on a short press; holding it is still the game's target camera) picks the target the head looks at: in head-tracked frames (the R3 cockpit view with stick head look) the valid target with the best score within that cone (half-angle) of the gaze (the head's forward axis turned ACZ_VR_GAZE_PITCH down): the gaze angle plus ACZ_VR_GAZE_NEAR / _TGT / _FRONT, targets hidden behind terrain or objects left out while one in the cone can be seen (ACZ_VR_GAZE_VISIBLE), from the game's own candidates (every group, as its own picks scan them -- before 2026-10-29 eve the scan stopped at group 64 and missed M17's mission targets in groups 65-70, bug #234; its validity test 0x251988: alive, enemy, air/ground by the weapon; its switch range, 6000 m), set through the game's own target fields and lock-on reset (player +0x1880: +0x98/+0x99, +0x7F/+0x80, 0x251630), so the HUD box, lock-on, missiles and radar follow. When the nearest is the current target already, the next one outward within ACZ_VR_GAZE_CYCLE of the gaze, and quick re-presses with a steady head walk on through that tight cone and wrap (cycling a group); nothing else there = the current target stays (the press changes nothing, the lock-on goes on); nothing in the cone at all = the game's own choice (the target nearest the nose). Views where the head does not turn the camera, the game's automatic pick after a target is lost and the special weapons' multi-target mode are untouched. One log line per press: the pick and how it was chosen, the head's direction from the nose, the current target's gaze angle, the counts in both cones and what the old rule (before 2026-10-29) would have picked. `0` = the game's choice always (wish #53) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5010`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5021` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_GAZE` | off | `1`..`5` | [debug] Gaze targeting: per press the head's and the gaze's direction, every candidate with its distance (metres as the HUD's: 5.12 units a metre, since pt_target; before ACZ_VR_UNITS_PER_M 6), TGT, its sight (visible, or HIDDEN with where the line hits), its angles to the gaze and to the nose, its score, its yaw / pitch from the head's axis and its position, the game's own nose pick, the picks by score, by angle alone and by the old rule, and the last gaze pick (the re-press walk); `2` also every unit with the game's tests; with ACZ_VR_GAZE_TARGET=0 it logs what the gaze would have picked. With ACZ_DEBUG_GAZE_PROBE (test, pt_target): `3` every unit with its position and the map's static objects once, `4` where the player keeps its position and attitude, `5` the free distance around the aircraft by heading (the segment test, 3 km) and to the floor and ceiling | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5021` |
| `ACZ_VR_GAZE_CYCLE` | `8` degrees | degrees `0`..the cone | Gaze targeting's tight cone (pt_gaze 2026-10-29; the 10:50 session 10:56:07 "I press Y and it flies off somewhere", 10:58:21, 11:03:31 "it aimed not where I look"): a press on the current target (it is the nearest to the gaze) takes the next one outward within this many degrees of the gaze, quick re-presses (within 3 s of the last gaze pick, the gaze moved less than 3 deg) walk on outward through it and wrap, nothing else in it = the current target stays. Before, whenever the current target was anywhere in the 20 deg cone the press took the next one outward from it in gaze angle -- past the targets the head pointed at: in that session 21 of 117 picks lay 12-18 deg from the head's axis, 17 of them right after a pick of the current target; video reconstruction (10:56:05, 10:58:20, 11:03:30): the current target 10-17 deg off, a group 3-5 deg off, the pick 14-17 deg off. A model of a 14-unit group with 3 deg head noise: picks more than 8 deg from the looked-at target 48 % -> 5 %. Sim (M1 F-15C, scripted head and Y): the head on a pair, the current target 2.0 deg off and the other 1.6 deg: the new rule takes the other, the old one (cone 60) a third target 59.8 deg off; a quick re-press walks to the next of the pair; the current target 6.1 deg off and nothing else within 8: it stays (old: the next outward, 10.5 deg off). `0` = that old outward rule (and with only the current target in the cone, the game's choice) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:5022` |

While anything in the cone is visible the hidden ones are left out (also from the re-press walk); nothing visible = all of them as before. The world it tests is the one the pad player's own collision tests use (M17 keeps two maps). | `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_MODE` | `layered` when smooth motion is on (PS2_XR_RERENDER=1; the user's decision 2026-11-03), else off | `rerender`, `layered` | Smooth mode (PS2_XR_RERENDER; perf4_smooth 2026-11-03; launcher words `rerender` / `layered`): how a frame between two game frames is drawn. `rerender` = the last game frame's whole draw list again from the extrapolated camera (95-98 % of a game frame's GPU time; at 120 Hz half the headset's GPU time). `layered` = each game frame copies its world layer -- slot 0's colour and depth right before the 3D cockpit model (world, sky, the game's and our clouds, effects, trails, edge AA; inside cloud right before our near clouds instead, which the in-between frames then march again: ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_CLOUDS) -- and its in-between frames warp that copy per pixel by its depth with the re-render's camera motion (smooth_warp.frag: gs.vert's reprojection per eye, a backward search with the background preferred where a near edge uncovers what the game frame did not see, the frame's near rule, Catmull-Rom resampling), move native objects with their own motion as the re-render does (ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_MOVERS), then draw only what comes after the model as before (the model, glass, composite, eye adaptation, the HUD, the sun's overlays, the game's post passes) and the off-screen targets those read. Frames without the 3D cockpit, two-pass stereo and in-between frames without a copy of their game frame fall back to the re-render (`smooth-layer:` lines every 10 s: warped / re-rendered and why, draws left out, movers). Sim, exclusive 4x, user settings, in-run A/B (ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_AB=60, PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2, ms per drawing, mean +- SE over block pairs): in-between frame M3 J35J 1.193 layered vs 3.286 re-rendered (paired -2.087 +- 0.031; 0.346 of a game frame), M18 X-02S 1.740 vs 2.712 (-0.994 +- 0.049; 0.587: 30 % of its in-between frames are inside the snow haze and march our near clouds again, ~2.7 ms each, the others ~1.32), M1 overcast inside cloud (near clouds marched again) 2.917 vs 3.643 (-0.728 +- 0.025; 0.763), M1 with the near clouds warped 1.657 vs 3.638 (0.433); game frames +0.064-0.075 for the copy (0.076 per drawing in the class timer, the warp 0.154 in M3); no skipped display periods either way (sim, 90 Hz). Prediction error (PS2_XR_EXTRAP=1, lockstep, head still; mean abs per channel, tools/perf/smooth_extrap_cmp.py): M4 40 m 0.282 vs re-render 0.258 vs a repeated frame 0.454, M1 in cloud 0.521 / 0.528 / 0.935. Headset untested | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25961` |
| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_CLOUDS` | `0.05` | in-cloud level 0..1, `0`, `off` | Layered in-between frames: inside cloud (our in-cloud value, ps2_clouds_in_cloud, at or above this, with the near field marching), the game frame's copy is taken right before our near composite and the in-between frames march and composite the near volume for their own camera, then draw what follows as the re-render does (the effects after the clouds, trails, edge AA, the model ...): warped fog moves by one depth per pixel, the volume's structure 10-300 m away does not. Costs the near pass and edge AA in those frames. `0` = whenever the near field has a cloud in reach, `off` = never (the near clouds warped with the rest). M18 snow haze (mean value 0.144): 30 % of the in-between frames, which then cost about a re-render (~2.7 vs 2.71 ms; a higher level, e.g. 0.2, keeps them warped at ~1.32 ms: their look untested). M3 next to cumulus (value 0.001): not triggered; with `0` it engaged in 93 % of M3's in-between frames (user clocks, one drawing: 2.081 vs re-render 3.611 ms, 0.536 of a game frame; the warp alone 0.346 at P0) and the prediction was no better than with the clouds warped (M3 0.445 vs 0.367; re-render 0.593, run-to-run floor 0.315) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25968` |

| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_NEAR` | PS2_XR_REPROJ_NEAR_COCKPIT's `100` | game units | Layered in-between frames: world-layer pixels nearer than this are not moved (the re-render's near rule for GS draws in the cockpit view) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25972` |
| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_MOVERS` | `1` | target px; `0` off | Layered in-between frames: native objects matched to themselves one game frame back (xr_reproj 2) whose own extrapolated motion lands this many target pixels or more off the world's at their origin (other aircraft, missiles; up to 8 a frame, the largest effect first) are warped with their own motion (the re-render's m + alpha (m - m_prev)) instead of as world; the static solution that falls on such an object's own pixels (its ghost) takes the background beside it. `0` = every object moves as world | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25976` |

| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_EDGE` | `auto` | `auto`, N target px at 4x, `0` | (perf5_reproj 2026-11-03) Layered in-between frames turn the game frame's image by the aircraft's rotation since it (the head held), so along each eye's crop the content comes in from past the crop -- where the game frame had none of our clouds (their composite and near march stop at the crop), 4x4 foveated shading and, past PS2_PASS_MASK_MARGIN, nothing in the copy: a band at the lens edge in every turn, as wide as the motion. `auto`: each layered game frame predicts its in-between frames' move of each eye's crop edge (the rotation between the last two game frames x the largest recent in-between alpha, 8 points per eye) and grows our clouds' crops, the foveated shading's crop and the pass mask's margin (the copy, the cockpit's area, edge AA) to at least that + 4 px (steps of 16 px at 4x, held 30 game frames, at most ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_EDGE_MAX). Game frames only (in-between frames keep the crop, one rate image for both); the headset shows only the crop, so a game frame's shown pixels do not change. Translation is left out (forward flight moves the edge's content outwards). The crops' own edges where they meet the layer's edge (the outer side and the bottom of each eye at the default layer) cannot grow: ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_EDGE_RR. N = a fixed margin; `0` = before. Same-frame dumps keeping the headset's crops (ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_DUMP_CROP=1, M3 full-stick rolls, 48 in-between frames each, 2x): the hard line in the top band (largest row-to-row jump of the crop's first 64 rows over its median) 5.4 -> 1.7, at the inner side (column jumps) 3.4 -> 2.4 (crops a private script); margins in the rolls mean 22.3 px at 2x (max 64), in M12's chase dogfight 19.0 (max 48). GPU (exclusive 4x, in-run ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_EDGE_AB=60): M3 level flight (no turn, no margin) game frames -0.030 +- 0.031, in-between -0.010 +- 0.009 ms (nothing); turning: M12 F-15C chase dogfight (turning from field 1500; margins mean 40.5 px at 4x, max 112, in 2526 game frames; 50 block pairs): game frames +0.033 +- 0.029, in-between +0.002 +- 0.020 ms per drawing (not significant; headset estimate <= +0.06 a game frame) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25977` |
| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_EDGE_MAX` | `256` | target px at 4x | ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_EDGE: the largest margin (scaled with PS2_UPSCALE) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25981` |
| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_EDGE_RR` | `0` (off) | target px at 4x | (perf5_reproj) Layered in-between frames: where an eye's crop ends at the layer's own edge (the outer side and the bottom at the default layer) the game drew nothing beyond, and a turn that brings content in from there is filled by stretching the edge's last pixels. With N > 0 an in-between frame whose camera motion moves a point of such a side (8 points per eye, the rotation) N px or more inwards is re-rendered instead (`smooth-layer:` lines: `turn past the layer's edge`). Off: a fast roll re-renders most of its in-between frames (M3 full-stick rolls turn 1.6-3.7 deg a game frame), costing a re-render (M3 3.29 vs 1.2 ms) while it lasts; the stretched band it removes sits at the lens's outermost edge | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25985` |

| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_DUMP_CROP` | off | `1` | [test] Eye dumps keep the eyes' crops as the headset's frames have them (the pass masks, our clouds' and the foveated shading's crops) instead of drawing our passes on the whole image: the crop-edge band of layered in-between frames shows in the dumps (tools/perf/smooth_edge_ab.py). Without it, with layered in-between frames, the game frames from 4 fields before a due dump until it is taken draw on the whole image as a dumped frame does, so a dumped in-between frame (PS2_XR_DUMP_RERENDER, PS2_PERF_AB re-draws) warps a complete world layer (before: rows outside the pass mask's box, 0-120 at 2x, kept stale pixels; test runs only) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:4741` |
| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_COCKPIT` | `0` (off) | `1`, `2` | (perf5_reproj 2026-11-03) Layered in-between frames keep the game frame's head orientation, projection, lights, animations and HUD blocks, so their 3D cockpit is the game frame's again, except where the eye moved (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_FRESH_HEAD) and where the canopy's weather moves with time or refracts the moving world. `1`: an in-between frame draws no model when the game frame's resolved cockpit is still in its target, the canopy was dry (no water, mist, film, frost, grime or soot: canopy_active), its cracks did not grow since, the cockpit's flash light (ACZ_VR_FLASH_LIGHT: launches, blasts, gun fire) changed by 1 % of full or less since the game frame and the afterburner glow did not change, the Quest Touch controllers are off (the gloves and the stick follow them every frame), no cloud veil is drawn, our near clouds are not marched again, and the eye moved at most ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_COCKPIT_MM since the game frame's model pass: the world warp composites that layer over the warped world (smooth_warp.frag, the CPC variant), each cockpit pixel moved by the eye's move at its own depth (the frame depth after the model, copied in game frames), the combiner's mark held, the moved model depth written; then the eye adaptation; else the cockpit is drawn as before. The glass with any weather is always drawn (the coordinator's rule, 2026-11-03: the glass and the weather must not step at 60 Hz). Game frames: + one depth copy after the model (not with canopy weather or inside cloud, where it is never used; M18 X-02S: never kept -- canopy weather 68 %, inside the snow haze 32 % of its in-between frames). `2` [test]: also with the canopy weather (its drops, refraction and frost then step at 60 Hz). Sim, exclusive 4x, the user's settings, governor off, PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2, ms per drawing (P0), M3 J35J with the census' head and weapons script (ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_COCKPIT_AB=60, 44 block pairs): an in-between frame with the layer kept 0.645 +- 0.001 (p99 0.749) vs 1.199 +- 0.002 drawn (p99 1.360), -0.55 (-46 %; 0.19 of a game frame instead of 0.36); kept in 90 % of in-between frames (the rest: the flash rule at the census' launches every 2.5 s); pooled in-between -0.496 +- 0.015; game frames +0.035 (the depth copy; pooled +0.021 +- 0.031). Per 120 Hz pair 4.56 -> 4.10 ms (-10 %), headset estimate (x1.75) 7.98 -> 7.17. Same frame, layer vs drawn (PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=lwcpit, 2x, head still): <= 2 levels (6-7 % of pixels by one: the blend's rounding) except at launches (flash light, up to 17 levels: hence the flash rule); the eye moving 0.26 mm a frame: 0.07 % of pixels > 16 levels (the panel's fine text softened by the resampling: moves under 0.35 px now keep the pixel). Bug #130's check-six head (0.23 m/s): kept in 35 % of the frames (between the turns). Headset untested | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25987` |
| `ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_COCKPIT_MM` | `0.5` | mm | ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_COCKPIT: the largest eye move since the game frame's model pass for which the layer is kept (moved in the warp); a larger move draws the cockpit (bug #130's check-six head at 0.23 m/s moves ~2 mm per in-between frame). 0.5 mm moves a panel at 0.3 m by ~1.2 px at 4x, the seat at 0.1 m by ~3.7 px | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:25988` |

| `PS2_XR_FRAME_OFF_CHECK` | on | `0` | Bug #65 (2026-10-23, M4 A-10A "my aircraft disappeared"): the frame's GS XYOFFSET, which places everything drawn with the frame camera by us (the cockpit model, our clouds and the cloud veil, the in-between frames' reprojection, the shading-rate crop), is taken from a world (class-1) GS draw only when it puts the camera centre inside the displayed frame. Every frame starts with the sky's full-screen fill at XYOFFSET 0,0; in frames without any world GS draw after it the frame kept 0,0 and all of the above went 1792 px off screen (headset log: `vrs-fov ... axis at 8192.0,8192.0`). `0` = as before | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:35474` |
| `PS2_XR_SMOOTH_CLOCK` | `auto` | `auto`, `1`, `0` | `auto` (perf6_cadence 2026-11-03): on by itself while smooth motion is on (PS2_XR_RERENDER=1) and the headset's display is uneven with the game's 60 Hz (72 / 80 / 90 / 96 / 100 / 144 Hz; a whole number of refreshes per field within 1 % is even: 60, 120, 180 Hz), judged from every xrWaitFrame's display period, not from the refresh-rate extension: a new period counts after 8 frames in a row, at once after the runtime's DISPLAY_REFRESH_RATE_CHANGED_FB event (`xr: display 90.00 Hz ...` and `xr-pace: smooth clock ON` lines). Off at 120 Hz (2:1, the user's setting: exactly the path before), before the first frame, without smooth motion and with PS2_PERF_AB (a moved game frame warps its world in the frame's pass but not in the A/B re-draw, so same-frame flips would differ). The OpenXR Simulator runs at 90 Hz, so a simulator run with smooth motion gets the clock too unless `0` is set (run_test.sh's choice). `1` = always, `0` = never (the 3:2 / 6:5 steps of before). What it does (bug #89, fx_pacing 2026-10-23): every headset frame shows the world at its display time minus one steady latency -- game frames too, not only the in-between frames. A game frame that reaches the headset a refresh late (its list published just after the renderer's decision, a runtime skip) or between refreshes (90 Hz: three refreshes per two game frames) is extrapolated / interpolated (alpha -0.6..1.5 of a game frame) by the same reprojection as the in-between frames; game time = the field clock's grid at the frame's first publication (the earliest publication of the last 128 fields anchors it); the latency = display time - game time of each game frame's first display, averaged over 32 game frames (resynced after a jump of 50 ms). Game frames within 0.02 of their slot stay exactly as drawn (a steady 120 Hz headset: identical images); they keep the game frame's work (cloud optics etc.). Game frames of two fields (the game below 60 fps) are extrapolated over their real length. Simulator, layered (M3, in-run A/B, tools/perf/cadence_ab.py): 90 Hz motion rms (q - 1) 0.408 -> 0.008, steps off by > 35 % 66.4 % -> 0 %, in-between alpha 0.67 -> 0.50, game frames moved +-0.18, the shown world's age 23.8 -> 24.8 ms (spread 3.2 -> 1.0); emulated 72 Hz (PS2_XR_TEST_HZ) rms 0.366 -> 0.009, jerks 16.8 % -> 0 %, alpha 0.83 -> 0.51, game frames moved +-0.22, age +1.0 ms (spread 4.3 -> 0.5). With layered a moved game frame warps its world in place (lw_kind 3): M3 / M1 overcast at P0 (PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2), per drawing, +0.17 +- 0.07 / +0.16 +- 0.05 ms a game frame at 90 Hz, +0.11 +- 0.10 / +0.14 +- 0.04 at 72 Hz; GPU per second +4.7 % / +3.5 % at 90 Hz, +2.9 % / +3.3 % at 72 Hz. Untested in the headset | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32152` |

Rows the page changed last session (`launcher_changed` in the file) are marked once. `0`: no launcher, no log line, a boot like before. `1`: shown even when the file says `launcher = 0`: the environment wins over the file. The page row (QUALITY's first, also F4 > Enhancements and the buttons of F4 > Quick): LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH write its rows and save them at once (a row the environment holds in that run is saved too and its switch wins until a start without it); most are restart rows, so the RESTART THE GAME TO APPLY notice follows. The row shows the preset whose values all its saved rows have, else CUSTOM, derived every time: changing one of the rows by hand makes it CUSTOM and nothing is rewritten by itself; the file's `quality_preset` line is only a record. HIGH = every row at its default (`def`), i.e. today's picture, by construction (a row whose default changes stays HIGH's). Rows (LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH): RESOLUTION 2X / 3X / 4X, FOREST DISTANCE NORMAL / FAR / FAR, FRAME-TIME GOVERNOR ON / ON / OFF, COCKPIT ANTI-ALIASING 2X / 2X / 4X (only while no COCKPIT DETAIL row exists: that row carries the MSAA rule); CLOUD DETAIL / CANOPY QUALITY / COCKPIT DETAIL low / medium / high (found by their row keys `cloud_detail`, `canopy_quality`, `cockpit_detail`; ENH_HAVE_CLOUD_DETAIL / ENH_HAVE_CANOPY_QUALITY in ps2_settings.c compile in the rows for `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_DETAIL` / `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_QUALITY`). Never set by a preset: SMOOTH MOTION (layered in-between frames stay on), REAR-VIEW MIRRORS, the clouds' mode, every look, comfort, HUD, sound and control row. Why: the resolution scales every pixel pass, ours included, so those keep their looks and get cheaper with it. Sweep (sim, RTX 3090 Ti, exclusive, the user's settings, layered, governor off, PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2 halved; GPU ms per game / in-between frame): M3 J35J 4x 3.36 / 1.19, 3x 2.39 / 0.79, 2x 1.60 / 0.48; M1 overcast F-5E 3.76 / 2.87, 2.51 / 1.82, 1.72 / 1.14; M18 X-02S 2.91 / 1.86, 2.03 / 1.21, 1.27 / 0.68; the 120 Hz pair 3x -30..-35 %, 2x -54..-59 %; T(S) = a + b S^2 leaves 69-74 % of a game frame and 79-84 % of an in-between frame pixel-proportional at 4x. Cockpit at 2 samples (in-run A/B in the same runs): -0.01..-0.04 game, -0.01..-0.06 in-between in these light cockpits. FOREST DISTANCE is not measured today (no trees in view in these flights; 10-21: M2 +0.38 ms at FAR). No other GPU was measured and nothing ran in the headset. An existing file is never touched (`[enhancements]` present). ### Quest Touch controllers

The Touch controllers as the pilot's controls (`runtime/src/ps2_touch.c`; the OpenXR actions in `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c`;
CONTROLS_RESEARCH_2026-10-23.md). Everything ends as DualShock 2 bytes: the game, PS2PAD2 recordings and replays are
unchanged; the Xbox pad stays live (per axis the larger input wins, buttons are OR'ed).

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ACZ_TOUCH` | `auto` (ctl_priority 2026-11-03; was off) | `auto`, `pad`, `stick`, `off` / `0` | Quest Touch controllers as input (needs `PS2_XR=1`; off = no OpenXR action set at all). `auto` (unset; ENHANCEMENTS > CONTROLS > TOUCH CONTROLLERS = AUTO) = `pad` while no gamepad is connected (the controller registry, ACZ_PAD_ACTIVE), muted while one is (no input to the game, no vibration, the cockpit's hands as with off) unless Touch is the active controller: forced, or by a press on it, and a hand closing on the stick or throttle makes the session `stick` (ACZ_TOUCH_AUTO_GRAB); a PS2_XR_TEST_TOUCH script without ACZ_TOUCH stays `pad`. `pad` = Touch as an Xbox pad with the the modern layout positions through the controller layout (left thumbstick flies, triggers = triggers, grips = bumpers with an analog value above a resting hand (0.3), A B X Y as on the pad, right thumbstick flicks = D-pad, right click = R3 on release, left click = View (tap: map; held 1 s: recentre), both clicks = L3, Menu = Menu on release (held 0.5 s = View + Menu: the quit / ENHANCEMENTS combo)). `stick` = while flying the right hand grabs a virtual stick with the grip (tilt relative to the grab = pitch / roll) and the left hand the throttle lever (the left thumbstick also thrust while held: ACZ_TOUCH_THUMB_THRUST); trigger gun, A missile / special, B target, X weapon switch, Y map (held 1 s recentre), right thumbstick flicks = wingman commands, its click = view, left click = autopilot, Menu = Start (held 1 s in flight: the TOUCH CONTROLS card, ACZ_TOUCH_CARD_HOLD); outside flight (menus, pause, events) as `pad`. The TOUCH CONTROLS card draws both mappings (bug #239) | `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:1902`, `runtime/src/ps2_touch.c:150` |
| `ACZ_TOUCH_GRIP` | `hold` | `hold`, `toggle` | STICK: hold the grip to hold the stick / lever, or tap to take and tap to let go (the couch variant: the controller can rest in the lap; let go automatically when the controller goes inactive or the headset loses focus) | `runtime/src/ps2_touch.c:129`, `runtime/src/ps2_touch.c:156` |
| `ACZ_TOUCH_GRAB` | `either` | `either`, `grip`, `trigger` | (bug #307, 2026-11-05; ENHANCEMENTS > CONTROLS > GRAB WITH) HANDS ON STICK: which button closes a hand on the stick / throttle. `either`: the grip from anywhere (ACZ_TOUCH_REACH as before), a trigger press when the controller is near the control (ACZ_TOUCH_GRAB_NEAR); a free hand near its control does not fire or brake with the trigger. `grip`: as before 2026-11-05, the trigger is only gun / brake. `trigger`: the trigger from anywhere. The button that closed the hand holds it (ACZ_TOUCH_GRIP: hold or tap); while a trigger holds, that hand's grip is its gun / brake. Log: `touch: stick grabbed (...; by the trigger)` | `runtime/src/ps2_touch.c:130`, `runtime/src/ps2_touch.c:157` |
| `ACZ_TOUCH_GRAB_NEAR` | `0.25` | metres `0.05`..`1` | (bug #307) ACZ_TOUCH_GRAB=either: how near the control's grip point a trigger press grabs it; in a model without gloves on its controls (no grip point known, the fallback gloves) 1.5 x this around a generic place (stick 5 cm right, throttle 28 cm left of the design eye, both ~47 cm down, ~30 cm ahead) | `runtime/src/ps2_touch.c:160` |
| `ACZ_TOUCH_AUTO_GRAB` | `near` (the owner, 2026-11-05) | `near`, `1`, `0` | (bugs #314, #315, 2026-11-05: "I do not see how to grab the stick with the gloves") ACZ_TOUCH=auto in flight: a hand closing at the stick or throttle, within ACZ_TOUCH_GRAB_NEAR, with a button ACZ_TOUCH_GRAB allows, makes the session HANDS ON STICK, with or without a pad: gloves, the card once (ACZ_TOUCH_CARD), `touch: AUTO -> HANDS ON STICK (grab, right hand; ...)`, `pad: active -> Quest Touch (Touch, reason: grab)` when a pad was active. It stays so for the session; a pad press mutes Touch again (the hands let go, the lever back at CRUISE), the next Touch press brings it back. `1`: the GRAB WITH rule as it stands (under `either` the grip from anywhere; a pad-style player's grips are yaw in the modern layout, so a firm squeeze would flip the session). `0`: AUTO never grabs (before). The press rule, also for the switch beside a pad: trigger or grip past 0.65, thumbstick past 0.60 (release 0.35 / 0.30), a button; held 50 ms; 0.5 s after the controller binds, a control already held at binding not until released; not while the active pad holds something past a quarter of its travel or did within 0.5 s, or a pad is forced. Reason in the log: `press, right trigger`. One line in the game's font once a session when a hand is within reach of the stick without pressing (AUTO, Touch active, ACZ_TOUCH_HINTS=0 off): PULL THE TRIGGER TO TAKE THE STICK, 3 s or until the grab | `runtime/src/ps2_touch.c:161` |

| `ACZ_PAD_STICK_REMAP` | `0` | `1` | Gamepad (and Touch PAD) left stick: its travel starts at the game's dead-zone edge instead of the centre (a 4-count inner dead zone), so the first part of the stick's travel is not dead | `runtime/src/ps2_hotas.c:752`, `runtime/src/ps2_hotas.c:760`, `runtime/src/ps2_touch.c:138` (+1 more) |
