| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB_WHAT` | `msaa2` | comma list of `msaa2`, `msaa4`, `cutpre`, `nocutpre`, `cvrs`, `cvrsg`, `cvrsa`, `cvrsd`, `tex`, `gxvoff`, `gwsnap`, `lod1`, `lod2`, `texb`, `cdmed`, `cdlow`, `cqmed`, `cqlow`, `cqa`..`cqd`; measurement only (wrong images): `gxa`..`gxf`, `nocpit`, `vx`, `nocomp`, `nodepth`, `noglass`, `nocut`, `noeq`, `noscr`, `nosm` | [measurement] The items of ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB: `msaa2` / `msaa4` the model at 2 / 4 samples (needs ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MSAA >= 4; the twins are made for it), `cutpre` / `nocutpre` ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_CUT_PREPASS on / off; `nocpit` no model pass at all (no multisampled pass, composite or depth; the veil and the scene light's copy stay), `vx` the model's passes with a 1-pixel scissor and no composite (what is left: vertex / primitive work, clear and resolve), `nocomp` no composite, `nodepth` no depth of the model into the frame (the HUD combiner's passes stay), `noglass` / `nocut` / `noeq` / `noscr` the multisampled pass without its glass / its parts with cut-out texels / the solid parts' colour (their depth pre-pass stays) / the HUD screens; `cvrs` / `cvrsg` / `cvrsa` the shading zones of ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_VRS `1` / `2` / `3`, `cvrsd` the glass's zones while the canopy is dry (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_VRS_DRY's gate, whatever the switch says; perf5_glasszones 2026-11-03), `tex` the textures through the A/B's sampler (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB_ANISO / _LOD_BIAS) (perf4_cockpit 2026-11-03); `gxvoff` the plain glass pipeline (PS2_PERF_GLASS_VARIANTS off), `gwsnap` the canopy's world snapshot copy (PS2_PERF_CANOPY_INPLACE off), `gxa`..`gxf` the glass with the parts of ACZ_VR_GLASS_AB_MASKS left out; these items also apply in PS2_BENCH_REPEAT's extra drawings, so with PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2 a frame's saving is that of two drawings (= one 120 Hz game + in-between pair under layered smooth motion) (perf5_glassexact 2026-11-03). (perf6_cockpitladder 2026-11-04, ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_DETAIL's rungs) `lod1` / `lod2` = mesh LOD level 1 / 2 (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_LOD; the side file is read for them), `texb` = the textures through the budget's twins (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB_TEX_BUDGET), `cdmed` / `cdlow` = the medium / low level whole (its LOD level, the texture twins, 2 samples where the level has them), `nosm` = no sun shadow map (measurement only: no shadows). These items apply to the frame's own drawing, not to PS2_BENCH_REPEAT's extra ones, so a saving is that of one drawing; `cqmed` / `cqlow` / `cqa`..`cqd` the glass with that CANOPY QUALITY set in both drawings of a PS2_BENCH_REPEAT frame (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_QUALITY, ACZ_VR_CANOPY_QUALITY_AB_MASKS) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:700`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9073`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9177` (+2 more) |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB_CSV` | off | path | [measurement] With ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB: one line per counted frame (frame, field, in-between, item number, GPU ms, item name) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:17001` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_DEPTH_CUT` | on | `0` | The model's depth in the frame (MSAA path) leaves out the see-through texels of parts with cut-out textures (`cockpit_cut.frag`: the colour pass's alpha cut). Before, the vertex-only depth pass wrote them, and the HUD's gun cross, target boxes and labels vanished behind a clear canopy: Su-27 72% of the forward view (canopy.glass, object.001; bug #43, also Su-32/37/47), YF-23A 15%, F-1 and F-5E 3%, the other models 0 | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:11817` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_HIDE` | `mig21bis.cpit=faceplate,radio_note_paper,paper_canvas` | `file=name,name;file=...` (the model file's base name; `*` at the end of a name = any rest; case-insensitive), `0` | Cockpit model parts left out by mesh name, per model, logged at load. Default: the MiG-21bis radar scope's faceplate -- an opaque mask with a narrow sector window 2 mm in front of the screen the radar is shown on, which covered 64 % of it from the design eye (bug #68: "the display is partly covered") -- and the radio-channel card on its sight's bumper (FlightGear writes the channels on it; here a blank ruled card in the middle of the view: "what is this panel ahead?"). `0` = everything as built | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:385` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_CONST_HIDE` | on | `0` | (ck_head 2026-10-28, bug #157: M7 A-10A "not a drop on the glass, no defects, as if there is no glass at all") Cockpit model parts behind a select animation whose terms read only constants (properties the runtime does not have read 0) and that is false are left out at load, as ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_HIDE leaves parts out. Such a part was never drawn (the select hides it every frame), but the load-time choices that pick one glass of a set took it: the A-10A carries its canopy and windscreen twice (F/BCanopyGlass behind "not (an unmapped property == 0)", always hidden, and the drawn F/BCanopyGlass-int copies), and the weather shell (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_WX_ONCE), fx_glass's wear / glare / reflection / dust and the crack glass all went onto the hidden pair: the drawn canopy got nothing (M7 rain, same-frame A/B of the canopy weather: 0 pixels changed, max 3 levels; after: 113 pixels over 16 levels, max 213, as the T-38's 95 / 162). The picture changes only where a load-time choice or bake took such a part (the glass effects; the canopy's interior reflection cube no longer bakes hidden parts). Logged per model (`N parts ... behind a select that is false for good left out`): a10a 102 parts (2 glass) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:8653` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_REAR` | on | `0` | `0` hides the T-38 rear (instructor) cockpit, F-5E-like; only visible looking back over the headrest (rear canopy frame); ~0.2 ms less looking back | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:16021` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_HEAD_FADE` | on, `0.01,0.07` | `0`; `start,full` metres | (ck_head 2026-10-28, bug #156: M7 Gripen C "I can very easily fall back like this; better not allow it": leaning back, the eye went through the seat -- the headrest's dark inside filled the view, then the fuselage from outside) The view fades to black as the eye passes into the 3D cockpit model's solid parts or out through its canopy, in proportion to how far it went: smoothstep(start, full) of the depth past the first surface on the straight way from the design eye to the eye (a neck cannot pass through the headrest or the panel, a head not through the canopy; the deeper eye decides, both eyes alike; past the grid round the cockpit, 1.6 x 1.5 x 2 m, counts as depth too). No timer and no camera stop (holding the camera against the head is the classic VR sickness trigger): the picture never moves and clears the moment the head comes back. The surfaces are marked at load in a 2 cm grid (ps2_headvol.c: every drawn part incl. glass, not the hands; 28-39 ms on the loader thread, 2.4 MB a model) and found 0.8-2.8 cm before the eye reaches them; the default starts the fade about where the near plane (1 cm) begins to cut into the surface and is black about 5 cm past it. Drawn with the G-vision veil (gforce.frag, its pipeline made also when ACZ_IMM_GLOC is off), only while the cockpit model is drawn; nothing is drawn while the way is clear. Clearance from the design eye before it starts (the 22 models): straight back 0.105 m (F-14D) .. 0.885 m (A-10A), up 0.185 (F-16C, MiG-21bis) .. 0.465 m; checking six (eyes 0.10 aside, 0.17 back) fades in the F-14D, Gripen C (headrest 0.105 / 0.145 m behind the design eye: the eyes 4-5 cm into it, which showed its inside before) and a little in the J35J. `cockpit: head fade:` lines log each event (how deep, where). Inspect a model offline: tools/cockpit/cpit_headvol.c | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:8633` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MODELS` | none | `id=file[,id=file...]` | More cockpit models by player aircraft id; an id's first entry wins, an id without one gets ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MODEL. The launcher fills it from cockpits/map.txt; ids: docs/aircraft_ids/README.md | `runtime/src/ps2_profile.c:198`, `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:1819`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:12109` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_CONTROLS_LAG` | `60` | ms, `0` | Time constant of the throttle lever and the pedals following their value (a button press would teleport the lever); the stick follows the game's value as is | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:12346` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_ENGINE_LAG` | `1` | `0` (as before), k | (bug #326, 2026-11-06: the owner in the T-38, M1: "two indicators in front of me were going crazy") The engine gauges (N1, EGT, fuel flow, oil pressure: the T-38's two tachometers and their drums, the F-15's and F-16's engine dials) follow the game's power through a turbojet's spool instead of stepping with the button: N1 lags 1.5 s near idle to 1.0 s near military (1.4 x on the way down), EGT 0.6 s behind the engine with a rise while it accelerates, fuel flow 0.3 s (it leads the rpm), oil pressure 0.4 s; k scales the time constants, `0` = the gauges step with the button as before | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:12390` |
| `ACZ_VR_HANDS` | `auto` | `auto`, `any`, `1`, `0` | (rule changed 2026-11-05, bugs #302 / #307: `auto` = only hands that can grab, in HANDS ON STICK while Touch is the active controller; `any` = before, in every Touch mode whether active or not; `1` always, also with a gamepad; `0` never; the log says `cockpit hands: shown / hidden`) Gloved pilot hands (Godot XR Tools hands, CC0, posed by `tools/cockpit/hands_bake.py`, in the files built with `ac2cpit.py --hands`; a sage flight glove with a sleeve cuff): `auto` = while the Quest Touch controllers are in use (ps2_ctl.touch_mode PAD or STICK), `1` = always (also with the gamepad), `0` = never. A hand holds its control (the right the stick, the left the throttle; moving with them) with the gamepad, in Touch PAD mode, while Touch holds the control, or while its controller is not tracked; in Touch STICK mode a free hand follows its controller's grip pose: pointing (index off the trigger), a fist (grip squeezed) or open; the changes glide over 0.12 s. The model's own pilot hands (F-14D) hide while the gloves show. Cost: ~3.1k triangles a glove (with its cuff), two drawn; included in ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_CONTROLS's A/B numbers | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:12517` |
| `ACZ_VR_HANDS_FALLBACK` | on | `0` | (bug #238, touch_ux 2026-10-29: "the hands are not visible" in the F-5E) A cockpit file without gloves of its own (16 of the 33 in cockpits/ on 2026-10-29: A-10A, ADF-11F, EA-6B, F-1, F-15C, F-20A, F/A-22A, F-35A, F-4E, the F-5E before its rebuild, M2000D, MiG-21bis, Tornado, Typhoon, YF-23A, the Gripen's FlightGear variant) gets those of ACZ_VR_HANDS_FILE at load: its textures, hand slots and meshes appended to the file image before it is parsed. A hand with no pose on a control of its own (these gloves, the Su-32's left hand) follows its controller's grip pose in both Touch modes, also while it holds Touch's virtual stick or lever (a fist while the grip is squeezed, pointing with the index off the trigger, else open); hidden with the gamepad (no controller pose). 18.9k triangles in the file (two of the six poses drawn: ~6.3k), +3 MB of host memory and ~5 MB on the GPU. `0` = such cockpits have no hands (as before) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:10489` |

The loader's one read of a whole 430 MB file filled the SATA SSD's queue for ~0.9 s while the game read the mission's data from the disc image on the same drive: with both files cold, no new game frame for 790-845 ms at the mission start (`cockpit: aircraft change: ... longest interval`; 4 MB: 126-142 ms, 1 MB: 46-48, 0.25 MB: 32, the scene's own being 28-30); the read takes ~15 % longer (1.0 s for 414 MB). With a warm cache: no difference. A hint for low I/O priority measured the same and is not used | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:10961` |

Format 3 files, and so the owner's own `cockpits/`, never notice it; the log line "format 4, N BC7 + N BC1 + N RGBA8 textures" tells which way a file went | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:121` |

| `ACZ_VR_MFD_DUAL` | `1` | `0`/`1` | Both maps at once (ck_f15 2026-10-29, bug #192; the user's wish 2026-10-28 23:08:44 "in the right monitor the local map, in the left the global one"): a cockpit with a round radar scope beside its radar / map display (screen 4 beside screen 1 or 3; of the built cockpits only the F-15C: the TEWS beside the VSD, F15C_TEWS; since 2026-10-31 also a rectangular radar display, screen 6: the Su-34's two upper displays, SU34_DUAL_MAPS) shows the game's small radar on the scope and its big map on the display at the same time, both live. The game draws one of the two a frame (the HUD manager update 0x24D9D8 picks them from the map button in f12: hud +0xF0 = 1 held, f12 > 0.1 = the map; else a toggle of the mode byte hud +0x36D); while the renderer shows both screens in cockpit frames and the player's view is the cockpit (player +0x1AA4 = 2), zero_vr.c makes the game frames alternate radar / big map (30 Hz each at the game's 60) and puts the big map's opening state back before each map frame (hud +0x2FC..+0x308, +0x368, +0x369: the radar frame resets it), so the map stays fully open; each screen keeps its block's last image (the map's copies have a place of their own in RT_MFD, 200 px right and 210 px up of the game's HUD). The HUD views are untouched (radar, or the map while the button is held, as before); in this cockpit view the map button changes nothing (both are shown) and a toggle state is kept and put back. Sim M2 F-15C: radar in 300 and big map in 300 of every 600 fields, both screens steady in consecutive frames, the HUD view's radar unchanged before and after. GPU (exclusive 4x multiview runs, the user's settings, governor off, M2 F-15C looking at both screens, two pairs of 12 windows of 5 s): -0.008 ms per stereo frame against `0` (window deltas sd 0.018 ms: within the noise; the game draws one block a frame either way); EE: a few memory reads and writes a frame. `0` = the scope shows the radar (dark while the map button is held), the VSD the radar or the big map while it is held | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:14504`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3878` |
| `ACZ_VR_MFD_DUAL_HOLD` | `4` | game frames `1`..`120` | With ACZ_VR_MFD_DUAL: how long a screen keeps showing its block's last image while the game draws the other one (counted in game frames by the frame list's XR number; in-between and re-rendered frames do not count); after that it goes dark (a pause menu, a stalled HUD) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:14505` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_MFD_DUAL` | off | N | [debug] Log every N-th alternation (`mfd-dual:`): the frame (radar / map), the big map's input, size, target, start and frames left, and the counts | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3879` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_MFD` | off | `1` | [debug] Log the HUD block detection (radar, weapons, SPEED, ALT, heading tape, ladder, brackets, SCORE, TIME, big map, wingman commands) and each new big-map window; since 2026-10-28 (pt_excal) also every draw clipped to the radar's or the big map's window that does not become part of that block (`mfd: field N: a draw in the radar / map window ... is not in its block`: class, marker, block, vertices' box, depth, colours; the first 60, then every 300th) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:36602` |
| `ACZ_VR_HUD_CLIP_CLASS` | on | `0` | (pt_excal 2026-10-28, bugs #184 / #185) The VR draw classes judge a 2D draw by the part its scissor lets through: full width / height = a screen pass (drawn where the game put it, not scaled), full in both = no HUD scaling, the HUD's middle zone by its centre. M9 "Excalibur": the HUD radar draws the laser as long red translucent triangles (alpha 90 -> 27) clipped to the radar's window 15..123 x 290..420; their vertices reach 410-720 PS2 px tall (> 0.9 x 448), so they were taken as a screen pass: a red wedge at the left edge of the eye over the cockpit, following the head (21:09:31 "some red thing dangles on the left, very eye-catching"), and never on the radar's cockpit screen (21:11:26 "the laser does not show on the normal radar, only on the big map": the big map's smaller scale kept the wedge short, a HUD draw). Now they are radar draws: on the cockpit screen with the radar (in the HUD radar without a screen). Post effects, fades and flashes have the frame's own scissor: unchanged. `0` = the vertices' box as before | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:35509` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_MFD_FIELD` | off | field | [debug] Log every HUD draw of this field with its block | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:36603` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_HOLD` | on | `0` | Once detected, the R3 cockpit view is held while the game's own view (pad player +0x1AA4) is the cockpit and no event holds the camera, with the player update in the last 8 fields or a menu over the flight (pause, RETRY reload, crash screen); entering still needs > 30 game cockpit draws in a field. `0` = the per-field count alone (head turns to the side/back flipped cockpit/outside every 2-3 frames, bug #19) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:4335` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_CKVIEW` | off | N | [debug] Log the cockpit-view detection: renderer every N-th scene field and each change (cockpit draws, hold, result); EE side every N-th field (head yaw/pitch, game view byte and age, hold, cockpit, engaged, menu, event; the game camera's own yaw / pitch at that update: in the cockpit view its look-around) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:40056`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:441` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_FRAME_OFF` | off | N lines | [debug] Log the frame's published GS offset (`frame-off:`): each change, frames with 3 or fewer world GS draws, every 300th; with the count of world draws at 0,0 and others and the last other one | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:5033` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_FRAME_OFF_ZERO` | off | `from,to` fields | [test] Recreates bug #65's frames: in those fields only the sky's 0,0 fill may set the frame offset (with PS2_XR_FRAME_OFF_CHECK=0 the cockpit and our clouds vanish) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:35475` |

## Sky, clouds and weather

Keeping Zero's sky, clouds and sun right in head-tracked frames (`runtime/src/zero_vr.c`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c`). There are no weather-specific variables; `PS2_VRS` (coarse shading of the cloud sprites) is listed under the renderer.

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_HEAD` | on (with head tracking) | `0` | Build the cloud field and sky layers with the head-turned camera, so clouds do not follow the head in world-locked frames | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7148` |
| `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_UPRIGHT` | on | `0`, `old` | Turn the cloud layer by the difference between the drawn roll and the roll the game placed the clouds with (0 with `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_HEAD`), and each sprite upright about its own centre (head roll plus the perspective tilt of verticals); `old` = the behaviour before 2026-10-20 (double roll, bug #18; debug A/B) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:36902`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7153` |
| `ACZ_VR_SKY_ANGLES` | on (with head tracking and `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_HEAD`) | `0` | Give the sky camera the head-turned angles during the sky draw, so the dome and horizon haze stay with the world in cockpit frames | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7214` |
| `ACZ_VR_SUN_SCALE` | `1.0` | multiplier | Shrink the sun flare, glow and ghosts to the game's angular size, like the HUD | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:38275` |
| `ACZ_VR_SKY_ROOT` | off | `1` (default root 0x1618A0) or a hex address | [experimental] Give recently head-turned view contexts their head-turned camera during the sky's frame draw | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7205` |
| `ACZ_VR_SKY_CAM` | on | `0` | Rebuild the sky camera struct's rotation-only matrices (+0xB0/+0xF0) from the head-turned view during the sky draw: the cloud deck/ceiling planes move with the world | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7219` |
| `ACZ_VR_SKY_SITES` | off | `1` | [experimental] Also head-turn the extra camera updates for sky, haze and environment passes; no visible effect found | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:299` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SKY` | off | any | [debug] Log the sky's view contexts, the swapped sky angles and camera rebuild deltas (from field 1500) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:930`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:999`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1028` (+3 more) |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SKY_ADDYAW` | off | radians | [debug] Add this much yaw to the head-turned sky angles (`ACZ_VR_SKY_ANGLES`) to exaggerate the effect | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1104` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_NEARMESH` | off | `1` | [debug] Log depth and Z saturation of near native meshes not detected as cockpit | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:37896` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SKY_YAW` | `0` | degrees | [debug] Turn the rebuilt sky matrices about the vertical axis (tests what depends on them) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1028` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SKY_KEEP` | off | any | [debug] Leave the rebuilt sky camera in place after the draw | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1067` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_CLOUD_UPRIGHT` | `0` | N | [debug] Log the cloud upright rotation every N XR frames | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:36905` |
| `PS2_DEBUG_SKIP_KIND` | `0` | `1` cloud sprites, `2` sky, `3` horizon haze, `4` horizon band | [debug] Drop one kind of display draw | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:38532` |
| `ACZ_VR_FX_ANGLES` | on | `0` | Give the effects camera (0x17E8A0: snow/rain particle box, billboards) the head-turned angles during its build, so the snow volume surrounds the gaze instead of the aircraft's nose | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7225` |
| `ACZ_VR_FX_SIZE` | on | `0` | The game's effect sprites (smoke, explosions, fire, missile and wreck smoke, glows, the rain / snow streak quads) sized for the head-tracked projection (ov_visual 2026-10-25; the audit bug #25 left open): the effects camera block 0x17E8A0 read the game's screen distance (ctx+8, 571) for its projection (0x17ECA8 -> 0x1069A8) and for the d / 1/d terms the effect programs scale their sprites with (block+0x6000..+0x6008, 0x17EECC), while its world->screen matrix was built with the eye's (232 on the simulator): every effect sprite was d_game / d_eye = 2.46x too large against the world in VR (fat, short missile trails; a burning target or a smoke column filling the whole eye with a grey disc when flown near: the user's M4 recording 10-23 21:27:12 / 21:27:19). For the duration of 0x17E8A0 the context now holds the d and virtH its head-turned update built the projection with (found with ACZ_DEBUG_CAMREAD): missile trails thin and full-length (they were fat and cut short), fires and smoke at the 2006 size. GPU (exclusive 4x sim, M10 combat with missiles, governor off, ACZ_VR_FX_SIZE_AB=30, 2 runs): +0.043 / +0.052 ms per stereo frame (more trail sprites drawn now). `0` = the game's d (old) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7225` |
| `ACZ_VR_FX_SIZE_AB` | off | fields N | [test] ACZ_VR_FX_SIZE on in even, off in odd blocks of N fields (`fx-size A/B:` lines; N = 1 logs every frame with its game frame number, so PS2_XR_DUMP_PAIR=2 dump pairs compare the same moment with and without) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7225` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_CAMREAD` | off | fields N | [debug] Which game functions read a head-tracked camera context's screen distance (ctx+8) after the head hook gave the game its own d back: the context's RAM page is unmapped (ps2_hw.c trap pages; every access served from RAM and reported), reads on the EE thread are counted per (r31 = inside the reader, context, value) and logged every N fields (`camread:`). Slow; diagnostic only | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1298` |
| `ACZ_IMMERSE` | on | `0` | Immersion master switch: canopy water, rumble and sound driven by the game's precipitation amount and type (sky+0xB8C / env+0x88), damage events (0x1FCD28) and destroyed units (0x1FC880: the debris of kills within 1.5 km (ACZ_IMM_DEBRIS), explosions within 400 m) | `runtime/src/ps2_gforce_audio.c:251`, `runtime/src/ps2_gforce_audio.c:806`, `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:301` (+5 more) |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY` | on | `0` | Water drops / streaks (moving aft with airspeed), mist and debris soot on the 3D cockpit canopy glass (the canopy and windscreen: ACZ_VR_CANOPY_INNER) | `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:293` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_WX_ONCE` | on | `0` | (cv_canopy 2026-10-27, bug #134) Canopies modelled as two or more shells a centimetre or two apart (F-16C CanopyForward/BackInside + ...Outside; F-15C CanopyGlass / CanopyGlassInside and its windscreen four times; A-10A F/BCanopyGlass and their -int copies: the outer glass meshes whose bounding sphere repeats an earlier one within 3 cm, logged `glass mesh N repeats the shell of mesh M`) take the canopy weather (water, mist, film, ice, grime, debris flecks, soot) on the first shell only -- the one fx_glass and the cracks already used; before every shell drew it (two independent bead sets, the film and mist over each other: alpha 0.5 -> 0.75; after an M3 cloud exit 42 % of the F-16C's pixels differed by > 8 levels between one and two shells, mean +5.4 levels brighter). `PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=wxonce`: the same-frame A/B. `0` = every shell as before | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:8579` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_OUTER_R` | `0.25` | metres | ACZ_VR_CANOPY_INNER: glass meshes with a bounding radius up to this are inner glass (instrument covers, sights, combiners: 0.21 m or less in the 23 models; canopies and windscreens 0.53 m or more) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:8499` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_TEST` | off | `wet,mist,soot,kt[,smear[,intensity[,frozen,frost]]]` | [test] Force the canopy effect inputs (look tests on the simulator): wetness, mist, soot 0..1, airspeed kt, wet smear 0..1, drop intensity 0..1 (0.45 = the drops as before), frozen share of the water 0..1, rime / frost cover 0..1 (the ice values need ACZ_VR_CANOPY_ICE on) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13944` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_TEST_SEQ` | off | `field:wet,mist,grime,kt[,smear[,intensity[,frozen,frost[,film]]]];field:...` (at most 8) | [test] (perf5_glassexact 2026-11-03) ACZ_VR_CANOPY_TEST's values switched at those fields (the last one reached holds; before the first, the canopy as the game has it; a 9th value holds the condensation film): one run's eye dumps see several canopy states (the glass's same-frame A/B per state) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13925` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_QUALITY` | `high` (unset) | `high`, `medium`, `low`; the ini key `canopy_quality`, F4 > Enhancements > Expert (ini): HIGH (unset) / MEDIUM (`medium`) / LOW (`low`); the QUALITY PRESET sets it (a restart item) | (perf6_glassladder 2026-11-04; the rungs for the MEDIUM / LOW presets) `high` = the glass as before: no new pipeline, no primitive shading rate; frozen and optimised, the glass module's seven variants and the plain case are the same instructions as main's (tools_gl/spv_same.sh), same-frame flips `gxv` byte-identical at 4x. `medium` / `low` keep every part on the glass (water, frost and rime, film, mist, grime, soot, flecks, cracks, the wear) and shade it with less work. Both: the cheaper noise (cockpit.frag GXQ_*: the water's value noise on the ice's one-round hash; where the water gathers from three sines; the film from one noise octave + two sines with 3 blur taps instead of 6; grime and soot from one octave, no soot grain: other patterns, the same statistics; the drops stay in their cells), plus the glass's shading zones where the device has a primitive shading rate (the rings around each eye's axis: 1x1 inside, 2x1 / 1x2 to the second ring, 2x2 beyond; `medium` 30 / 40 deg, `low` 20 / 30 deg; not in the recordings' cinematic view). Beyond the rings small drops and dust specks are soft, a 1-px scratch fainter. Without a shading rate (GTX 10xx, RX 5xx class) only the noise saves. The rung's bits go into the variants' masks, so the exact savings stay. Only the multisampled pass's glass has rungs (COCKPIT ANTI-ALIASING OFF: plain glass); PS2_PERF_GLASS_VARIANTS=0: high. Load: 10 more pipelines at a cockpit's load when a rung is configured (20 with the 2-sample twins the governor asks for), none at high. Measured (simulator on the 3090 Ti, exclusive, the user's settings, governor off, PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2, in-run ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB items `cqmed` / `cqlow`, ms per 120 Hz pair, glass class): M1 overcast, natural weather, HIGH -> medium / low saving, 4x: MiG-31 1.29 -> -0.65 / -0.78, Rafale M 1.62 -> -0.87 / -1.03, F-117A 1.26 -> -0.60 / -0.73; X-02S M18 0.67 -> -0.33 / -0.40; 3x medium MiG-31 0.78 -> -0.38, Rafale M 0.97 -> -0.51; 2x low MiG-31 0.43 -> -0.24, Rafale M 0.49 -> -0.29; inside the presets (ACZ_QUALITY_PRESET, F-5E, M1): MEDIUM 3x 0.61 -> -0.31 (the whole GPU pair 4.15 ms, -7.5 %), LOW 2x 0.32 -> -0.17 (2.60 ms, -6.7 %). Forced states, Rafale M 4x: dry 0.47 -> -0.20 / -0.25, light rain 1.05 -> -0.52 / -0.60, heavy rain + mist 1.18 -> -0.55 / -0.68, frozen drops + rime 1.35 -> -0.65 / -0.85, film + mist 1.38 -> -0.80 / -0.96, grime + smear 0.97 -> -0.49 / -0.59, grime + soot + flecks 1.41 -> -0.82 / -0.94, dry + cracks 0.80 -> -0.35 / -0.45. The shader parts alone (no shading rate): dry and cracks 0, rain -0.09, frost -0.11, film -0.39, grime -0.14, grime + soot -0.36. No terrain pack in these runs. The headset is not measured: x1.15-1.75 of the simulator's figures (disputed). NOTES_ZERO_VR.md perf6_glassladder has the table per aircraft and state | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9165` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_QUALITY_AB_MASKS` | off | `mask[:zones[:r1:r2]],...` (up to 4 sets) | [measurement] (perf6_glassladder) The sets of the ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB items and PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP names `cqa`..`cqd`: bit mask of the rung's parts (cockpit.frag GXQ_*: 32768 noise on the cheap hash, 65536 the film, 2097152 the clumps, 8388608 grime and soot; both rungs = 10584064), the shading zones (0 none, 1 the dry gate, 2 always) and their rings in degrees (default 30, 40) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9171` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_QUALITY_TEST` | off | `mask:zones[:r1:r2],mask:zones[:r1:r2]` | [test] (perf6_glassladder) The medium and the low rung's own parts and zones instead of the built-in ones | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9180` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_ICE` | F4 Immersion "Ice on the canopy when cold" (on) | `0` | Canopy ice, visual only (bug #24): above the freezing level the canopy water freezes where it sits (milky drops with small glints, beads and rivulets stop moving, frozen drops do not blow off), in cloud rime / frost grows from the sides inwards (feathery, the windscreen ahead stays clearest; full cover after ~35 s of dense cloud); below the freezing level it melts back into drops that run aft (2-10 s); in dry cold air it sublimates over minutes. Since 2026-10-30 the pace by the glass's temperature and the latent heat (ACZ_IMM_ICE_TIMELINE) and the frost drawn as rime (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_RIME). GPU only while there is ice on wet glass or frost (+0.06..0.09 ms per stereo frame on top of the wet canopy at 4x multiview); overrides the F4 setting | `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:294` |
| `ACZ_IMM_FREEZE_M` | `2700` m; `0` in the snow missions 1, 12, 17, 18 and wherever snow falls | metres, or `m1:field:m2` | Canopy ice: the freezing level in metres of altitude as the cockpit altimeter shows it (the glass is colder by 6.5 K per km above it); `m1:field:m2` switches from m1 to m2 at that vblank field (melt tests) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3329` |
| `ACZ_IMM_ICE_RAM` | `0` | factor `0`..`1` | Canopy ice: warm the glass by this share of the airflow's ram rise v^2 / 2 cp (1 = physical: +21 K at 450 kt; fast flight then melts the ice) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13168` |
| `ACZ_IMM_DROP_INTENSITY` | on | `0` | Canopy drop size by how hard the water comes (bug #27): in cloud from the in-cloud density and the cloud type (C1 type: a thin veil or a cloud's edge = fine sparse droplets, a dense stratus deck ~ the old drops, the dense core of a cumulus = larger drops and streaming rivulets; grows over ~5 s in the core, relaxes over ~12 s), rain by its amount, snow small; `0` = the fixed drops as before | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13233` |
| `ACZ_IMM_WATER_FOLLOW` | on | `0` | Canopy water fed only while water arrives (bug #51): in cloud the supply rises with the cloud feel (~2.5 s) and stops within ~1 s after the cloud, rain by its amount; the airflow then blows the water off (half-life ~1.4 s at speed, ~4.6 s slow) and the condensation film clears with it (~3.3 s at speed); frozen drops stay (they only sublimate). M3 exit (same flight, A/B): water 0.69 -> 0.40 / 0.14 / 0.03 at +2 / +4.5 / +8 s (before 0.76 -> 0.73 / 0.66 / 0.55). `0` = as before: the ~15 s humidity memory holds the water up and the film clears over ~7 s | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13418` |
| `ACZ_IMM_WATER_EXIT` | on | `0` | (cv_canopy 2026-10-27, bug #134, M3 F-16C leaving a cumulus at ~320 kt: "where does so much new water come from? some remains, fine, but not this much; the wetness stays") The canopy water after a cloud by the physics at speed: no water arrives behind the cloud's edge (the supply falls with the in-cloud value itself); the water is two parts -- beads and rivulets (0.6 of what arrives), blown off at 0.35 + 1.4 q per s (q = dynamic pressure / that at 300 kt, at most 2.5: half-life 0.4 s at 300 kt, 1 s at 150 kt, 2 s at rest), and fine droplets (0.4) that sit in the boundary layer and evaporate at (0.08 + 0.2 v / 300 kt) x (1 - 0.85 in-cloud) per s (half-life 2.5 s at 300 kt, 9 s at rest); the fine droplets alone draw as small sparse droplets (drop intensity 0.3), in steady cloud as before; the condensation film is sheared off at (0.3 + q) x the airflow factor per s (tau 0.9 s at 300 kt; before 3.3 s), the mist at 0.7 + 1.5 q per s (before 0.7). Sim, M3 F-16C, the same exit at 324 kt (old / new, the same frames): water 0.70 / 0.31 at the cloud's edge, +0.8 s 0.56 / 0.18, +1.9 s 0.38 / 0.10, +3 s 0.24 / 0.065, +4.8 s 0.12 / 0.036, +6.5 s 0.055 / 0.019 (the rest fine droplets); film 0.76 / 0.37, 0.66 / 0.18, 0.52 / 0.05, 0.39 / 0.01; frozen drops still stick (ice). A second state integrated next to the old one: `PS2_PERF_AB=1 PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=waterexit` draws the other in the same frame; PS2_DEBUG_CANOPY logs both. `0` = as before | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13450` |
| `ACZ_IMM_WET_S` | `0.8` (s) | `0` = old, `0.1`..`10` | Canopy water from cloud (bug #71): the time constant of the water's rise from 150 m/s up (the supply rises in half of it, the condensation film in 1.5 x), slower in proportion to the airspeed below that (0.1 of the rate at rest: the collected water is E x LWC x V). Low cumulus is warm liquid cloud (0.1-0.5 g/m3): at 150-250 m/s a windscreen collects some 20-30 g/m2 of water a second, so drops and a film show within seconds of entering. M3 F-14D cumulus entry (same flight, with `ACZ_IMM_WET_CURVE`): water 0.13 / 0.46 / 0.69 at +2.2 / +3.7 / +6 s (before 0.09 / 0.21 / 0.42); after the exit as before (0.36 at +4 s). The dry-off is `ACZ_IMM_WATER_FOLLOW`'s. `0` = the old rates (supply ~2.2 s, water ~1.7 s, film ~2.9 s at speed, 0.3 of them at rest) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13469` |
| `ACZ_IMM_WET_CURVE` | `0.25,0.75` | `lo,hi` (0..1), `0` = linear | Canopy water, condensation film and mist from the in-cloud value (bug #73, M2 start "textures still murky and they move"): below `hi` their targets are hi x smoothstep(lo, hi, value), above it the value itself -- haze and cloud edges hold too little water to wet a canopy, a cloud's water rises steeply into its core. The M2 take-off set's band reaches below the airfield (in-cloud 0.39-0.40 on the runway, ~3.8 km visibility): the glass took water 0.31, film 0.36, mist 0.24 on the take-off roll -- condensation patches and rivulets drifting aft over the ground in view; now 0.12 / 0.13 / 0.09 (a few drops). Cumulus cores (0.8+) unchanged. `0` = linear as before. The cloud feel (veil, cockpit light, sound, rime) keeps the plain value | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13495` |
| `ACZ_IMM_EVENT_HOLD` | on | `0` | While a mission event holds the camera (player +0x1A68 flag 0x2000: mission 2's take-off clearance, cutscenes) the canopy, the cloud feel, the immersion sounds and rumble hold as in a pause: the camera is not the pilot's and the airspeed reads 0 (bug #44). `0` = they run on during events (before) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3738` |
| `ACZ_IMM_RESET` | on | `0` | The canopy starts dry and the cloud feel at rest at a mission start (GameControl::enter) and when the aircraft is put somewhere else -- more than 300 m (+ 600 m/s) between flight frames, or more than 300 m across a stage (re)load (sky / cloud object restarting): a RETRY, mission 2's take-off roll giving way to the flight (bug #44; logged `canopy state reset`). Before, a wet or iced glass carried over through the menus into the next mission or past a RETRY. Since bug #268 (ACZ_IMM_RESET_KEEP, default) a jump alone no longer resets the canopy: only a mission start, the game's mission (re)start (RETRY, RESTART) or the aircraft's restore. `0` = never reset | `runtime/src/ps2_cracks.c:1576`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13959`, `runtime/src/zero_cracks.c:433` |
| `ACZ_IMM_RESET_RESPAWN` | on | `0` | (cv_canopy 2026-10-27, bug #133, M5 campaign: "is this crack left from the last game? the aircraft is not damaged") The same canopy reset as ACZ_IMM_RESET also when the player's aircraft is restored: once it has taken damage since the last reset (a hit, a crash), the first rise of its HP (s16 unit +0x398) to above 0 seen in a flight frame (the HUD manager runs; not during the crash replay, which re-enacts the last 15 s with its own HP -- 255 in M5 -- and damage calls) asks zero_vr.c for the reset (logged `canopy state reset (the player's aircraft restored: a respawn ...)` and `cracks: the player's aircraft restored`): cracks, debris chips and soot, water, mist, film, ice, grime, flecks, the cloud feel, the trails, the governor's new flight. Once per restore (a further rise needs new damage first); normal hits never reset. While the aircraft is down (HP <= 0 after a hit) the replay's damage calls make no cracks and leave the HP scale alone; the HP scale (the HP at the mission start) is kept through a restore. The game has no repair: in M5 the checkpoint restart (RESTART after a crash) put the aircraft back without a stage load or a jump between flight frames, so before only the next stage load (6 min later) cleared the glass. `0` (or ACZ_IMM_RESET=0) = as before | `runtime/src/zero_cracks.c:433` |
| `ACZ_IMM_RESET_KEEP` | on | `0` | (canopy_keep 2026-11-02, bug #268, M18 X-02: "when I went from the second phase to the third phase ... my glass was miraculously restored") The canopy is reset only where the aircraft really starts afresh, each time at the next flight frame: a new mission (GameControl::enter, also after the menus), the game's mission (re)start 0x1D1F00 -- run by the mission's start, the pause menu's RETRY and MISSION FAILED's RESTART (logged `mission (re)start 0x1D1F00 at field N (from RA: ...)`) -- and the aircraft's restore (ACZ_IMM_RESET_RESPAWN: its HP rising after damage; a repair, if the game makes one, counts). A jump of the aircraft alone -- more than 300 m between flight frames or across a stage load: a stage change within the mission (M18's phase 2 -> 3 moved it 9123 m; M17's take-off sequence -> flight 34.8 km; M12 after the nukes; the stages of M13 / M15) -- keeps the canopy: cracks, chips, soot, water, mist, film, ice, grime, flecks, the cloud feel and the cracks' HP scale (logged `the canopy kept`, with the player's HP; M18 keeps the damage over its phases: 2026-10-29 HP 52, 20, 29 before and after each; where the game restores the aircraft at such a change -- M17's take-off sequence -> flight put a damaged HP 50 back to 90 in the sim -- ACZ_IMM_RESET_RESPAWN resets the canopy); it still starts a new flight for what belongs to the place (the trails, the debris model's kills, the governor's fresh window: ps2_imm_flight). Without the 0x1D1F00 hook (code mismatch, logged) the old rule stays. `0` = as before: every such jump resets the canopy | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1609` |
| `ACZ_MENU_RETRY_EDGE` | on | `0` | (menu_1105b retry_edge 2026-11-05, bug #318, the owner's answer) A new attempt counts as a next mission for the page's rows: the pause menu's RETRY and MISSION FAILED's RESTART publish what waits, at the mission (re)start 0x1D1F00. START VIEW, BUILDINGS, TERRAIN LOOK and TAKE-OFF RECENTRE keep waiting for the game to enter a mission (the log says why, docs/SETTINGS.md). `0`: nothing is published there (the page's words still say RETRY). No GPU cost; about 100 us on the game thread, once | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1629` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_ICE_AB` | off | seconds`[,drops]` | [test] Draw the canopy ice only in every other period of that many seconds (logged `canopy ice A/B: ice on/off`): an in-run GPU A/B against the class timer's 5 s windows (`a private script`); `,drops`: the off periods also send the old drop intensity 0.45 (drop-intensity A/B) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13243` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_BEAD_CREEP` | `0.25` | `0`..`1` (`1` = the classic speed) | (cv_drops2 2026-10-29) ACZ_VR_CANOPY_DROPS=lensdrops only: how fast the beads creep aft, as a share of the classic 0.5 % of the airspeed. At 0.5 % a bead moved ~7 mm -- its own size -- between two 120 Hz frames at 325 kt (M16 dump sequence: no bead could be followed from one frame to the next, the field of drops strobed); 0.25 = 0.12 % of the airspeed, ~1.7 mm a frame at 325 kt: pinned beads that creep while the rivulets and trails carry the speed (the rivulets keep their 2 %). Their own offset, so classic keeps its motion | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:13308` |

| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_SCRATCH` | `1` | strength `0`..`4` (`0` = off) | The canopy as a material (wish 2026-10-23): the acrylic's own wear lit by the sun through it -- hairline scratches (hundreds a square decimetre, a few long wiping strokes), polishing swirls, pits, dust specks, dried water-spot rings, crazing near the frame (the glass's edge distance is computed at load). Each lights only where it scatters the sun towards the eye: a line where its tangent is square to the sun-eye geometry, so the lit hairlines line up on circles around the sun's image (the halo of a worn windscreen) and a swirl lights as a streak through its middle pointing at the sun; forward scatter falling off with the angle from the sun (lines ~11 deg, specks ~16 deg e-fold; looked up within 23 deg, faded out from 17); nothing with the sun behind the glass, none below the horizon; dimmed by our clouds' shadow at the aircraft and inside cloud; hidden where water, condensation film or frost cover the glass. In glass space (the cockpit frame: no UVs, no screen-space noise), each eye with its own geometry as real scratches (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_GLASS_EYES); sub-pixel lines drawn at least 1.3 px wide with their energy kept (steady without TAA). Canopy and windscreen meshes only (glass wider than 0.12 m, not the HUD's combiner, not an inner copy of the glass). The sun from C1 (S1's angles; the game's model light is not the sun). GPU (exclusive in-run A/B ACZ_VR_CANOPY_GLASS_AB=10, user config, 4x multiview, governor off, cockpit class timer, 2 runs): looking at the sun through the F-14D canopy all three glass switches +0.082 / +0.085 ms per stereo frame (the wear and glare alone +0.081, the reflection alone +0.056: they share the glass path); away from the sun the wear costs nothing (the glass path returns beyond 23 deg of the sun); ~1.75x on the headset | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9355` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_GLARE` | `1` | strength `0`..`4` (`0` = off) | Veiling glare: a faint brightening of the canopy glass close to the sun (most within ~5 deg, a tail to ~20; the sun's colour), the haze of the finest wear and the acrylic itself, stronger with ACZ_VR_CANOPY_SCRATCH; same gating as the scratches | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9358` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_REFLECT` | `1` | strength `0`..`4` (`0` = off) | The cockpit's interior reflected in the canopy: at load the model's solid parts in front of the pilot (not the seat or what is around the head, not the airframe outside) are drawn once into a cube from the design eye (6 x ACZ_VR_CANOPY_REFLECT_RES^2 RGBA8, 0.4 MB, 1-2 ms of GPU; colours x baked AO, instruments at rest), looked up along the glass's reflection of each eye (parallax: the interior as a sphere of 0.7 m; one bilinear tap), lit by the cockpit's light now, x acrylic's Fresnel (0.04 square on, held below 0.15); it replaces the old uniform grazing reflection where the interior is seen. Faint by day (the windscreen shows the glare shield and the HUD frame against dark ground), more at dusk. GPU looking ahead through the F-14D windscreen +0.037 / +0.036 ms per stereo frame (J35J ~+0.03), only where the glass is seen 50 deg or more off square (nearer square on the acrylic reflects 4-5 %: below a level or two) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9354`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9361` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_REFLECT_RES` | `128` | `32`..`1024` | Face size of the ACZ_VR_CANOPY_REFLECT interior cube | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9372` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_DUST` | `1` | strength `0`..`4` (`0` = off) | (cv_canopy 2026-10-27, bug #142, M3 F-16C: "looking out I feel there is no glass here") Dust and pits on the canopy lit by the sun away from the sun's image too: wherever the sun shines on the glass (from outside, or through the canopy from inside at 0.45: dust scatters forward) the specks of ACZ_VR_CANOPY_SCRATCH's wear layer (pits and dust 0.05-0.3 mm, ~260 a square metre, one cell lookup a pixel) show as faint points at the glass's depth -- the cue that tells the two eyes there is a surface. Strength 1 = 0.35 x 25 (1.4 % of the near-sun peak), a speck at most +64 levels (no fireflies): at 4x the largest a few tens of levels on one or two pixels, the many small ones a few levels; hidden under water and in the airframe's shadow (the sun map). The F-16C's bubble (one piece, no bow in front; the outer shell a uniform gold tint of 0.15: the whole glass changes 57-99 % of the pixels by a few levels, but a uniform tint gives no surface) showed the wear only within 23 deg of the sun before. `PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=dust`: the same-frame A/B (push light.w -3: without the dust and ACZ_VR_CANOPY_REFLECT_ALL) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9366` |
| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_NIGHT` | `0.4` | factor `0`..`1` (`1` = as before) | (night_sky 2026-10-29, bug #196: M11 "the stars are very strange") At night (S5's night flag: M11) the moonlit canopy wear -- scratches, dust specks, glare, crack glints -- x this. The moon lit them at 0.5 of the sun's strength (the game's key light / 0.3) while the night sky behind is ~0.2 of a day sky (M11 zenith 16,26,31): the specks stood out ~2.5x more than by day, dozens of single-pixel points at the glass's depth among the stars (F-15C above M11's smoke: 22 extra points over 10 levels, median +27 levels on a 25-level sky). 0.4 gives them the day's contrast against the sky | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9386` |

| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS` | `tone` (unset; ENHANCEMENTS > COCKPIT > REAR-VIEW MIRRORS: TONE; since 2026-11-04) | `tone`; `live` or a strength `0`..`2` above 0 (LIVE: everything below, the default until 2026-11-04); `sky` (SKY: no live map, ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_LIVE); `0` or anything else (OFF: the glass as the model paints it) | TONE (perf6_mirrors 2026-11-04; the user: "the mirrors can be switched off by default; put light stand-ins that reflect the tonality ... they are useless and don't look good"): silvered glass that carries the scene's tonality and no picture (cockpit.frag mir_tone, a smooth function of each eye's own reflected ray, so it sits at infinity in stereo and slides across the glass with the head): the game's sky (C1's zenith and horizon on the gradient below, the horizon spread over +-ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_TONE_SOFT), under the horizon C1's haze giving way by ~17 deg to the ground's own tone as the eyes see it ahead (the scene light's ground band, ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_SCENE_LIGHT; C1's fog x 0.8 without it), the sun behind as a soft glow without a disc, our far clouds' tone (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_TONE_CLOUDS; no pass of its own), a cloud's grey and a tunnel's gloom as in the other modes, the glass's Fresnel and tint. Nothing else runs: no live map (pass, worker's batches, buffers), no real view (pass, atlas, chunks), no cubes, no mirror terrain (atlas copy, grid), no aim clearance's ray casts (the plain ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AIM, so a raised mirror of LIVE sits lower: F-4E +17..+20 -> +3 deg), no silhouettes (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AIRCRAFT). The solid parts' shader holds TONE's mirror code alone (cockpit.frag CPIT_MIR 2: binary 48 -> 26 KB, the pipeline with 60 registers 56; cockpit class within noise, paired -0.014..+0.006 ms); LIVE / SKY / OFF keep CPIT_MIR 1: the same registers and binary sizes as before TONE. What it saves against LIVE, in-run A/B ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_TONE_AB=10 (exclusive 4x multiview, the user's settings, governor off, PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2, 130 s, the head on the mirrors, ms per stereo frame, whole frame mean +- SE; in brackets the class timers' real view + live map): F-15C M2 +0.076 +- 0.044 and on the final build +0.119 +- 0.046 (0.092 + 0.015), J35J M3 +0.200 +- 0.100 (0.124 + 0.013), Tornado M2 +0.137 +- 0.053 (0.154 + 0.013), F-4E M2 +0.009 +- 0.012 and on the final build +0.109 +- 0.039 (0.105 + 0.008), Mirage 2000D M2 +0.048 +- 0.030 and +0.016 +- 0.023 (0.149 + 0.015: the whole frame does not show all of its passes), Typhoon M2 +0.196 +- 0.052 (0.148 + 0.015), MiG-29A M2 +0.077 +- 0.045 (0.083 + 0.016); the renderer -0.12 +- 0.05 ms a field (9 runs), the graphics worker within noise; nothing with no mirror in view in either mode. TONE against no mirrors (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AB=10, F-15C M2): -0.013 +- 0.059 ms (noise). VRAM freed at 4x (the resources' logged sizes: cubes and depth + live map + atlas + mirror terrain 4.3 + live map buffers 2.7): F-15C 36.6 MB, J35J 37.2, Tornado 41.6, F-4E 36.3, Mirage 2000D 25.7, Typhoon 42.7, MiG-29A 42.1. Cockpit load, warm, exclusive (`default model loaded`): F-15C 252 -> 214 ms, J35J 88 -> 79, Tornado 172 -> 151, F-4E 247 -> 147, Mirage 2000D 234 -> 202, Typhoon 195 -> 178, MiG-29A 206 -> 189. Shimmer, top mirror of the F-15C, head drifting ~0.8 mm a field, 8 consecutive frames, glass only (HUD marks masked): frame-to-frame change 0.933 / 0.710 levels (L / R) with LIVE, 0.007 / 0.047 with TONE; pixels changing by more than 16 levels 1.29 / 0.85 % against 0.00 / 0.08 % (16 px at the box's corner, the glass's rim). LIVE and SKY: rear-view mirrors (qw_mirror, stage 1 of docs/research/2026-10-24/mirrors-lookback.md): the mirror glass of the 9 models that have it (F-15C, Typhoon, MiG-29A, Tornado, J35J and j35j_plain, F-20A, F-4E, Mirage 2000D -- a flat grey or a painted placeholder before: FlightGear's fixed sky-cubemap effect does not convert) reflects what lies behind, by each eye's own ray reflected about the glass, so the reflection lies at infinity (stereo-correct, it moves with the head as a real mirror's distant view does): the game's sky (C1's zenith and horizon colours, which S5 captures from the game's dome, on a gradient fitted to the game's own sky in M2, rms 0.023; below the horizon C1's fog colour darkening to 0.7x within ~10 deg), the sun (fx_glass's: C1's direction, the disc's hue x day x cloud shadow x in-cloud), our far clouds' direction map over it when they run (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_CLOUDS), and the cockpit and airframe behind the pilot (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_INTERIOR). Silvered glass: 0.82 reflectance + the front face's Fresnel at grazing angles, a faint cool tint, 3.5 % of the part's own lit colour as a film; in a cloud the reflection gives way to the cloud's colour. The value scales the reflectance. Which meshes: ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_MESH. GPU (exclusive in-run A/B ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AB=10, the user's settings + clouds3d, 4x multiview, governor off, the cockpit class timer, M2, all of a model's mirrors in view, 2 runs): F-15C -0.001 / +0.015 ms per stereo frame, MiG-29A +0.002 / 0.000, J35J (the most cockpit in its mirrors) +0.016 / +0.007 -- within the windows' noise (the whole frame moved +-0.1 ms either way); ~1.75x on the headset; at load the cube bake 1.2-5.9 ms of GPU and 7 MB | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:810`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9790`, `runtime/src/zero_terrain.c:849` |

| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_INTERIOR` | `1` | `0`..`2` (`0` = sky and clouds only) | Not with TONE (no cube is baked). What lies behind the pilot in the mirrors: the solid parts from the front-most mirror aft (seat and headrest, the canopy frame, a rear cockpit, the exterior parts a model carries such as the J35J's canopy frame and spine), not the hands, the screens, the mirrors themselves, their housings / rims / knobs (small parts around a mirror's glass) or select-animated parts (warning flags, alternative parts), drawn at load into a cube seen from each mirror's glass (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_CUBE_EACH; `0`: one from (0, 0, -0.45 m) in the cockpit frame, among the mirrors) (6 x ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_RES^2 RGBA8, the distance / 4 m in alpha, half the baked AO; 1.2-5.9 ms of GPU at load). Each mirror pixel marches its reflected ray against it (9 steps from 8 cm to 3.2 m and a secant step; a crossing counts only where the surface is no nearer to the cube's centre than the step's segment comes, else the step jumped behind a nearer part such as the headrest), so near parts keep their parallax; lit by the cockpit's light now (the game's ambient + 0.45 x its lights, less in a cloud). Checked offline against true ray casts (a private script, 25 rays x 21 mirrors): hit / miss agree on 95.0 % (the same part 90.7 %; from the design eye 87.4 %). fx_glass's interior cube (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_REFLECT) cannot serve: it leaves out everything behind the eye and the airframe. Above 1 the parts are brighter. Since mir_sharp (2026-10-29, bug #230) the parts are drawn for real from each eye's mirror image (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_REAL, on); the cube and its march stay for `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_REAL=0` and the A/B, and this value is the real view's strength too | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9803` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_CUBE_EACH` | on | `0` (one cube from (0, 0, -0.45 m), as before) | (mir_live 2026-10-29, bug #205) A cube of what lies behind the pilot (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_INTERIOR) for each mirror, seen from the glass's own centre (up to 4; a fifth shares the nearest): the reflected ray starts within the glass's few centimetres of the cube's centre, so the march reads the cube along the ray itself instead of from up to 0.4 m aside. Offline (a private script: each pixel's true first hit against the march over an ideal cube; the F-4E's mirrors aimed +17 deg) the march agreed with the truth on 50 / 26 / 52 % of the pixels from the shared centre -- the rear cockpit's frames and the seat's handles in ragged fragments, dark where it found the seat and sky where it missed it -- and on 93 / 95 / 94 % from each glass; 17 steps instead of 9 from the shared centre: 57 / 35 / 61 %. Cost: the bakes at load (3 cubes 6-29 ms of GPU) and 6.3 MB of memory a cube at 512 (3 mirrors: 18 MB instead of 6); per pixel the same march (the centre from the live map's block) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9574` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_INTERIOR_NEAR` | on | `0` (as before) | (mir_live 2026-10-29, bug #205) The mirrors' cube march: a hit counts only once the ray has been in front of the cube's surfaces. A first step (8 cm from the glass) already behind one lies in the mirror's own surroundings -- the canopy arch the glass sits against -- which the march's 8 cm start meant to leave out: the Tornado's side mirrors showed the arch 4 cm away under a third of the glass, a dark wedge (offline: 33-36 % of the left glass's rays at every aim) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9818` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_INTERIOR_CRISP` | on | `0` (as before) | (mir_live 2026-10-29, bug #205) The mirrors' cube march takes a step only where all four of the cube's nearest texels (textureGather of the distance) hold a surface, and puts the hit at the secant's point only where they do there too (else at the step's own). At a part's silhouette against nothing the bilinear blend of its distance with 0 (and of its colour with black) gave a nearer, darker surface a step could stop at: dark specks scattered over the open sky of the glass (the M2000D's retro mirror, the F-4E's). Asking the four to agree within 10 % as well left light dotted lines where a near part crosses a far one (the J35J's left mirror). One more fetch (a gather) where a step finds a surface | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9816` |

No smoke, no missiles (effects are culled to the game's view). The value is the strength. GPU (exclusive 4x, governor off, M2 F-15C looking at the three mirrors, the cockpit class timer, one pair of 12 windows of 5 s, two aircraft): -0.018 ms against `0` (sd 0.007 / 0.023: below the noise; estimate ~0.002 ms for two aircraft, ~0.01 ms for eight: a few ALU per mirror pixel). | `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_LIVE` | on with ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS=live (ENHANCEMENTS > COCKPIT > REAR-VIEW MIRRORS: LIVE); off with SKY; never with TONE (the default since 2026-11-04) or OFF | `0`; `1` with SKY | The rear-view mirrors' live map (mir_live 2026-10-29, bug #193, step M2 of docs/research/2026-10-24/mirrors-lookback.md; the user 2026-10-28 23:15 "not a single aircraft ... not a real mirror", 2026-10-29 "they must not load the system much"): the other aircraft the game draws every frame -- also behind its camera; they come through the model VU1 program (the aircraft program draws the own aircraft and its stores), whose batches the native path converts anyway -- drawn once per game frame by an aircraft-fixed rear camera (the base camera turned 180 deg about up; its frustum the union of the mirrors' reflected cones from the design eye + ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_LIVE_MARGIN) into a small map with the game's own pipelines, textures, light rows and fog (model -> rear screen rows m' = m S_head^-1 S_rear in the rows' own form; the draw state the GS would have with the batch's setup and prefix registers, nothing sent to the GS), mono, 2 layers with multiview so the frame's own pipeline states serve (no new pipelines). The mirror glass looks it up by each eye's reflected ray, as the sky (at infinity), over stage 1's sky, clouds and haze and under the cube's cockpit parts, its coverage from the map's depth with the colour's bilinear weights; the glass's reflectance and tint as before (no bloom). Frames between game frames reuse it. Left out: the own aircraft and its stores, the game's cockpit (an origin or box within 12 m of the eye), ground objects (an origin within 30 m of the ground, the realistic terrain's heightmap: ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_LIVE_GROUND), beyond ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_LIVE_RANGE; effects and smoke (the game culls them to its view). Strips of one block and state merge into one draw (M2's wingman at 28 m: ~425 strips a frame -> 10 draws). No work at all while off, without a model with mirrors, outside the cockpit view, or while no mirror (with ~15 deg around it) was in view in the last second. ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AIRCRAFT's silhouettes give way to it. Cost (exclusive 4x, governor off, M2 F-15C looking at its mirrors, the wingman 28 m behind in the map: ~400 strips a frame -> 10 draws): GPU 0.008 +- 0.001 ms per stereo frame for the map pass (the class timer, MV=0; p95 0.016) -- the whole frame's in-run A/B (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_LIVE_AB) -0.06 +- 0.10 ms (MV=1), within its noise; the graphics worker 0.07 ms a game frame (its own timer: ~130 ns a strip taken, ~8 ns a batch passed by; the A/B 0.00 +- 0.12); the renderer ~0.08 ms a game frame (recording the pass); the EE nothing (no game call). The cap ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_LIVE_MAXBATCH keeps the worker near 0.1 ms with more aircraft close behind. VRAM: the map W x H x 8 bytes x 2 layers (F-15C 1024 x 312: 5 MB; at most W x W/2) + 3 x 0.9 MB of vertex / index buffers | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:841`, `runtime/src/zero_terrain.c:848` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_TERRAIN` | on with the live map (not with TONE: no atlas copy, no grid) | `0` (C1's haze colour below the horizon, as phase 1) | The rear-view mirrors' terrain (mir_live 2026-10-29, phase 2 = step M3 of docs/research/2026-10-24/mirrors-lookback.md; the user 2026-10-28 23:15 "the ground is very strange"): zero_terrain.c builds a static grid of the loaded map once on its bake thread (ps2_terrain_mirror_grid: 257 x 257 vertices = every second height sample of the game's 513 x 513 heightmap, 400 m apart; each vertex's colour the mean of the game's own atlas texels (64 x 64 PSMT8 per tile, its palette) over the tiles around it x the terrain's vertex colour 90 / 128 x the realistic terrain's relief light at one texel a tile; open water (tile flags 0x2000 / 0x800) at the water's level, the lowest height sample around it) -- the tile grid taken in the bake's own height loop, the atlas and palette copied once on the EE thread with a new map (one memcpy, as the shader water does). The renderer copies a new grid once into one of two vertex buffers and draws it into the live map (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_LIVE) before the aircraft, by the game frame's rear camera, with the model program's depth (aircraft and ground hide each other) and the game's fog (C1's fog colour and near / far distances, by the distance), fading into C1's horizon colour just below the horizon (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_HAZE); only push constants change a frame, nothing on the graphics worker. The map is then drawn whenever a mirror is in view (not only with an aircraft behind). Needs the realistic terrain (ACZ_TERRAIN_REAL, on by default: the heights and the relief); without it the mirror keeps C1's haze. Cost (2026-10-29, sim, exclusive 4x, governor off, M2 F-15C at its mirrors with the wingman behind; in-run A/B with ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_TERRAIN_AB=10): the GPU class `mirrors' live map` 0.012 ms per stereo frame with the terrain against 0.006 without (+0.005 +- 0.000; the whole live map, aircraft and terrain, 0.012 against the 0.06 limit; whole-frame A/B within its noise, -0.03 +- 0.04 MV=1); the graphics worker nothing (0.0685 ms a map, 126 ns a strip, against phase 1's 0.0695 / 131 ns: the grid is static, only the camera's push constants change); the EE once per map: the atlas memcpy, 0.25-0.5 ms (472-890 tiles, 1.9-3.6 MB: M2, M8, M4); the bake thread 2-4 ms per map; the renderer one 1 MB copy per new map; VRAM 3.5 MB (two vertex buffers of 1 MB, a 1.5 MB index buffer, the pipeline: all made at start). | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:5266`, `runtime/src/zero_terrain.c:848` |
| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_TERRAIN_TEX` | on | `0` (the tiles' mean colours, as before) | (mir_live phase 4, 2026-10-29; the user 14:28 in M16: "the reflection is somehow strange ... I see strange textures behind ... this mirror is not very realistic") The mirrors' terrain (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_TERRAIN) textured with the game's own atlas: zero_terrain.c turns each tile of a new map's atlas (64 x 64 PSMT8 + its palette) into 32 x 32 (2 x 2 boxes) with mips to 1 x 1, BC1, on its bake thread (~20 ms a map, with the tile means); the renderer copies them and the tile grid (256 x 256 ids) once into a texture array (1024 layers, 0.7 MB) and an R16 image (128 KB) and the terrain's fragment shader reads the cell's tile at the point (texel column along x, row along z: the game's grid has no flips) times the vertex shade (the terrain's vertex colour x the relief light), into the game's fog as before. Before, each 400 m vertex carried its tiles' mean colour: smooth blotches where the game shows fields, rock, rivers (M8: the map's terrain had a texel spread of 4-5 levels against the frame's 10-14; textured 16-18, mean within a few levels). Cost (2026-10-29, exclusive 4x, governor off, M2 F-15C at its mirrors, in-run A/B of the whole live map): the GPU class `mirrors' live map` 0.013 ms per stereo frame with the textures against 0.012 without them; the mirrors' reading of the map (cockpit class, live map on - off) +0.007 +- 0.015 ms with phase 4's three switches, -0.004 +- 0.015 without (noise); the graphics worker unchanged (0.067-0.071 ms a map); the EE unchanged (the atlas was copied already); the bake thread ~20 ms a new map; VRAM +0.8 MB (the array and the grid; the 0.8 MB staging is host memory). | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:5270`, `runtime/src/zero_terrain.c:852` |

| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AIM_CLEAR` | `0.5` (TONE: not used, it shows no parts: the plain aim, no ray casts) | share `0`..`1`; `1` / `off` = the plain ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AIM | (mir_live 2026-10-29, bug #205) A pilot sets his mirrors to look past what stands behind him: at load each mirror's aim rises from ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AIM, 1 deg at a time, until the parts the mirrors' cube takes fill at most this share of its view -- the reflected rays from 32 points over the glass (from the design eye) that meet one of them 8 cm-4 m away, ray casts on the CPU against the file's triangles (bounding spheres per part and per 32 triangles) -- but never above the model's own aim (its designers'); if no elevation gets there, the one with the least. With the plain +3 the F-4E's mirrors looked straight at the back of the pilot's own seat and the rear cockpit (97-100 % of the glass) and the Tornado's centre mirror at the headrest (56 %): the cube's march found ~70 % of it and showed dark ragged patches. Logged `mirror "..." clearance: the parts behind the pilot fill N % of its view at +3 deg, M % at +E deg (... ms)`. With the cockpit files of 2026-10-29: the F-4E's side mirrors +3 -> +17 deg (the parts fill 100 / 97 % -> 41 / 38 % of the view: its rear canopy frame and the rear seat below, sky above; its own aim +20.5), its centre one +3 -> +20 (100 -> 31 %); the Tornado's centre +3 -> +4 (56 -> 28 %: the headrest); the J35J's top +3 -> +9 (100 -> 47 %); the M2000D's +3 -> +6 (75 -> 50 %); the Typhoon's left +3 -> its own +7 (88 -> 62 %); at +3 as before: the F-15C's (0 / 47 / 0 %), the Typhoon's right and top, the Tornado's sides, the J35J's left (62 %: its own +3.2) and right, the F-20A's, MiG-29A's, Gripen's, Rafale M's and MiG-31's (81 / 78 %: its own aim lies below +3). The time at load: 1-50 ms a mirror (the F-4E's 3: ~125 ms, its 175 k triangles gathered once). | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:874` | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:867` |

| `ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_REAL` | on (LIVE, SKY; never with TONE) | `0` (the cube march, ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_INTERIOR, as before) | (mir_sharp 2026-10-29, bug #230; the user 19:43:50 "what I see behind in the mirror is pixelated", 22:24:20 "the upper mirror, there is something pixelated, a pixel mush. This definitely needs fixing", 22:52:42 "the mirrors still need fixing") What stands behind the pilot in the mirrors -- the headrest, the canopy's frame and rails, the spine and fins -- drawn for real instead of marched through a cube: a flat mirror shows the scene from the eye's mirror image E' = E - 2 ((E - c) . n) n, so every frame (in-between frames too: the eyes move) and for each eye and each mirror within ~15 deg of the view, cockpit.vert draws the model from E' with the glass's own axes as the camera (x along the glass, y = n x x, looking through it along n) and a projection that lays the glass's extent onto the mirror's slot of an atlas at k texels a metre (texel = k (u - u0), k (v1 - v): the glass reads it by its own point, cockpit.frag mirv_look; the plane is the glass's centre with the aimed normal, ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_AIM), the near plane at the glass (what lies behind the mirror is clipped); the same lighting as the cockpit's solid parts (the game's light block, the scene light, the sun's shadow map, flashes, the cloud light), 4x multisampled and resolved (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_REAL_MSAA), alpha = coverage over the far scene (sky, our clouds, the live map). The parts are culled by bounding spheres -- at load the solid parts that may stand in front of each mirror, every part also in chunks of 32 triangles (file order) and groups of 16 chunks -- once for each eye and mirror and kept while that eye stays within 5 mm (a margin for it), into runs of chunks a draw; parts an animation moves are tested every view (the renderer's recording: 0.056 ms a view, F-15C, all three mirrors; one culling ~0.15 ms). The cube march drew the F-15C top mirror's view over the headrest (47 % of its rays meet a part at its +3 deg aim) as terraced, stair-stepped streaks 3-4 eye pixels wide, different in each eye and changing with every millimetre of head motion (head drifting 0.2 mm a frame: pixels changing by more than 16 levels from frame to frame 0.54 / 0.88 % of the glass with the march, 0.18 / 0.24 % with the real view; by more than 32: 0.04 / 0.10 % against 0.00 / 0.00 %). Cost (sim, exclusive 4x, governor off, M2 F-15C with all three mirrors in view, in-run A/B ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_REAL_AB=10, main 78152ac): the pass 0.056 +- 0.001 ms per stereo frame MV=1 (p95 0.061; MV=0 0.051), the cockpit model class +0.031 +- 0.012, the whole frame -0.053 +- 0.072 (noise): at most ~0.09 ms (sim), ~0.15 ms on the headset path; ~144 k triangles a view (both eyes, three mirrors), 891 draws; renderer CPU within its noise (+0.08 +- 0.13 ms a field); VRAM the atlas with its 4x colour and depth: F-15C 1047 x 143 x 2 layers 5.7 MB (the largest model so far, Tornado 1508 x 212: 12.8 MB; the atlas keeps the largest size seen); the cubes are still baked (the A/B and `0`) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:910` |

| `ACZ_VR_CANOPY_CRACKS` | `1` | strength `0` (off), `0.5` subtle, `1`, `2` strong (max `3`) | Canopy cracks from the player's hits (the user's wish 2026-10-23; CRACKS_RESEARCH_2026-10-23.md; ps2_cracks.c, zero_cracks.c, cockpit.frag crk_glass): each hit on the player (the game's damage function, the damage it dealt as a share of the HP at the mission start; kind gun 0.8, missile / special 1, other 0.5; x this strength) cracks the 3D cockpit's canopy / windscreen where the source's direction from the eye meets the glass (the missile's burst point, else the attacker): radial arms (N ~ (E / 0.3)^1/4, length ~ E^1/2) that run straight near the hit and kink further out, drift fore-aft (hoop stress), branch at 25-40 deg, end at the frame and on earlier cracks; concentric chords; a crushed milky core and a halo of micro-cracks; a missile's burst adds a few small fragment impacts, a gun burst one or two impacts that grow with further rounds; a direction that misses the glass (E > 0.15) cracks it from the nearest frame edge. Materials per cockpit and mesh (stretched acrylic; F-16 acrylic-polycarbonate laminate: short stars, a white ring, a hole for big hits; F-22 polycarbonate: holes; F-14 / A-10 / MiG-21 glass-laminate windscreens: a dense spider web). Drawn as lines >= 1.3 px with their energy kept (steady without TAA), the same geometry in both eyes, silver against the sky / dark where they mirror the cockpit, a faint band through the glass's thickness, glints along them near the sun, x (1 - 0.6 wash) on wet glass; holes let the world through. The pattern is made on a worker thread (0.1-2.7 ms a hit; the renderer only copies it when it changes). For the mission: cleared with the canopy water at a mission start, RETRY or a jump (ACZ_IMM_RESET) and when the cockpit model changes. Nothing in the game changes | `runtime/src/ps2_cracks.c:104`, `runtime/src/ps2_cracks.c:117` |

| `ACZ_IMM_DEBRIS` | on (`sound,glass,rumble`) | `0`, `none`, or a list of `sound`, `glass`, `rumble` | Debris of destroyed units (wish #47; nothing in the game changes): from the kill point the game reports (0x1FC880; its explosion effects stay there) a cloud of bits widening from 4 to ~30 m over 3 s and fading by 6 s, ten chunks flung out on drag-and-gravity paths, and the wreck itself where the game moves it (unit +0x260 while its hit points stay <= 0), each for 8 s. Flying through them: `sound` -- chunks passing within 25 m (the wreck 40 m) whoosh past, positioned binaurally with Doppler (the spatial mixer's head model), a rattle and hiss inside a cloud of bits, ticks / clanks where bits meet the canopy; `glass` -- each canopy hit leaves a small soot fleck on the glass there (at most 3, fading and smearing aft in 2-5 s; F4 Immersion grime off = none); `rumble` -- a short buzz per passing chunk, a knock per canopy hit, a faint grain in the cloud. Rare by construction: straight through the cloud's middle ~0.6 expected canopy hits at 0.5 s, ~0.27 at 1 s, ~0.2 at 1.3 s (the bits' cloud is 4 -> 30 m wide); the smoke itself is ACZ_IMM_SMOKE_PASS. One summary line per kill followed: ACZ_IMM_DEBRIS_LOG. `0` = as before (passing within 90 m of the wreck in its first 1.5 s: a tick burst, a grime film and smear over the whole canopy, a buzz); `none` = no debris effects | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1923` |

| `ACZ_VR_SMOKE_VOLUME` | `1` | `0` = off, strength `0.1`..`2` (ENHANCEMENTS > PICTURE > SMOKE: ON 1 / SUBTLE 0.5 / OFF) | (pt_fx 2026-10-29, bug #214: playtest 11:17:14, M15 base under a bombing raid, "these explosions could be a bit better ... more volumetric") The game's explosion smoke (its blend-0x144 effect sprites; missile trails too) shaded as a volume from its own sprites, once per game frame on the renderer thread, only the vertex colours of the frame's copy (no new draw, texture or blend; no GPU cost): the sprites back in the world (bug #213's depth ratio 1.245), their mass (alpha x the visible puff cubed) in a 12 m grid, the fire sprites' light in a second channel; each smoke sprite's light share s = 55 % the sun (C1, above the horizon) + 45 % the sky above (all of it at night), exp(-1.2 tau) over 3 cells (36 m) toward each: colour x 0.58 .. 1.22 (core, far side, underside .. sunlit top); across the sprite a gradient (corners toward the light brighter, by s); the fire's light in its cell and the one below warms the smoke near it (r x1.7, g x1.3 at most): the glowing core of a fresh explosion, gone with the fire (~2.5 s). M3 test explosion (100-200 smoke sprites in view): light share min 0.14 .. mean 0.67-0.76; renderer CPU 35-86 us per game frame with 100-140 smoke sprites in view, once per game frame (frames drawn between reuse it; logged `vk: smoke volume:` every 600 game frames with smoke); GPU exclusive 4x A/B M3 with an explosion every 4 s: on 3.633 / 3.656, off 3.637 / 3.645 ms (+0.004, noise). | `ACZ_VR_FX_BILLBOARD` | `1` | `0` (ENHANCEMENTS > PICTURE > EXPLOSIONS: SOFT and SOFT WITH VOLUMES / GAME) | (fx_boom 2026-10-29, bug #231: playtest M17 / M18, F-15: 18:55:36 "wow, very strange explosions", 20:07:26 "make it not turn with movement, as it turns", 22:52:42 "make them 3D so they don't spin when I turn my head") The game's effect sprites -- explosions' smoke and fire, missile and wreck smoke, glows -- were cards upright in the image of the head-turned camera: rolling the head or the aircraft turned every puff with it, and turning the head away shrank and squashed them (a card parallel to the view plane 45 deg off the axis subtends cos^2 = 0.5 of its size across). Each card (the effect programs' puffs: four triangles fanned about their middle, the spin already in their corners; or a GS sprite pair; all corners at one depth) is rebuilt as the same card -- the same world size at the same place -- facing the eye and upright to the world's up instead of the camera's (corner = c + a R + b U at unit depth: U the world's up made perpendicular to the ray, mixed with north near the zenith / nadir; projected with the frame's own camera, also for the frames drawn between game frames, which reproject the moved corners). Cards over ~20 deg half-size (a puff around the eye) only turn upright, blended up to 30 deg. Both eyes alike; same draws, textures, colours, depths; target boxes (frag flag bit 31), trail strips and single triangles untouched; the smoke volume's lit side follows the moved corners. Sim (M3 F-15C, a kill explosion 600 m ahead, head rolled 30 deg): the puffs turned 28-31 deg back to the world's up, 70-170 cards a frame, 0 kept; renderer CPU ~10-40 us a frame with an explosion in view. `0` = the game's rectangles | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:22457` |

| `ACZ_VR_FX_BILLBOARD_BIG` | `1` | `0` (upright to the world as #231) | (fx_mpbm 2026-10-31, bug #254: M18 X-02, 10:44:30 "see, the explosion spins, it needs fixing" -- Morgan's burst: a pink halo and white bands the size of the view; the session's effect cards turned by 69 deg on average) With ACZ_VR_FX_BILLBOARD, a big card -- over ~20 deg half-size, the ones only turned upright in the image (a flash or a burst around the eye, a near puff in cloud, the MPBM's halo and bands) -- turns upright to the cockpit instead of the world: the aircraft camera's up (the game's camera without the head, ps2_xr_camera_base_of) made perpendicular to the card's ray, the cockpit's forward mixed in within ~37 deg of its up / down axis. The head's own roll and turn stay taken out (as #231), the aircraft's roll stays as the game drew it: upright to the world, such a card turned by the whole bank in the cockpit and swept round the canopy in a roll. | `ACZ_VR_FX_BURST` | `1` | `0` = the game's glow as before; strength `0.1`..`2` (ENHANCEMENTS > PICTURE > EXPLOSIONS: SOFT WITH VOLUMES / OFF) | (fx_burst 2026-11-02, bug #266: M18 X-02 trailer runs, "the huge explosion Pixy releases ... a very harsh gradient; it does not look volumetric, it still looks 2D; there is no beautiful explosion gradient") Pixy's MPBM burst (M18's second phase: his detonation 0x2216A4 makes the 0x180300 set with effect 102, seen with nothing else) becomes a volume marched in true stereo where the game's burst is (burst.vert / burst.frag after the world and its effects, with the trails): a turbulent shell whose lumps ride the blast outwards, white-hot in the middle cooling through yellow, orange and deep red into smoke (a piecewise-linear black-body ramp, no flat bands at its stops), soft edges, each eye's own ray, ended at the scene's depth and faded by our clouds' transmittance in front where the effects' cloud veil is bound (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FX_SOFT, multiview), else by the frame's depth test at the sphere's front; 22 jittered steps a pixel, dithered output. Faithful timing and size: the game draws the burst as one big quad of an 8 x 8 texel glow (atlas texels u 248..255, v 193..200) split into up to ~16 columns plus a copy into its glow target (blurred, reduced to 1/8 and laid back over the frame: the blocky lavender halo with the stair-stepped edge); each game frame the frame's columns give the radius (R = d sin(0.9 x the quad's half height about the burst's centre)) and the strength (the columns' alpha: 255 for ~3.2 s, then e^(-(t - 3.2) / 0.9 s)); while they do not show, fitted curves (R = 1120 m (1 - 0.55 e^(-t / 1.6 s)) scaled to the last measurement). The heat follows the game's alpha (white-hot while it is full, deep red as it fades); as it fades the volume dissipates (its thin parts first). Its point flash and hit flashes stay as before. GPU (exclusive 4x, ACZ_VR_FX_BURST_AB, frames with a burst): M18 +0.01..+0.25 ms, mean +0.15 per stereo frame; the eye inside a test burst +0.30..+0.49 (ACZ_VR_FX_BURST_RATE); nothing without a burst.
