| `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_WAKE` | `1` | `0` = off, strength `0.1`..`2` (menu: ON 1, SUBTLE 0.5, OFF) | The cloud wake (2026-10-29, agent cl_wake; docs/research/2026-10-29/cloud-wake.md; zero_trails.c, ps2_clouds_wake.c + cloud_wake.comp, the trails pass `trl_*`, trails.frag): leaving one of our volumetric clouds (needs ACZ_VR_CLOUDS near / all), every aircraft the game flies drags out a short torn veil of the cloud in its vortex wake (1.8 spans wide, widening 2.4 m/s with ACZ_VR_CLOUD_WAKE_LOOK 2; 1.4 / 1.4 before, at the edge about as opaque as ~15 m of real cloud, thinning over ACZ_VR_CLOUD_WAKE_DRAG metres past the cloud's edge) and, in the humid shell around the cloud or in a pull, its two vortex cores condense into thin threads (1 g: 40-120 m; >= 2 g: up to ~600 m, 400 with ACZ_VR_CLOUD_WAKE_LOOK=1). Both sink with the wake (1.8 m/s x the load factor, against the lift, until the vortex pair links after ~4 t0: ~25-30 m), widen, the threads meander (Crow), and everything breaks up and evaporates in the time a 12 um droplet needs in the mixed air (0.85 s / (1 - RH): 7-10 s in mission 3). Inside / outside from a GPU probe of our clouds' own density at each aircraft (up to 32 within ACZ_VR_CLOUD_WAKE_RANGE, ~3 game frames late, asked 0.35 s ahead and held 0.35 s over the clear pockets inside a cloud). World strips in true stereo through the contrail pass (lit with the game's cloud palette and our cloud shadow map, behind terrain, aircraft and our clouds' stamped depth). A head-locked veil over the eye images (a neutral grey closing in: clear to 50 deg at the onset, 34 deg at the full level, edge alpha at most 0.62 -- never black), only in the cockpit / HUD view; G from the flight model (airspeed x pitch rate + gravity). `strength` scales the level (0.5 = milder) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:21258`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:34845`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3450` |
| `ACZ_IMM_GSUIT` | `0` (ENHANCEMENTS > COMFORT > PILOT SOUNDS) | level `0`..`3` | G-suit (wish #77): the anti-G valve fills the bladders from ~2 G at 1.5 psi per G. A G-suit is felt (a squeeze), not heard (no pilot account describes a sound), so (hot-mic model, the default) no hiss: one low "whump" when the valve starts filling after a rest (a 90 Hz resonance, the bladders on the body, with a 0.2 s muffled air rush below 600 Hz; peak ~-37 dBFS at a 9-12 G onset) and a soft short "pff" when a filled suit vents, from below (legs and abdomen; binaural with ACZ_AUDIO_HRTF). With ACZ_IMM_PILOT_MODEL=hiss: the first version (an inflation hiss through 1.5 / 3.8 kHz while filling, a venting "pssht" at 2.8 / 6 kHz). Synthesised | `runtime/src/ps2_gforce_audio.c:235` |
| `ACZ_IMM_BREATH` | `0` (ENHANCEMENTS > COMFORT > PILOT SOUNDS) | `level[,calm]`, level `0`..`3` | Breathing in the oxygen mask (wish #82) as the pilot hears it: through the mask microphone on the intercom ("hot mic"; high-pass 180 Hz, +2 dB at 1.5 kHz, 4th-order low-pass 3.4 kHz: nothing above ~4 kHz) -- the inhale the demand regulator's flow through the mask cavity (500 / 1450-1700 / 2650 Hz) ending in the valve's click, the exhale a darker puff through the exhalation valve with its flutter; centred, never muffled. Heard only under effort (stress: missile alert, lock, hits, firing, STALL / PULL UP; grey-out; G-work in the last ~20 s) unless `,calm`: then also at rest, 6 dB lower. 12 breaths a minute, up to 26 with effort; -10 dB under the radio, up to -6 dB under a loud game (ACZ_IMM_DUCK). With ACZ_IMM_PILOT_MODEL=hiss: the first version (a hiss through 0.9 / 2.2 / 5.2 kHz, always heard, default 1). Cockpit and HUD views only. Synthesised | `runtime/src/ps2_gforce_audio.c:231` |
| `ACZ_IMM_STRAIN` | `1` | level `0`..`3` | The anti-G straining manoeuvre (wish #77) on hot mic, as the Navy's "hook": for sustained G (above 5.5 G for 1 s or 7 G for 0.3 s, so the ~6 G spike of a lift-off pull does not start it; it ends below 4 G) a quick gasp, a short voiced grunt cut by the closing glottis ("hoo-"), the breath held 2.5-3.2 s, the forceful release ("k-hah") and the next gasp; a long relieved exhale when the G comes off. Through the same mic chain as the breath (0.18-3.4 kHz). `0` = ordinary (faster) breathing under G | `runtime/src/ps2_gforce_audio.c:234` |
| `ACZ_IMM_BUFFET` | `1` (`sound,rumble,needles`) | `strength[,list]`, list of `sound`, `rumble`, `needles`; `0` off | Buffet (wish #81), from the lift the pull needs against the wing's maximum (n (Vs / V)^2, Vs the aircraft's stall onset speed FM+0x1C at this altitude: from 55 %), the game's stall factor and the air brake at speed: the airframe's shudder and separated airflow (outside the canopy: muffled in the cockpit view), shudder pulses on the controller under the game's own rumble, the needles' jitter (accelerometer, AoA, VSI, airspeed, altimeter; never the camera or the cockpit) | `runtime/src/ps2_gforce_audio.c:236`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:12249` |
| `ACZ_IMM_PILOT_MODEL` | `natural` | `natural`, `hotmic`, `hiss` | The pilot's sounds (ps2_gforce_audio.c): `natural` (bug #138 "robotic", 2026-10-27) = the hot-mic model with natural breathing: each breath planned from a physiological process (period and volume as AR(1) series: period CV ~13 % at rest, bigger breaths last longer; 12 -> 28 a minute and x1.9 volume with effort; a sigh every 20-50 calm breaths), the airflow shaped per sample (the inhale a skewed half-sine, the exhale the lungs' passive recoil) and the sound made from it (turbulence ~ flow^0.9, brighter with flow, a smooth random intermittency), broad airway / mask resonances re-drawn per breath and gliding within it, the demand valve opening and shutting at its cracking flow (a click whose time, level and pitch follow the breath, sometimes bouncing), the exhalation valve lifting and flapping irregularly at low flow, the breath stream on the microphone, a little voicing in effortful exhales; the straining voices with a Rosenberg glottis (jitter, shimmer). Same hot-mic band (0.18-3.4 kHz); calm breathing ~-45 dBFS rms (hotmic -53), under effort ~-29 (-41). `hotmic` = the 2026-10-23 rework (fixed band-pass noise under a fixed envelope); `hiss` = the first versions of 2026-10-23 (breath and G-suit on by default) for A/B | `runtime/src/ps2_gforce_audio.c:252` |
| `ACZ_IMM_DUCK` | `-24,0.7,10` | `from_dbfs,slope,max_db`; `0` off | The immersion bus: our added layers give way to the game's own mix (engine, effects, music, radio; its RMS each audio chunk, attack 50 ms, release 0.6 s, measured before our layers are added): the continuous beds -- the wheels' roll and the gear / flaps / air brake airflow (cockpit system sounds), the cloud airflow (weather sound), half of it on the buffet's air -- by `slope` dB per dB above `from_dbfs`, at most `max_db`, and 4 dB more under radio speech; the pilot voices (breath, strain, G-suit) by 0.4 dB per dB above `from_dbfs` + 4, at most 6 dB. HDR-style mixing: what the loud game would mask anyway is taken out instead of smearing it (M2 take-off roll at full power, game -9 dBFS: beds -10 dB, pilot -4.5 dB; cruise -15: beds -6.5). `0` = as before (only the radio ducking) | `runtime/src/ps2_gforce_audio.c:117` |

In a windy stage C1 changes a cloud every 0.3 s (M8 Merlon, wind 133 m/s: the 3-6 km clouds drift over the game's fixed coverage map, a cloud around the eye 0 -> 1 or 1 -> 0 in 2-3 s, as the game's own sprite alpha); the restarted ramp then lagged behind (at C1's updates median 0.26, p90 0.53; each cloud's worst lag median 0.69, max 0.82) and did most of the change only after the game's had ended, as one 2 s S-curve (median 0.52 of opacity after the game stopped; the cloud you fly in "reloading" a few seconds late). Follower: lag at the updates median 0.065 (p90 0.12), worst per cloud 0.26, after-stop change 0.16; peak rate median 0.40 / s (ramp 0.51, game 0.53). | `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_SPLAT_MIX` | `1` | `0`/`1` | Weather splat (bug #40, 2026-10-22): a cloud's coverage c = profile x min(1, density-from-opacity / 0.05) (vanishes as it fades out); the texel's coverage = max c, its density = the 4-norm of the clouds' expected densities c x density over that coverage (one cloud: as before; a dense cloud inside a faint one keeps its density); a cloud's column counts as far as its strength relative to the strongest at the texel, u = smoothstep(0.25, 1, s / s_max) (base raised / top lowered by (1 - u) band thicknesses). `0` = before: the widest profile's cloud whatever its opacity gave the density, and every covering cloud's column joined the union down to a point at its rim -- a faint or fading wide cloud blanked a dense one and stretched it to its own top or base along its disc edge (straight walls, flat lids: "a very rectangular cloud", "a loaf of bread"). M1 F-5E inside the overcast: -0.018 / -0.012 ms per stereo frame (2 runs; our cloud passes -0.029 / -0.019); M3 cumulus +0.008 / +0.016 (no gain): off by default. Both eyes alike. No reuse with flash lights, the axis blend or the coarse periphery. Engages only while the game frames' near pass costs >= ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_REUSE_MIN_MS; then all cloud passes of an in-between frame use its game frame's C1 (colours, fog, drift). Exclusive 4x in-run A/B (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_REUSE_AB=60, governor off, user settings, our clouds), frame GPU per stereo frame on - off: M1 overcast in cloud (3 runs) in-between -0.30..-0.32 ms (near pass 0.68 -> 0.34 ms), game frames +0.02..+0.05 (the history output ~0.03, plus weather / far work the C1 pinning moves from in-between to game frames), so about -0.13 ms a frame there; M5 in cloud (2 runs) in-between -0.13..-0.30 (near pass 0.37-0.70 -> 0.22-0.37), game +0.04..+0.07; M3 cumulus (near pass 0.14-0.25 ms: not engaged) and M7 (near pass idle) 0 +- 0.01. | `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FX_SOFT` | `0.25` | `0` = the binary stamp as before; half-width `0.05`..`1` (multiview) | (fx_mpbm 2026-10-31, bug #254: M18 X-02 in the snow cloud, 10:41:17 "the explosion isn't 3D", 10:44:30 "the explosion spins" -- Morgan's burst's pink halo and light bands cut along a coarse staircase of rounded steps 1.5..4 deg wide) The game's effects fade by our cloud's transmittance in front of them instead of being cut by the binary depth stamp where it crosses ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FX_T: the stamp's in-or-out edge followed the transmittance's own blocky field (the near volume's 39 m weather texels, the far map's tiles) -- a staircase across a big burst behind the cloud. Now the effects' depth snapshot (ACZ_VR_FX_SOFT's set-1 image, an RGBA16F image here) is the cloud's veil after a cloud point: the scene's view depth (m) and our cloud's transmittance ramp in front (the depth where it starts, where it has reached its total, the total; near volume x far map as the stamp's modes, the near volume taken as uniform over its range, the far map from its opacity profile bilinear over the texels with cloud; cloud_comp.frag -DVEIL); gs.frag scales an effect's alpha (blends by the source alpha) or colour (additive Cs x FIX + Cd) by smoothstep over the transmittance in front of its own depth between ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FX_T - w and + w (0.25 .. 0.75 by default): in front of the cloud and in thin haze as before, gone deep behind dense cloud as before, smooth between -- the edge follows the cloud's own soft density. The stamp stays only below ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FX_T - w (for blends that cannot fade), where the faded effects are gone already. Covers the draws the stamp met (also those reading a target, untextured lines; not the cloud sprites, target boxes, DATE). Sim M3 (effects 800 m ahead in a cloud's edge, the near composite before them): the smoke's straight cut at the stamp's texel edge becomes a smooth fade (a private script); in clear air and in front of the cloud the image is identical (exact A/B, 0 px over 8 levels in 30 M18 frames). `0` = the binary stamp; PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=cloudsoft = exact same-frame image A/B; GPU: ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FX_SOFT_AB | `runtime/src/ps2_clouds.c:462` |

As a loop, its four taps sat in a local array indexed by the loop counter and the chosen tap was read back by its run-time index (s[bi]): that array lived in local memory for every pixel of both eyes. The near composite's own GPU time (ACZ_DEBUG_CLOUDS timestamps, exclusive 4x, user settings) 0.43-0.45 -> 0.07-0.11 ms per stereo frame while the near volume draws (M3, M4 40 m, M12; M5 0.39 -> 0.11); whole frame M4 MiG-29A 40 m -0.282 +- 0.020 ms per stereo frame (3 lines of ~2,400 frames; frames over 4.7 ms 265 -> 19, latency -0.28), M5 J35J in cloud -0.311 +- 0.023 (3 lines) (in-run A/B `PS2_PASS_AB=60 PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=compunroll`). Same-frame image A/B (`PS2_PERF_AB=1 PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=compunroll`) at the re-draw noise floor of our clouds (the re-draw re-marches them with the C1 of its own record time: `PS2_PERF_AB=2` shows the same spread, M3 up to 60 levels on ~0.004 % of the pixels). `0` = the loop as before | `runtime/src/ps2_clouds.c:1028` |

| `ACZ_VR_CLOUD_REDRAW_KEEP` | `1` | `0` | (perf6_clouds 2026-11-03) A re-draw of a dumped frame in its own command buffer (PS2_PERF_AB: the frame drawn again as configured and with a switch flipped) keeps the frame's cloud inputs: its C1 copy (S1 publishes a newer one with the drift and the list's eased opacities while the frame records) and the maps its early update made (no second weather splat, shadow slice or far-map march). Both re-draws then run our passes on the same inputs and a flip's difference is the flip's alone. Before, every re-draw re-read C1 and updated the maps: the PS2_PERF_AB=2 control differed by 1-3 levels over whole cloud banks (M1 in the overcast: 20-330 k of 0.9 M px). With the time-driven effects off too (`ACZ_VR_CANOPY=0 ACZ_VR_FLASH_LIGHT=0 ACZ_IMMERSE=0 ACZ_VR_CONTRAILS=0 ACZ_VR_VAPOUR=0 ACZ_VR_HEAT_HAZE=0 ACZ_VR_EYE_ADAPT=0`) game frames are byte-identical in such flips; in-between frames keep a floor of a few hundred pixels, and a dump outside the eye's visible box (the `pass-mask:` line) holds stale content there. `0` = as before. A rung changes only the near volume (4-600 m: the in-cloud veil and its lumps, near cloud edges); the far map beyond 600 m, its composite, the shadow map, the optics and the wake stay as they are. Levers: `div` = the near buffer's texel covers div x div target pixels (HIGH 2: half resolution per axis), `step` / `grow` / `tier` = ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_STEP / _GROW / _TIER, `detail` = ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_DETAIL. `medium` = div 3, `low` = div 4 (not step 12.5: near march -71 % instead of -58 %, but the two plane families' seam and the plane contours show in thin cloud against the sky, up to ~15 levels). An explicitly set ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_* switch wins on every rung. The near images are made for the smallest divisor in use (at 4x 18.3 MB -> 8.1 / 4.6 MB); the in-between near reuse and the coarse periphery run at divisor 2 only. Each is a cheap angular lobe in our cloud composite, drawn only where the physics allows it: the halo by the pixel's own optical depth through our clouds (thin cloud, not clear sky or thick cloud), above the freezing level (anywhere in the snow missions), with the sun visible through the clouds; glory and spectre on the opaque, sunlit cloud surface a one-workgroup probe finds along the antisolar ray below the aircraft (near volume or far map); the rainbow by the rain's optical depth along the ray (the stage's precipitation volume up to the scene distance) and the cloud shadow map's sunlight on that rain. Directions at infinity (halo, rainbow) are the same in both eyes; glory and spectre sit on the cloud surface per eye. Day only, none inside cloud, every condition fades over ~1 s, no screen-space history. Needs our far clouds (`ACZ_VR_CLOUDS=all` or `far`). The game moves its cloud field with the stage's wind at full speed: M8 "Merlon" and M9 800 units/s = 120 m/s, 430 km/h (6.65 units/m: the eye moved 1109 units/s at 324 kt in the M8 sim run; 72 % of that airspeed), M1, M2, M5, M10, M11, M15, M16 500 units/s (75 m/s), M14 400 (config_zero/cloud_missions.json lists them at 6 units/m), while the aircraft flies over the ground and never drifts with that air -- relative to the aircraft a whole cloud deck slides sideways at the wind speed (M8, 20:59:13 "why do I feel I am being carried away somewhere, or are the clouds like that?"). The 2006 game culled its sprites within 680 m and faded them in to 2 km; our clouds pass right by the canopy, which turns the slide into strong false sideways motion. `1` = the game's drift (default, faithful); `0` = clouds fixed over the ground (as the air would be for a pilot drifting with it); between = a slower drift. Only our clouds (`ACZ_VR_CLOUDS`); the game's own sprites keep the game's drift. No GPU cost | `runtime/src/zero_clouds.c:557`, `runtime/src/zero_clouds.c:575` |

The band only bounds our renderer's density (S2 / S3 / the shadow map) | `runtime/src/zero_clouds.c:553` |

M2's take-off set is a stratus deck of 10-12 km sprites centred 1530-2420 m up whose bodies (vertical semi-axis 0.262 x sprite) put the band's base at -248 m: the runway (eye 311 m) sat inside our clouds (C1 in-cloud 0.35, our body 0.40, ~3.8 km visibility: haze over the runway and the mountains, the glass wet). The game itself draws no cloud within 4096 units (683 m) of its camera. With 680: base 991 m (the set's own weather band starts at 853 m), in-cloud 0, the mountains as clear as with our clouds off (mountain box high-pass 1.30 -> 3.09, the game alone 2.0-3.6), the deck above unchanged; GPU on the runway (exclusive 4x, 2 runs each, 5 s windows) 4.15-4.28 -> 3.43-3.55 ms per stereo frame (the near field idle). Flight-start stages never touch it | `runtime/src/zero_clouds.c:555` |

## Renderer, upscaling and performance

### Resolution, presentation and timing

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PS2_UPSCALE` | F4 setting (`play_zero_vr.sh`: `3`; its sharp mode: ENHANCEMENTS RESOLUTION, `4`) | `0` = Auto, `1`..`8` | Internal resolution multiplier (capped by the GPU's image size limit); overrides the setting | `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:521`, `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:673`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:34595` (+1 more) |
| `PS2_FPS_CAP` | F4 setting | fps; `<=0` = no limit | Presentation frame-rate cap, overrides the setting | `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:469`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:34556` |
| `PS2_PRESENT_MODE` | F4 setting (mailbox) | `fifo`, `mailbox`, `immediate`, `relaxed` | Vulkan present mode of the window | `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:453`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:4216` |
| `PS2_ASPECT` | F4 setting | float, e.g. `1.3333` | Force the displayed aspect ratio | `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:409`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:30580` |
| `PS2_FIELD_HZ` | NTSC 59.94 Hz (`play_zero_vr.sh`: `60` in VR) | Hz (1..10000) | Vblank field clock; 60 makes 60 fps line up with a 120 Hz headset (game runs 0.1% fast) | `runtime/src/ps2_kernel.c:1237`, `runtime/src/ps2_recexact.c:96`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32184` (+1 more) |
| `PS2_LOCKSTEP` | off (gsreplay: on) | non-0 / `0` | Renderer waits for every field and never drops one (deterministic output) | `runtime/src/ps2_replay.c:533`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:35324` |
| `PS2_ASYNC_GFX` | on | `0` | Graphics machine (VIF, VU1, GIF, GS) on its own thread; `0` keeps it on the EE thread. While a DMA-level GS capture (capture.on / F6, level dma) is wanted or recording, the EE drains this queue at the end of every field and writes the capture's field records, start and stop itself (bug #111: they used to come from the worker, up to two fields behind, so such captures lost their first field's records and were torn by two unsynchronised writers); GS-level captures stay with the worker; nothing changes without a capture | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:1609` |
| `PS2_ASYNC_MB` | `64` | MB, `1`..`1024` | Size of the async graphics ring buffer | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:1626` |
| `PS2_ASYNC_FIELDS` | `2` | `1`..`8` | How many fields the async graphics queue may run ahead | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:1628` |
| `PS2_GFX_SPLIT` | on (since 2026-10-25, ov_cpu) | `0` | The graphics worker in two stages (op_threads 2026-10-23): this thread runs the ring and the VIF1 command stream (codes, UNPACK) and forwards everything else in stream order through a second ring to a second thread, which runs the VU1 programs (native decoders), the GIF, the GS and the field's present at the same point of the stream (UNPACK writes as VU1 memory records before each MSCAL/MSCNT, MPG and DIRECT data, every other record verbatim; syncs at a VIF1 interrupt, a mode change and the EE's drain). Same work, same order, same results (replays of one capture: identical signatures and images; PS2_GFX_SPLIT_VERIFY). Sim in-run A/B: the critical stage's busy time per field -16 % (M3 heavy) / -24 % (M4), -27 % on top of op_stream's decode pool (M3 heavy 5.80 -> 4.21 ms), +0.8 ms CPU in all, +0.03-0.05 ms from the field's end to its publication, EE +0.4-1.3 ms; with the worker pinned to E-cores a ~3.6 ms publication tail on a quarter of the fields (THREADS_RESEARCH_2026-10-23.md). On by default since ov_cpu's in-run A/B in the user's settings (2026-10-25): M4 critical stage 5.16 -> 3.91 ms a field (-1.24 +- 0.08, p99 -1.13), M9 -0.99 +- 0.34, EE +0.76 ms, recording +0.04 ms, frame latency n.s., publication +0.02 ms (p99 +0.06); `0` = one worker (the headset's `speed: graphics stage 2` lines show it) | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:1641` |
| `PS2_GFX_SPLIT_AB` | off | seconds | [diagnostic] In-run A/B of PS2_GFX_SPLIT: two stages in odd blocks of that many seconds, one worker in the even ones (switched at a field after stage 2 has finished; PS2_THREAD_PROF event 15 marks the blocks) | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:1641` |
| `PS2_GFX_SPLIT_VERIFY` | off | `1` | [test] With PS2_GFX_SPLIT: every VU1 memory record carries a hash of the lanes stage 1 wrote; stage 2 re-reads them after applying and logs differences (`gfx-split verify:`) | `runtime/src/ps2_vu.c:1873` |
| `PS2_PIPELINE_CACHE` | `<exe dir>/ac5_pipelines` | path prefix; `0` = off | Vulkan pipeline cache (`.keys` / `.cache`), so later runs build pipelines at start-up instead of stuttering | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:3762` |
| `PS2_PIPE_WARM_THREADS` | `4` | `0`..`8` | Worker threads that build the remembered pipelines at start-up (`0` = renderer thread in 4 ms slices) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:4000` |
| `PS2_PIPE_CACHE_COMPACT` | on | `0` | `ac5_pipelines.cache` gets our own header (shader-set stamp from the `.spv` names / sizes / times, blob size and FNV-1a 64) and starts EMPTY when the shaders changed, the file has no header (older builds), fails its size / hash check or exceeds `PS2_PIPE_CACHE_MAX_MB`; the start-up warm-up rebuilds it from the remembered keys (Vulkan cannot drop cache entries; the driver blob kept every old shader version: 31.3 MB for 246 states -> 4.7 MB). Also keeps remembered keys of features that are off this session (foveated bit 52, dynamic blend bit 53) in `.keys`. `0` = the old behaviour (plain blob, any valid one loaded) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:3823` |
| `PS2_PIPE_CACHE_MAX_MB` | `32` | MB; `0` = no limit | With `PS2_PIPE_CACHE_COMPACT`: a cache blob larger than this starts empty (rebuilt by the warm-up); the file reader refuses 64 MB | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:3907` |
| `PS2_PERF_PIPE_SAVE_ASYNC` | on | `0` | The periodic pipeline-cache save during play (20 s after new pipeline states appeared) on a short-lived background thread (the renderer copies the key list only): vkGetPipelineCacheData, the hash and the two file writes held the renderer thread 8.5-16.9 ms between two attempts (0.05 ms now); the same files and bytes; the save at exit stays synchronous (ov_cpu 2026-10-25) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:4125` |
| `PS2_VB_DEVICE` | off | `1` | Vertex buffers in device-local, host-visible (ReBAR) memory instead of system RAM | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:6186` |

### GPU features and GS emulation

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PS2_GS_MIPS` | off | `1` | Mip chains for GS textures: less shimmer, but costs spikes because Zero re-uploads atlases while the head turns | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:39889` |
| `PS2_VRS` | on if the GPU supports it | `0` | Coarse (variable-rate) shading for the cloud sprites | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:5626` |
| `PS2_VRS_RATE` | `2` | `2` or `4` | Coarse shading block: 2x2 or 4x4 pixels | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:3698` |
| `PS2_VRS_FOV` | `crop` (VR stereo, if the GPU has attachment shading rates) | `0`/`off`, `crop`/`1`, `zones`/`2` | Foveated shading through a VK_KHR_fragment_shading_rate attachment per eye layer (op_vrs 2026-10-23). Each eye layer is the whole symmetric image but the headset shows only that eye's crop (Quest 3 and the simulator: L x 0..1649, R x 399..2048, both y ~280..1792 of 2048x1792: 32 % of every layer is drawn and never shown). `crop`: that part is shaded once per 4x4 pixels (world, HUD, edge AA, the cockpit's MSAA pass); the crop itself is shaded exactly as before. `zones`: also the world draws (class 1) in rings around each eye's optical axis (PS2_VRS_FOV_ZONES); HUD, text, target boxes, the cockpit model, its screens and the edge AA stay 1x1. `0` = the old pipelines, no attachment. Every pipeline of ps2_video.c gets the attachment flag when it is on (the first start after a change recompiles the pipeline cache) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:5168` |
| `PS2_VRS_FOV_ZONES` | `30,40,50` | `r1,r2,r3` degrees | `zones`: 1x1 within r1 of the eye's axis, 2x1 left/right and 1x2 above/below (coarse along the radius) to r2, 2x2 to r3, 4x4 beyond (by attachment texel: 16x16 px on the RTX 3090 Ti, the texel's nearest point decides) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:24195` |
| `PS2_VRS_FOV_CENTER` | `0,0` | `x,y` degrees | `zones`: the rings' centre moved off each eye's axis (x toward the nose, y up), for the headset check of where the lens is sharp | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:24199` |
| `PS2_VRS_FOV_DUMP_CROP` | off | `1` | Eye dumps (PS2_XR_DUMP_EYES) get the crop's 4x4 outside the eye crop too (default: dump frames keep the whole image 1x1 outside the zones, as our clouds do, so harness images stay complete) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:24215` |
| `PS2_PASS_MASK` | on (`cpit,eaa,cloud,copy`; `cloud` acts only with a lens mask) | `0`, `1`, or a list of `cpit`, `eaa`, `cloud`, `copy` | Pixels no eye sees kept out of our own passes (ov_passes 2026-10-25; never the game's): each eye's visible area = its crop of the symmetric layer (or the runtime's visible-area mesh, XR_KHR_visibility_mask) grown by PS2_PASS_MASK_MARGIN, the rest hidden. `cpit`: the cockpit's 4x MSAA pass renders only the union of the eyes' visible boxes (the top band of ~13 % of the layer is not cleared, drawn or resolved) and its depth is pre-filled with the nearest value over each eye's hidden area (pmask.vert, per view): the model's depth pre-pass and colour pass fail there by early-Z; the composite covers the visible mesh. `eaa`: edge AA covers each eye's visible mesh. `copy`: the eye copies to the headset take only the eye's crop (the imageRect the layer submits) and a margin of w / 64 px instead of the whole layer. `cloud` (only with a lens mask: with the crop alone the composites' own crop test discards as cheaply, +0.012 ms measured): the cloud composites cover the visible mesh and the near march skips the lens' hidden 16x16 px blocks. Outside the visible area the layer keeps the frame without cockpit / edge AA: never shown by the headset; the desktop window (which shows the right eye's whole layer) is cleared to black there (PS2_PASS_MASK_WINDOW), so it shows exactly what that eye sees. Sim (exclusive 4x, user settings, governor off, in-run A/B in 60-frame blocks): M3 J35J -0.035 / -0.043 ms per stereo frame (2 runs; blocks -0.021..-0.064; game and in-between frames alike), M4 F-1 -0.005 (inside its +-0.05 block noise). The crop the headset shows: the same-frame re-draw with the masks off differs by <= 7 levels on ~1 % of it, only in our cloud bank, as with an unrelated flip (re-draw noise). The lens path was exercised with PS2_PASS_MASK_TEST_LENS (corners masked, the rest as with the crop alone); the runtime's real mesh is untested (no headset). Eye dumps keep the whole image unless PS2_PASS_MASK_DUMP=1; exact image A/B PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=pmask, GPU A/B PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=mask | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:18384` |
| `PS2_PASS_MASK_WINDOW` | on | `0` | PS2_PASS_MASK: the desktop window shows the right eye's whole symmetric layer; where the masks left out the cockpit and edge AA (outside that eye's visible box: at 1920x1009 a strip x 0..343 and the band y 0..135, ~29 % of the window) it is cleared to black, so the window shows what the eye sees instead of a cockpit cut off at the crop. The recordings' scenes (OBS game capture, every collection) crop the window to the right eye at l374 t163, inside the box (31 / 28 px to spare): unchanged; the top-left FPS inset (200x44) shows the counter on black. Cost ~0 (a clear of the window's strips). `0` = the masked frame as it is (no cockpit / edge AA there) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:18395` |
| `PS2_PASS_MASK_MARGIN` | `32` | pixels at 4x (scaled with PS2_UPSCALE) | PS2_PASS_MASK: how far the visible area reaches past each eye's crop / lens mesh (edge-AA taps reach 17 px; the compositor's filter and Link's sharpening read a few pixels past the crop) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:18389` |
| `PS2_PASS_MASK_DUMP` | off | `1` | [test] PS2_PASS_MASK also in eye-dump frames (the dump then shows the masked layer; with PS2_PERF_AB=1 PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=pmask the `_nc` re-draw is the unmasked same frame) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:18391` |
| `PS2_PASS_MASK_TEST_LENS` | off | fraction `0.05`..`0.9` | [test] Without a runtime mask: cut each eye's crop corners as a lens would (triangles with legs = this fraction of the half width / height), to exercise the lens path (cockpit pre-fill, visible meshes, the near march's hidden blocks) on the simulator | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:18392` |
| `PS2_PASS_MASK_LOG` | off (6 lines) | `1` | [test] `pass-mask:` lines (each eye's visible box, hidden share, union, vertices; the near march's hidden blocks) whenever the masks change | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:18394` |
| `PS2_DATE_LOCAL` | on if supported | `0` | Destination alpha test reads the target in place (dynamic rendering local read); `0` goes back to copies. Native meshes that also write alpha only when flat (PS2_DATE_LOCAL_QUAD) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:5674` |
| `PS2_DATE_SNAP_EVERY` | off | `1` | One target snapshot per DATE draw instead of one shared by a run of them (old behaviour) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:6997` |
| `PS2_DATE_LOCAL_QUAD` | on | `0` | (ov_date 2026-10-25) Native-mesh draws with the destination alpha test (DATE) that also write alpha read the target in place too (PS2_DATE_LOCAL) when they are flat: up to 16 triangles in one plane that do not overlap there (an edge of one separates each pair; shared edges by equal positions), the eye well off the plane (more than its stereo shift and, in in-between frames, its motion since the game frame), no sliver on screen (corners >= 1/8 PS2 px off the opposite sides); in-between frames of unmatched meshes (screen-space path) keep the copy. Such a draw covers no pixel twice, so no fragment can read alpha the same draw wrote, and the in-place read gives exactly the pre-draw alpha the snapshot copy gave. Before, each one stopped the rendering for a snapshot copy of its box (two barriers, a copy, a restart): M4 over the sea draws its sea tiles in 5 passes, the 4th (tex 4, Cd - Cs*FIX, DATE "bit 7 clear", z + alpha written) one quad per tile, 40-60 a stereo frame. M4 sea capture (flat replay, per frame): snapshots 51 -> 10, begin-rendering 93 -> 53, barrier calls 215 -> 135, 0.72 M PS2 px of copies -> 0; 41 of 44 such draws read in place (the rest: 2 x 2 tiles not in one plane, kept). GPU (exclusive 4x, the user's settings, our clouds, governor off, in-run A/B PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=date in 60-frame blocks, paired blocks, 3 runs): M4 MiG-29A 40 m over the coast / port -0.32 / -0.34 / -0.34 (+-0.055) ms per stereo frame, over the sea -0.73 / -0.74 / -0.75 (+-0.035) (game and in-between frames alike; 5.12 -> 4.38 ms); M3 has no such draws (0 a frame: no change). Exact: gsreplay of a DMA capture over the M4 sea byte-identical on / off at fields 100, 200, 300 (and M3 captures); same-frame A/B (PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=datequad) at the re-draw noise floor of PS2_PERF_AB=2. `0` = the copies as before; counters in the vk-count line (PS2_SPEED_LOG: date-alpha-meshes, why kept) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:17223` |
| `PS2_DATE_BATCH` | `3` (since perf4_passes 2026-11-03; nx_date: `2`) | `0`, `1`, `2` | (nx_date 2026-10-25) DATE local-read feedback barriers batched. A DATE draw that reads the target in place (PS2_DATE_LOCAL) takes a feedback barrier whenever alpha was written into the target since the last one; the game draws its sea and terrain tile by tile in 3-5 passes (pass 1 writes alpha (FBA), a later DATE pass reads it), so every tile paid one (flat replays of DMA captures: 55.7 a frame over the M4 sea, 235 at the M14 sea peak, 734 in the M2 intro). `2`: consecutive native-mesh tiles (draws with bit-equal triangles = one tile's passes) all in one plane are re-issued pass-major (the 1st draw of every tile, then the 2nd, ...) when no pixel can belong to two tiles in any eye or in-between view: triangles of different tiles pairwise separated in the plane (an edge separates each pair; contacts only at equal positions; diagonal neighbours touching at one vertex by their angular wedges, in the plane and >= 0.05 rad on screen), the same view (block matrix, offsets, eye shift, in-between matrix), date_quad_ok's guards (eye off the plane by > 2 x (stereo shift + in-between camera motion) and 1e-3 of the distance to every tile, no sliver: corners >= 1/8 PS2 px off), never across one of our own passes (clouds, cockpit, edge AA, trails, depth snapshot), no cockpit / HUD-block / MFD / house / self-reading / colour-clip / overlay draws, not in the synchronous second stereo pass. Each pixel then still sees its own tile's draws in their order, and a DATE draw of a run takes a barrier only when its own tile wrote alpha since the last one: one per pass group instead of one per tile; the grouping also halves the pipeline binds. `1` (test only): instead a per-target box of the alpha writes since the last barrier (game frames), a DATE draw whose box misses it skipping the barrier: 0-3 a frame for +0.45-0.65 ms of recording in the M2 intro, not part of `2`. The in-between frames and the A/B re-draws reuse the game frame's runs. `3`: also heightfield runs (tiles not in one plane, e.g. M2's 2 x 2 terrain cells: their triangles cover a convex domain once, every eye on the same side of every triangle) -- in the M2 intro feedback barriers 733 -> 590 but GPU -0.08 ms for latency +0.3 ms (builder), so not by default. Replays (flat, 4x): feedback barriers M4 55.7 -> 23.7, M14 234.7 -> 41.5, M2 intro unchanged (`3`: 734 -> 588); barrier calls 137 -> 105 / 325 -> 133; pipeline binds M4 253 -> 158, M14 679 -> 324; byte-identical images at fields 60-300 of all three captures in modes 0 / 1 / 2 / 3. GPU per stereo frame (exclusive 4x, the user's settings + our clouds, governor off, in-run A/B PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=datebatch in 60-frame blocks, paired, no class timers; final build): M4 MiG-29A at 40 m over the coast / port -0.158 +- 0.031 ms, over the sea -0.279 +- 0.032 (earlier builds with the box / heightfield runs: -0.16 / -0.28 twice), latency -0.15 / -0.28; M14 A-10A low over the sea -0.22 +- 0.04 before the peak and -0.60 +- 0.14 at the sea peak (fields 1900-2200, 2 runs pooled), latency -0.14 / -0.37, unchanged after it; M2 with the intro: no change (intro -0.018 +- 0.005, flight +-0.01; latency n.s.). Same-frame A/B (PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=datebatch, our clouds / immersion off): game and in-between frames 0 px over the M4 sea and the M14 sea peak (control PS2_PERF_AB=2: in-between frames 0.002-0.4 % at the re-draw floor). The M4 gain is above nx_census' ceiling for leaving out all feedback barriers (-0.09..-0.10): most of it is the halved pipeline / state changes of the pass-major order. Builder on the renderer thread 0.04 ms per game-frame pass in M4 and the M2 intro, 0.35 ms at the M14 sea peak (replay recording +0.15 there; in game the latency still drops). `0` = one barrier per tile in the game's draw order. Counters: the vk-date line (feedback barriers) and the vk-count date-batch line (PS2_SPEED_LOG: runs, why runs ended, builder time, box skips); PS2_DATE_BATCH_TRACE=N: frame N's runs (dbt-run lines) **Default `3` since perf4_passes (2026-11-03)**: in-run A/B of `3` against `2` (PS2_DATE_BATCH_AB_BASE=2, PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=datebatch, exclusive 4x, the user's settings, governor off, 60-frame blocks, perf4_timeline's scenes): M3 J35J GPU -0.114 +- 0.015 ms per stereo frame, frame latency -0.088 +- 0.019 (100 pairs, p99 4.49 -> 4.35); M1 overcast -0.061 +- 0.015 / -0.052 +- 0.018; M18 X-02S -0.060 +- 0.017 / -0.023 +- 0.024 (57 pairs); M2 Su-27 with the intro -0.023 +- 0.011 / -0.009 +- 0.015; the builder 0.03-0.04 ms a pass in that M2 run, 0.13-0.14 in M3 flight (light runs); M3 feedback barriers 52 -> 25 a frame, pipeline binds ~200 -> 110-175 (light runs). Exact: gsreplay images with `3` and `2` byte-identical (M14 sea, M2 intro: fields 120 and after 2 x 300), same-frame eye images 8 of 8 identical (PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=datebatch with PS2_DATE_BATCH_AB_BASE=2, M3 at 2x, ~7 heightfield runs a frame; the A/B re-draws now rebuild the runs when their mode differs). `2` = the planar runs only (nx_date's default) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:17534` |
| `PS2_DATE_BATCH_AB_BASE` | `0` | `0`-`3` | [test] (perf4_passes) PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=datebatch and PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=datebatch: the "off" blocks / the re-draw use this PS2_DATE_BATCH mode instead of 0 (e.g. PS2_DATE_BATCH=3 against the default 2) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:28141` |
| `PS2_DATE_LOCAL_HF` | off | `1` | (nx_date 2026-10-25) Alpha-writing DATE native meshes that date_quad_ok (PS2_DATE_LOCAL_QUAD) keeps on their snapshot copy as not planar (M4's 2 x 2 coast / sea tiles, ~3 a frame; M14 1.6; M12 4-10 by nx_census) read the target in place when they are a heightfield: their triangles (up to 32) cover a convex domain once along the first triangle's main normal axis (the directed-edge test: no fold, the boundary one convex cycle), none steeper than ~84 deg, every eye on the same side of every triangle's plane (date_quad_ok's margins), no sliver -- a ray from the eye cannot meet such a surface twice, so no fragment reads alpha its own draw wrote. Replays: DATE snapshot-path draws M4 3.3 -> 0, M14 1.6 -> 0.1; snapshots 10.3 -> 7.0 / 8.6 -> 7.1, begin-rendering 53 -> 49 / 44 -> 42; byte-identical images. In-run A/B over M4 (PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=datehf): -0.008 +- 0.026 / +0.009 +- 0.021 ms (coast / sea; only 0-2.5 such draws a frame in game); exact in the same-frame A/B (flip datehf). Off by default (no measurable gain in M4; exact) -- `1` turns it on; counters in the vk-count date-hf line | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:17527` |
| `PS2_DATE_BATCH_TRACE` | off | frame N | [diagnostic] (nx_date) Frame N's runs (PS2_DATE_BATCH): one `dbt-run:` line per run tried (positions, tiles, rule, why it ended; the heightfield rule's reason when it fails too); the draws themselves: PS2_DATE_TRACE (nx_census) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:18170` |
| `PS2_FAST_CLEAR` | on | `0` | Turn full-screen clear quads into `vkCmdClearAttachments` | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:19750` |
| `PS2_AFAIL_MERGE` | on | `0` | Alpha-test-fail draws without depth write in a single pass; `0` always splits them | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:39066` |
| `PS2_GS_STATE_MEMO` | on | `0` | Remember the worked-out GS draw state; `0` recomputes it for every primitive | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:4529` |
| `PS2_SNAP_WHOLE` | off | `1` | [debug] Target snapshots copy the whole target instead of the region a draw needs | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:7061` |
| `PS2_EXP_RT_GENERAL` | off | `1` | [experimental] Render targets kept in GENERAL layout so snapshots need no layout transitions | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:408` |
| `PS2_EXP_NO_SNAP` | off | `1` | [experimental] Reuse any old snapshot without copying; measurement only, gives wrong images | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:7040` |
| `PS2_NO_AFAIL_SPLIT` | off | non-0 | [debug] Never split alpha-test-fail draws into two passes | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:39056` |
| `PS2_NO_MIP` | off | non-0 | [debug] Turn the game's mipmap level selection off (garbles distant terrain) | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:755`, `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:710` |
| `PS2_TEX_NOCACHE` | off | non-0 | [debug] Disable the GS texture cache | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:2483` |
| `PS2_TEX_NOSHADOW` | off | non-0 | [debug] Disable GS page shadows | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:311` |
| `PS2_NO_COLCLIP` | off | non-0 | [debug] Wrapping blends clamp instead | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:4592` |
| `PS2_NO_DATE` | off | non-0 | [debug] Disable the destination alpha test | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:4613` |
| `PS2_NO_RT_TEX` | off | `1` = every address, or a texture base address | [debug] Refuse binding render targets as textures | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:4692` |
| `PS2_NO_ZCOPY` | off | non-0 | [debug] Disable Z copies into depth | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:4736` |
| `PS2_NO_ZTEST` | off | non-0 | [debug] Depth test always passes | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:3323` |
| `PS2_NO_FOG` | off | non-0 | [debug] Ignore GS fog | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:39100` |

| `PS2_LOCK_ALPHA` | off | non-0 | [debug] Never write destination alpha | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:39122` |

Before (bug #235), at about half the movie starts the player showed its two picture buffers for 1-3 fields before drawing into them, i.e. the movie before's last picture (whole, its columns x >= 512 left by a 512-wide clear, squeezed into the one 640 x 896 double-height field, or -- after the in-engine ending had shrunk the targets to 512 x 448 -- its x < 512 part at the start of the credits), also in the headset; logged as `not drawn since the game's SetGsCrt` (24 lines, also with 0) | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:5897` |

### Game detail knobs (Zero)

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ACZ_LOD_BIAS` | `1.0` (ENHANCEMENTS AIRCRAFT DETAIL: VERY HIGH = `4` since 2026-10-26; `2` before) | `0.05`..`32` (`16` = official PC port) | Model LOD distance multiplier (aircraft, ground units, ships): every LOD switch and the draw cutoff N times farther. LodModel::SelectLod 0x131118 takes k = camera distance x [0x3F7800] / focal and the first of the model's 5 thresholds above k (none: not drawn), so N is written as 512 / N (bug #105, 2026-10-24; until then 512 x N, which brought them N times closer: HIGH halved the detail distances and the draw distance, see ACZ_LOD_BIAS_MAP). Fighters (thresholds 400 / 800 / 1600 / 6400 / 12800 units, focal 571): x1 finest model within 67 m, coarsest beyond 1.07 km, not drawn beyond 2.15 km; x2 134 m / 2.15 km / 4.3 km; x4 268 m / 4.3 km / 8.6 km. In-run A/B against x1 (sim 4x, user settings, governor off, 2 runs each): M1 x2 / x4 GPU -0.04 / -0.02 ms +- 0.06 (noise); M9 (27 models, 7 fighters at 1.1-3.3 km) x2 GPU +0.04 +- 0.04 ms, host record +0.00, +163 draws; x4 GPU +0.065 +- 0.045 ms, host record +0.125 +- 0.045 ms, +388 draws per stereo frame | `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:2067`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7084` |
| `ACZ_LOD_BIAS_MAP` | `1` | `0` | How ACZ_LOD_BIAS N (the AIRCRAFT DETAIL menu: NORMAL 1 / HIGH 2 / VERY HIGH 4) is written: `1` = SelectLod's distance constant 512 / N, higher = more detail and a longer draw distance (bug #105, 2026-10-24). `0` = the mapping before 2026-10-24, 512 x N: HIGH / VERY HIGH halved / quartered every LOD distance and the draw cutoff (fighters vanished beyond 1.07 / 0.54 km instead of 2.15 km; the wingman in formation at 44 m got LOD 1 / 2 instead of 0) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:6530` |
| `ACZ_TERRAIN_DETAIL` | `1.0` (ENHANCEMENTS TERRAIN DETAIL: `2`) | `0.25`..`4` (`2` = PC port) | Terrain detail ring radius multiplier; costs draws and GPU time | `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:2056`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7084` |
| `ACZ_TREES_DIST` | `1.0` (ENHANCEMENTS FOREST DISTANCE: FAR = `2` since 2026-10-26) | `0.25`..`4` | Tree draw distance multiplier on the per-map table (M2 12800 units ~2.1 km, M3 10240); `2` fills the forests to the horizon (3-6x trees) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7007`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7112` |

| `ACZ_TERRAIN_REAL` | F4 Rendering "Realistic terrain" (on) | `0`, `1` | Realistic terrain T2 (bug #25 follow-up): relief light baked per mission from the game's own heightmap (513 x 513 samples, read from the terrain object of 0x15E1D8 when the map changes) on 4 worker threads (0.2-0.5 s) -- slopes towards the mission's sun lighter, the others darker, gullies down the slopes, valleys a little darker, the terrain's own sun shadow -- applied in mesh.frag as a soft-limited ratio over the terrain's first pass before the fog (the game's vertex colours carry no light at all: a constant 90/128); read per vertex, prefiltered to the vertex spacing, into the vertex colour of the terrain's first pass (no fragment cost: in-run A/B M2 / M3 low pass, M17 mountains within +-0.01 ms per stereo frame at 4x). They grow out of the photo over the outer 30 % of ACZ_TERRAIN_CITY_DIST (16 levels per cell): no pop. What looked plastic: each roof was 2-6 texels of the 6.25 m photo magnified 10-40x into smooth glossy gradients; each wall one flat colour with a linear 0.70 -> 1 vertex gradient over its whole height and a stamped window grid cut at the corners. Now, roofs where a photo texel spans ~3 pixels and more: the photo's mean over the roof (its centre, at the mip of the roof's narrow side) with 30 % of the texel, a faint per-building material (gravel, bitumen, old tiles), a 0.67 m grain, a parapet's coping and inner shadow (flat roofs) or tile courses, ridge cap and gutter (pitched); farther the photo exactly as before. Walls: the foot's darkening in the lowest metres (contact with the ground, the street's occlusion) instead of the whole-wall gradient, the mission's low sun (M6: 8 deg) reaching the lower floors less, whole bays of windows between 0.7 m corner piers up to the cornice with frames, sills, the lintel's shadow and glass = the dark room mixed with the sky's (the game's fog colour's) reflection by the view angle (Schlick), varied per window, a faint trace under the sills, a 2 m plaster noise, a calm hue per building, a cornice band under flat roofs / the eaves' shadow under pitched ones; x0.90 so a wall's mean stays as before. World-fixed (both eyes, in-between frames), every detail faded into its mean under ~3 pixels: far rows are unchanged (same frame, 100 m pass: rows of the horizon band identical within 8 levels; nearer differing pixels 0.96 / 0.88 of the old mean luminance: the glossy roof highlights gone). The building's numbers ride in the records zero_props.c writes only when on (half length / width in quadword 1 w bits 16-31, 0.5 m steps; the wall's top and foot in the position's w, 1/16 m, + 1024 for a pitched roof; roofs as kinds 3 flat / 4 pitched with their corners' local position along the length / width in quadword 1 x / y, m x 16): the same vertex count, no CPU cost. The variant tests depth early (a building behind another is not shaded); the city's draws inherit the terrain pass's alpha test (alpha > 0.75: fragment flags 02276068) but are opaque buildings on land texels, so the variant never discards them. GPU (exclusive 4x in-run A/B ACZ_TERRAIN_CITY_LOOK_AB=10, M6 150 m passes over the residential districts, head 20 deg down, HUD view, governor off): the look +0.084 +- 0.007 / +0.085 +- 0.008 ms per stereo frame over the city (2 runs, 10 blocks of 400 frames each, frames ~2.5 ms; ~0.15 ms on the headset); the whole city on / off (ACZ_TERRAIN_PROPS_AB, with the look's previous build) +0.223 +- 0.024 / +0.229 +- 0.052. | `ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK` | off (ENHANCEMENTS GROUND TEXTURES: FAITHFUL 2X = `faithful` since 2026-10-26) | `faithful`, `crisp`, `x4`, any pack folder name (`1` = `faithful`; `0` / `off` / `game` = off) | T5 terrain texture pack (realistic terrain stage T5, bug #78 / the user's wish for sharper ground; runtime/src/zero_texpack.c, tools/terrain/t5_pack.py, docs/research/2026-10-24/terrain-upscale-t5.md): the ground photo (64 x 64 tiles, 5.3 m texels) replaced in the terrain's first pass by a pack of the same tiles at 2x (128 x 128, 2.65 m), upscaled offline with a model inside each tile's real 3 x 3 world neighbourhoods (averaged per tile id: no seams from missing context; border / interior step within the Lanczos-in-context reference) and back-projected so that every 2 x 2 box of the pack IS the 2006 texel bit for bit: the pack's mip 1 is the game's tile, so where a 2006 texel covers about a pixel or less (beyond ~3-4 km in the headset) the image is the game's; patches the game draws with its own mip level 1 keep the game's texture. `faithful` = DAT x2 (Apache-2.0; sharper edges, little new texture: high-pass x1.09-1.14 of a Lanczos 2x on 2.7 km crops), `crisp` = 4xLSDIRplusN (CC-BY-4.0; more surface texture: x1.25-1.42). Mean colour unchanged (box exact). The packs (one PNG per tile set, 22 sets for the 25 maps, 10-40 MB each) are game-derived and never in git: `ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_DIR`/<name>/t5_<CRC>_<nt>.png, found by the CRC-32 of the map's atlas + palette (a map without a pack file keeps the game's texture). Loading: the pack is decoded on a lowest-priority worker once the map is stable (sim: PNG decode 0.18-0.43 s, box mips + staging 11-29 ms; 30 stable frames first, as the water) and uploaded in chunks of `ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_CHUNK` layers per frame; until then, and on the frame the map changes, the game's texture. Memory: 85 MB of VRAM (RGBA8, 1024 layers, all levels; made at start when on) and an 85 MB host staging buffer; the upload of a 900-1024-tile pack is 7-8 chunks over 50-130 ms. GPU, in-run A/B (ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_AB=30, exclusive 4x, the user's settings, cockpit, head 20 deg down, governor off, 2 runs): M2 30 m +0.021 / +0.022 ms per stereo frame, M3 40 m +0.019 / +0.022, M4 over the sea -0.003 / -0.002 (~0.04 ms on the headset path). Near-ground materials (ACZ_TERRAIN_MATERIALS) still classify by the 2006 texel and modulate the pack's colour; the shader water uses the pack's colour as its base. Exact image A/B: `PS2_PERF_AB=1 PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=texpack`. Shading only, collision unchanged; off at start: no hook, no images, no variant 2x M2: 722 / 637 of 917,504 px differ from the main build, tree-vs-tree noise 640. Headset untested. `x4` (gr_mush 2026-10-28, bug #183: M8 low over grass in rain "the ground like mush"; ENHANCEMENTS GROUND TEXTURES: REAL 4X; off unless chosen): a 4x pack, 256 x 256 tiles (1.33 m texels), made by tools/terrain/t5_pack4.py -- 4xLSDIRplusN's own 4x output (the crisp pack's model; CC-BY-4.0) in each tile's 3 x 3 world neighbourhoods, the low band (gauss 4 px) from a bicubic 4x of the 2006 texels, luma detail soft-limited to +-25 levels, chroma detail x0.7, back-projected with a bicubic residual (no 4-texel lattice) and rounded in two exact stages: its level 1 is an exact 2x and its level 2 the 2006 tile bit for bit. Within ~0.5 km the photo's soft 5 m blobs (34 px per 2006 texel at 100 m on the 4x simulator eye) become texture of grass, bushes and soil at 1-5 m; beyond ~1 km it is the same as the 2x packs. Same-frame A/B against the 2006 texture (PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=texpack, exclusive 4x, the user's settings, head 20 deg down; band energies of the luma, fine |Y - box3| / mid |box3 - box9|): M8 at 40 m, ground 150 / 200 / 300-800 m: mid x1.34 / x1.43 / x1.36-1.39, fine up to x1.59 (faithful at the same pose: x1.05 / x1.19 / x1.21-1.22); M8 at 250 m, 500 m-1.5 km: mid x1.30-1.81 (faithful x1.04-1.31); M2 at 100 m, 200-500 m: x1.31-1.48; mean luma within 0.6 levels, eyes identical. The 2x packs leave the ground below ~150 m as it was (x1.00-1.05). The renderer reads the tile side from the pack folder's first sheet at start (8192 wide = 256): 1024 layers x 9 levels = 341 MiB of VRAM and the same host staging (2x packs: 85), 32 layers a frame by default (ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_CHUNK). Sheets ~4x a 2x pack's PNG; the build needs the map dumps (a private script) and the GPU for a few minutes per set (game_lock.sh + heavy.sh) | `runtime/src/zero_texpack.c:73` |
| `ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_DIR` | `out/terrain_t5` | folder | T5 terrain pack: the folder that holds the pack folders (`faithful/`, `crisp/`; relative to the working directory, the repository root for the launchers) | `runtime/src/zero_texpack.c:87` |
| `ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_BIAS` | `0` | `-2`..`4` | T5 terrain pack: mip bias of the pack (higher = softer: 1 = the 2006 sharpness everywhere, one level blurrier than the game where the game is minified) | `runtime/src/zero_texpack.c:89` |
| `ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_MINLOD` | `0` | `0`..`7` | T5 terrain pack: the finest level sampled (`1` = never the pack's own 2x level: the 2006 texels everywhere -- with a 4x pack (`x4`) `1` is its exact 2x level and `2` the 2006 texels; with the nearest-neighbour test pack `ident` the image is the game's except on 0.17 % of pixels, the tile borders where the game's own sampler bleeds into the next tile of its atlas -- the alignment check; `0.5` = half the new detail) | `runtime/src/zero_texpack.c:91` |
| `ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_ANISO` | `0` | `0`, `1`..`16` | T5 terrain pack: anisotropic filtering of the pack (`0` = the game's mipmapped textures' setting, the F4 anisotropy: 16 in the user's settings) | `runtime/src/zero_texpack.c:90` |
| `ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK_CHUNK` | `128` (a 4x pack: `32`) | `8`..`1024` layers | T5 terrain pack: tiles uploaded per frame after a map change (128 = 11 MB of copies per frame with all levels; a 900-tile pack in 8 frames; a 4x pack's 32 layers are the same 11 MB, 28 frames for 879 tiles) | `runtime/src/zero_texpack.c:119` |

### Native renderer (VU1 programs as GPU meshes)

On Zero the native VU1 path is opt-in: without `PS2_RN_VP` set, `zero_vr.c` turns it off.

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PS2_RN_VP` | Zero: off; AC5: on | any non-`0` = native, `verify`, `0` = off | [experimental] Run the PS2 vector-unit programs natively as GPU meshes instead of emulating VU1; `verify` compares against VU1 | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7031`, `runtime/src/rn/rn_vp.c:301` |
| `PS2_RN_VP_<PROG>` | on | `0` | Turn one native program off; PROG = `MODEL`, `PART`, `SHADOW`, `SKINNED`, `AIRCRAFT`, `FLAT`, `TERRAIN`, `DETAIL`, `LOADMAP`, `LIT`, `FX1`, `RAIN`, `FX2`, `TREES` | `runtime/src/rn/rn_vp.c:314` |
| `PS2_RN_MESH` | on | `0` | Native programs hand the GPU meshes; `0` makes them emit GS packets | `runtime/src/rn/rn_vp.c:13` |
| `PS2_RN_SHADOW_MESH` | on | `0` | The shadow program emits meshes too | `runtime/src/rn/rn_vp_shadow.c:15` |
| `PS2_RN_MESH_MAT` | on | `0` | Meshes use their own fragment shader (`mesh.frag`); `0` keeps `gs.frag` for all | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:38659` |
| `PS2_RN_TEX` | on | `0` | Native mesh textures decoded from the recorded uploads instead of GS memory | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:5352` |
| `PS2_RN_TEX_VERIFY` | off | non-0 | [debug] Decode from the upload records and compare with the GS decode, logging mismatches | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:5162`, `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:5173`, `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:5354` |
| `PS2_RN_TEX_LOG_COPY` | off | any | [debug] Log the first 40 local-to-local VRAM copies | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:1651` |
| `PS2_RN_FXDBG` | `0` | N | [debug] Log the next N fx2 ribbon triangles | `runtime/src/rn/rn_vp_fx.c:135` |
| `PS2_RN_DUMP` | off | file path | [debug] Dump one field of the graphics stream to that file | `runtime/src/rn/rn_dump.c:58` |
| `PS2_RN_DUMP_FIELD` | `10` | field number (`0` = from the start) | Which field `PS2_RN_DUMP` writes | `runtime/src/rn/rn_dump.c:59` |
| `PS2_MESH_BEHIND_Z` | on | `0` | Native meshes (bug #93, 2026-10-23): a vertex behind the eye keeps its homogeneous GS depth (linear in clip space) instead of 0, so a triangle the GPU clips at the eye plane gets its true depth. `0` = as before: a triangle reaching behind the eye had its depth pulled toward the far end over its near part, and M2's airfield -- big blended ground quads (runway, aprons, grass) drawn over the terrain -- lost the depth test wherever a quad reached behind the eye: shown when the head looked across it, gone (the terrain photo instead) when it looked along it (the user's "I turn my head and the texture changes", "I see through the textures"; on the roll at a fixed head 0.07-6.6 % of the eye's pixels came and went). Same-frame A/B M3 low flight: 0.00-0.03 % px > 8 (cloud noise only). GPU, exclusive 4x in-run A/B: M2 take-off roll -0.10 / -0.11 ms, M3 40 m +0.01 ms per stereo frame. PS2_MESH_BEHIND_Z_AB, PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=behindz | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:3005` |

The originals carry a TV scanline overlay -- horizontal lines constant along x (coherence 0.8-0.97), static on the screen, luma only, periods 5.75-6.8 px (OBC shots of movies 1 and 4, 1.0-2.5 levels), 4.79 px (movie 4's night-vision footage) and 3.004 px (the archive footage of 10 interviews, 5-10 levels) -- which Real-ESRGAN x4plus drew as hard dark lines and broken bricks / planks (movie 4 frames 900-929: line amplitude 5.84 instead of 1.97 levels, 2nd harmonic 2.71 instead of 0.06, stripe ratio E_y/E_x 3.37 against 1.75 for the original) and, on the 3-px overlay, black-and-white bars. In those 14 shots (4,430 frames in 12 movies, found by a per-frame line detector over all 90,040 frames and checked by eye) the -tv packs have x4plus of the frames with the overlay removed at 1x (local demodulation at the overlay's period and harmonics, edge-gated, frame by frame) plus the same overlay drawn again at 4x as soft regular lines of the original's period, phase and amplitude (movie 4: amplitude 1.93, 2nd harmonic 0.06, stripe ratio 2.52; mean gradient 2.82 -> 0.59, the Lanczos-upscaled original 0.77: x4plus's extra 'detail' there was the bricks; the interviews' 3-px footage: line amplitude 31-68 -> 5.0-8.0 levels, the original 4.7-7.6, 2nd harmonic 4.7-27 -> <= 0.09). Only the 15 upscale segments the shots touch are re-encoded (5 composed again with x4plus-text's letters). `0` = the packs without -tv (x4plus's stripes). By the PS2 button bound to the trigger: R1 (modern R2) a gate where the afterburner starts (Weapon effect, zones ACZ_PAD_TRIGGERS_GATE, strength 5 of 8), in afterburner a hum past it instead (Vibration 30 Hz, amplitude 2 + 3 x the smoothed buffet); L1 (modern L2) a wall where the air brake opens (Feedback from zone ACZ_PAD_TRIGGERS_BRAKE, strength 2 + 5 q, q = sigma (kt / 500)^2 up to 1); L2 / R2 (classic) the rudder (Feedback from zone 1, strength 1 + 3 q). The value scales the strengths. Needs a DualSense or DualSense Edge through SDL's HIDAPI driver; a controller that refuses the packets is logged once and left alone. One lever counts: a device of type throttle wins over a stick's slider, else the lowest slot. Sim: without load Windows keeps the hot threads on P-cores (E-core share 0-4 %); with load it moves the worker and the renderer to E-cores 15-50 % of the time | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:700` |

| `PS2_PERF_GLASS_VARIANTS` | on | `0` | (perf5_glassexact 2026-11-03) The canopy glass's shader specialised by what each glass draw of the cockpit's multisampled pass can show: cockpit.frag compiled with -DGXV (cockpit_gx[_mv].frag.spv; the plain module stays byte for byte the SPIR-V it was) with constant 1 = the parts it leaves out -- the canopy weather as a whole, the cracks, fx_glass's light, its near-sun wear and glare, its dust, one or both drop looks, ice, film, mist, grime, soot, debris flecks -- and the lensdrops drop layers' empty cells returned after their first hash. A draw takes the first of seven variants whose parts are all no-ops for it, decided from its own push constants with the shader's own tests (gxv_can; NaN never strips) and, per drawing, from the sun in the glass's light block (no sunlight; no dust strength; the sun's image more than 23 + 5 deg from every pixel ray of the eyes' target); so a variant takes the branch the plain shader would and draws the same colour without that code and its registers (plain glass 96 registers; the canopy's repeated shells and the instruments' cover glasses 43, a dry canopy 48 with the sun's image away from the view / 66, wet 80 with or without ice, no water (film, grime, soot) 72, the rest (cracks, flecks) 94). Not with the shading zones' pipelines (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_VRS), the cpspec A/B or a cine recording's view. GPU (sim, exclusive 4x, in-run A/B, PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2: per 120 Hz game + in-between pair; the glass pass): dry (M4) MiG-31 -0.091 +- 0.004 ms, Rafale M -0.116 +- 0.005, F-117A -0.086 +- 0.004; wet (M1 overcast: drops, ice, film) -0.184 +- 0.015 / -0.233 +- 0.056 / -0.191 +- 0.013; Rafale rain -0.198 +- 0.016, grime + soot + flecks -0.164 +- 0.017; X-02S M18 -0.062 +- 0.010 (headset ~x1.75). Same-frame eye images byte-identical with PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=gxv,gwsnap at 4x in M4 (our clouds off: every game frame of dry, a missile's cracks, the sun's glare and wear, light rain, heavy rain + mist, frozen drops + rime, film + mist, grime + smear, soot + flecks), the null control too. Costs ~0.6-1.3 s of pipeline compiles per cockpit model the first time (then the pipeline cache). `0` = the plain pipeline for every glass draw. Measurement: ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB items `gxvoff` (the plain pipeline in its blocks) and `gxa`..`gxf` (wrong images: the parts of ACZ_VR_GLASS_AB_MASKS left out), PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=gxv; PS2_PERF_GLASS_VARIANT_MASKS (test) other masks | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9217` |

| `PS2_PERF_CANOPY_INPLACE` | on | `0` | (perf5_glassexact 2026-11-03) The canopy water's refraction (drops, mist, film, rime, grime, soot over the world behind the glass) reads the frame's own colour target in place instead of a snapshot copy of it: the model is drawn into its own multisampled target and nothing writes the frame's target between the snapshot's point and the model's resolve, so the copy held the same texels. The target (GENERAL layout with the DATE local read) through a descriptor set of its own that names that layout and the snapshots' sampler, a barrier from its writes before the pass and one before its next writes after it. GPU (sim, exclusive 4x, in-run A/B per 120 Hz pair): the snapshot class -0.094 ms in every wet state measured (M1 MiG-31 / Rafale M / F-117A, rain, grime + soot, X-02S M18), the whole frame -0.06..-0.18; same-frame eye images byte-identical (as PS2_PERF_GLASS_VARIANTS); sync validation (portable VVL 1.4.363, unlimited duplicates): the same message signatures with it on and off. | `PS2_THREAD_POLICY` | `auto` (on when P <= 6; this 12900K: P = 8, off) | `0`, `1` | The spin-free thread policy (perf6_threads 2026-11-03; ps2_gfxq.c). P = the physical cores of the fastest efficiency class the process may run on (its affinity mask; on a hybrid CPU the P-cores only: 12600K / 13400 / 13600K / 12700H count 6, 12700K and this 12900K 8; E-cores never raise P; SMT siblings count once). On: the EE's vblank polling blocks on an event (PS2_PERF_EE_IDLE=1), clamp(P - 4, 0, 3) texture decode pool helpers (PS2_PERF_TEX_POOL: 2 at P = 6, 1 at 5, 0 at <= 4) that spin 50 us (PS2_PERF_TEX_POOL_SPIN), the EE rings graphics stage 1 every 64 KB (PS2_GFXQ_DOORBELL), and both graphics stages, the renderer and the pool run one priority level above the EE (PS2_THREAD_POLICY_PRIO); each of those envs, when set, wins. Thread placement stays Windows' own (PS2_THREAD_CORES unchanged). Nothing the guest or the GPU sees changes (same record stream, same decodes: gsreplay signatures and images identical). In-run A/B, M3, 4x, the user's settings (PS2_CPU_AB=8, `policy`; ms per field, A -> B): exclusive 4 E-cores (PS2_CPU_LIMIT=e4, P = 4): process CPU 33.7 -> 20.3 (-13.4 +- 0.7), EE thread 15.7 -> 6.2, pool 3.1 -> 0, stage 1 busy 2.44 -> 2.11 (-0.34 +- 0.04), stage 2 p99 13.0 -> 7.8, vblank -> list published mean 0.86 -> 0.42 (-0.44 +- 0.05), p99 10.2 -> 4.3 (-6.0 +- 0.8), the EE's field intervals over 25 ms 33.5 -> 0 per 1000, the renderer's wait-to-wait over 1.5 periods 40.5 -> 4.1 per 1000; the EE's own work 7.2 -> 8.5 (+1.24 +- 0.10: it is the thread that gives way). Light 3 P-cores with SMT (l6, P = 3): process 33.7 -> 18.3, EE work -0.75 +- 0.13, stage 2 -0.61 +- 0.14; light 6 E-cores (e6, P = 6): process 40.4 -> 29.4. Forced on the full 12900K (exclusive): process 30.2 -> 15.0 (-15.2 +- 0.6), EE thread 16.73 -> 4.23, EE work -0.39 +- 0.04, stage 1 -0.31 +- 0.01, list published +0.021 +- 0.002 (p99 +0.053 +- 0.012), vblank -> polling EE mean 0.7 -> 13 us, p99 7 -> 54 us. The log line `threads: spin-free thread policy ...` names the decision and P at start; `threads-cpu:` lines (PS2_SPEED_LOG) give each thread role's CPU per field. Untested in the headset: check the `kernel: vblank -> the polling EE` lines (p99), `speed: fields=` / dropped and the xr-pace lines | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:357` |

An EE that sleeps wakes at the vblank onto whatever core is free and works for 5-8 ms; with all cores taken stage 1, woken by that field, queued behind it at equal priority and the field's list came out up to 6 ms late. Light in-run A/B on 4 E-cores with the rest of the policy on in both arms (`prio`, two runs): vblank -> list published mean -0.90 +- 0.22 / -0.32 +- 0.14, p99 -4.5 +- 0.7 / -2.0 +- 0.9 ms; the EE's own work +1.15 +- 0.42 / +0.39 +- 0.38 ms and its wake after the vblank later at p99 (up to ~1 ms): the EE is the thread with the slack. | `PS2_GFXQ_DOORBELL` | the thread policy's: `65536` when on, `0` when off (this machine: `0`) | bytes; `0` = a ring per record | The EE -> graphics stage 1 doorbell (perf6_threads): the EE publishes every record at once (a release store of the ring head) but wakes stage 1 only every that many bytes, at every field, before it blocks (ring-full wait, drain, field-depth wait, a guest thread's wait) or polls (INTC_STAT polling), and once per field from the vblank clock; `0` = as before (a sequentially consistent publication per record and a signal whenever stage 1 looks idle). Exact: the same record stream in the same order. Measured: exclusive M3 on the full 12900K (`bell` alone): EE work 4.34 -> 4.00 ms a field (-0.33 +- 0.09), stage 1 busy 1.69 -> 1.43 (-0.27 +- 0.02), list published +0.005 +- 0.002 (p99 -0.001 +- 0.010), 28 rings a field. | `PS2_PERF_COCKPIT_FOLD` | on | `0` | (perf4_passes 2026-11-03) Multiview, the 3D cockpit drawn late (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_LATE): its multisampled pass (the mirrors' real view, the model, the resolve) reads nothing of the frame being drawn unless the canopy is wet (canopy water, mist, film, ice or soot: the refraction samples a snapshot of the world behind it). With a dry canopy it is recorded before the frame's first draw (after the early far cloud update and the first early submission), or, when the mirrors show our far clouds and PS2_PERF_CLOUD_ASYNC runs, at the far cloud point (the frame's rendering is closed there anyway); at the model's place only the composite and the depth-only pass go into the open rendering: one restart of slot 0 and its drain less a frame. The same inputs (pose, lights, shadow map, animations, mirrors, cracks are set before the draws), the same commands; a canopy that turns wet within the frame keeps the old path from the next frame; layered in-between frames (ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_MODE=layered) fold it too (their cockpit is drawn whole), PS2_XR_EXTRAP's layered test frames do not (the model's camera changes at the copy). Applies in M3 (J35J, F-15C) every frame, not in M18 (the X-02S canopy carries film / grime: kept in place). GPU (exclusive 4x, in-run A/B): M3 J35J -0.012 +- 0.014 ms, latency +0.012 +- 0.018 (n.s.). Exact: same-frame eye images (PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=cpfold) 6 of 6 identical at 2x M3, 8 of 8 at 4x in M3 and in M18 (immersion off, so a dry canopy) at 4x; layered (ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_MODE=layered, M3 at 2x, with dynstate): game frames 6 of 6 identical, the in-between frame differs only in rows 0-120 (outside every eye's pass-mask box, row 120 by <= 2 levels), exactly as the PS2_PERF_AB=2 control (nothing flipped) does there: the warp leaves those rows as the previous re-draw had them; the headset's crop (from row 136) identical. | `PS2_PERF_CLOUD_ASYNC` | `0` | `1`, `2` | (perf4_passes 2026-11-03, opt-in) Our far cloud update (classify / args / indirect march, ~0.2-0.5 ms of latency-bound compute a frame) and the shaft slice on a second queue, in frames submitted in parts (PS2_PERF_EARLY_SUBMIT): `1` a second queue of the graphics family, `2` a queue of a compute-only family (our cloud images and buffers then CONCURRENT across both families). A timeline semaphore orders it: the frame's part 0 (uploads, weather, shadow, probes) signals, the update waits for it; the part from the far composite on (a split right before it) and the cockpit mirrors' cloud view wait for the update; the frame's last submission waits if no part did; an update no graphics submission waited for is waited for by the next one. Same commands, same inputs. Sim, exclusive 4x, in-run A/B (M3 J35J, 100 block pairs): the graphics queue's GPU time -0.222 +- 0.015 ms (`1`) / -0.220 +- 0.016 (`2`) per stereo frame, p99 4.46 -> 4.26 / 4.30, but the frame latency +0.276 +- 0.018 (`1`) / +0.088 +- 0.018 (`2`) ms: the far composite's part waits for the update (counted as a gap between parts, not as GPU time), so frames finish later; M18 not measured. Off by default for that reason. Sync validation (portable VVL 1.4.363, submit-time) clean of new hazards with `1` and `2` | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:1010` |

| `PS2_PERF_RT_FIT` | on | `0` | (perf6_renderer 2026-11-04) The game's off-screen target slots right-sized: each slot's colour + depth images (two layers in multiview, and its snapshot) cover what its draws write (each draw's scissor clipped to its vertices' box) and what its samplers can reach (GS sprite quads: the coordinates at the pixels each quad covers; triangles: at the vertices; by wrap mode), + 8 PS2 px, instead of the whole rt_w x rt_h (58.7 MB a slot at 4x). Exact: the viewport stays the whole target, scissors and the render area are clipped to the images (no draw has a pixel outside them); gs.frag's integer texel paths (point, repeat, region repeat) read the same texels, a repeat's neighbour under 0 that wraps to the texture's last texel reads the same texel or 0 in both (the extent is chosen so), and they keep img_size = the target's size (`own`); slots read through the normalised path (bilinear with clamp / region clamp) keep the axis's full size unless that is a power of two (the 512 width in flight), then full size / 2^k, byte-identical in replays (free sizes, and 640 / 2^k with 448 / 2^k, differed in 40-240 of 3.7 M pixels, mostly by 1 level, a few by up to 53); a slot's first rendering clears it to 0 as before. Full size, sticky: the scene, the displayed slot, RT_MFD; a slot drawn by an AC5 native pass, a colour-clamp pass or a cockpit / HUD draw; sampled by a native mesh, an AC5 native pass or as depth; the depth target of a full-size slot. Off at upscale 1, with ACZ_REC_CLEAN / ACZ_REC_CINE (private copies) and ACZ_VR_GOV_OFFRT_SCALE=1. A slot that needs more grows at a game frame's start (device idle once, content copied 1:1, the rest cleared to 0, `rt-fit: slot N grows` lines); never shrinks within a session, except when the target size changes. VRAM of the off-screen slots and their snapshots, VR 2 layers (extents do not depend on the upscale), full -> right-sized: M4 224 -> 97 MB at 2x, 896 -> 388 at 4x; M1 266 -> 92, 1064 -> 366; M3 210 -> 99, 840 -> 395 (exclusive 4x run); M18 196 -> 42, 784 -> 170 (a 5-min soak: 210 -> 77 at 2x), i.e. -111..-175 MB at 2x, -250..-393 at 3x, -445..-698 at 4x. gsreplay final images byte-identical in 20 of 20 comparisons (5 captures: M2 intro, M2, M3, M14 sea, op_threads'; at 150 fields with the replay's 640 width, at 270 / 330 / 390 at the flight's 512), controls too. Renderer: the plan 20-40 us a game frame, 0-3 growths a mission (1-7 ms each with the device idle). `0` = full-size slots as before | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:26968` |
