| `PS2_PERF_TERRAIN_ZCULL` | on | `0` | (perf5_terrain 2026-11-04) The native terrain's overlay passes (block program 1, passes 1-3: the game's two detail textures and its world-XZ layer, the drifting cloud-shadow pattern it draws into its own render target each frame) blend Cd - Cs x FIX (GS 0x162, FIX 128) with MODULATE, no fog: where their vertex colour is 0 (beyond the game's detail fade, passes 1-2; the fog value 0, pass 3) they leave the frame exactly as it is. On: every native mesh draws with mesh.vert's variant mesh_zc*.vert (-DZCULL: one vertex program for all the terrain's passes, as before), and a draw marked by push-constant bit 1 << 27 (ps2_video.c tzc_gate: D = Cd, A / B = Cs and 0, blending on; no depth, alpha or float-target write; not the channel shuffle; no debug colour) culls the triangles whose three vertices give the texture function rgb 0 (gl_CullDistance, which never clips a triangle that keeps one vertex). Fragment census (PS2_TERRAIN_ZCENSUS, light 2x, the user's settings, median and range of 10-s windows): vertex-colour-0 share of pass 1 M3 7 % (6-11), M2 6 % (2-11), M4 3 % (0-100), M18 99 % (91-100), M1 97 % (43-100); of pass 3 (the layer: M3, M4, M14) M3 1.5 % (1.1-1.8), M4 0.9 % (0.2-1.4); with the cull on 0-0.02 %, ~1,050 draws a frame marked in M3. Pass 2 (M4, M18, M14) has no alpha test and writes alpha, so the gate leaves it. GPU (exclusive 4x, the user's settings, governor off, PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2, layered, in-run A/B in 60-frame blocks, mean +- SE over block pairs, per game frame = two drawings): M3 -0.249 +- 0.037 ms (92 pairs; before the rebase -0.250 +- 0.037; the marks alone, the same vertex program in both arms, -0.237 +- 0.037), M18 -0.058 +- 0.030 (50), M4 -0.028 +- 0.037 (88), M2 -0.023 +- 0.025 (84), M1 -0.062 +- 0.028 (91); in-between frames unchanged (M3 -0.004 +- 0.010). Under layered that is one drawing per 120 Hz pair: M3 -0.12 ms (headset, x1.75 estimated, ~-0.22). Exact: same-frame PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=terrzc (the re-draw with mesh.vert itself and no marks; 2x, immersion and our clouds off, the old program's pipelines built ahead: on-demand builds inside the re-draw stall it, and time-driven cockpit effects then differ) M3 and M1 16 of 16 eye images byte-identical (M3 at upscale 4: 12 of 12); M2 13 of 14, M18 14 of 16, M4 11 of 16, the rest on the image border (row 0 of the sky, last column) or in small cockpit spots, the kinds PS2_PERF_AB=2 shows with nothing flipped; gsreplay at 4x on / off byte-identical (M14 at fields 150 and 300; M2 at 150; M2 at 300 synchronously 8 of 8, with PS2_REPLAY_ASYNC=1 2 of 14 on-runs and 0 of 14 off-runs gave a second image, not explained). Needs shaderCullDistance (else off, logged). `0` = mesh.vert as before; in-run A/B PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT=terrzc (with PS2_TERRAIN_ZCULL_AB_MARKS=1: the marks alone) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:1408` |
| `PS2_PERF_GPU_BOOST` | on | `0` | NVIDIA: keep the GPU clocks up (VK_NV_low_latency2 `lowLatencyBoost` on the window swapchain; the low-latency sleep mode stays off, the pacing stays xrWaitFrame's). The driver otherwise drops to P3 (memory 10501 -> 5001 MHz) whenever our 30-45 % load allows it: in a level M3 flight every 2 s for 0.5 s with +1.0 ms on the frame of each switch, for 30 s at a time in an M2 flight (frames +24 %), and after the pause menu, the loading screens or other light phases the first 0.5-0.75 s of flight run at 830-950 MHz (4.7-6.6 ms frames instead of 3.8). Simulator M3: in-run A/B (PS2_PERF_GPU_BOOST_AB=900, 8 block pairs) GPU mean -0.26 +- 0.12 ms, p99 -0.60 +- 0.10, block max -0.66 +- 0.09, frames over 4.76 ms 0.35 % -> 0, latency -0.27 +- 0.11 ms, P-state changes 24 -> 0; three pause/resume cycles: first second after a resume mean 4.69 -> 3.70 ms, p99 7.25 -> 4.21, flight max 7.55 -> 4.59 ms. Same commands (identical images), host times unchanged, ~+10 W GPU power. No effect without the extension. `0` = the driver's own clock policy (as before) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:4187` |
| `PS2_PERF_EARLY_DEFER` | off | `1` | Bug #89 (fx_pacing, 2026-10-23): frames recorded before xrWaitFrame (PS2_PERF_RECORD_EARLY) keep their GPU submissions until the wait returns (the parts are ended and held, then submitted in order; the same command buffers): the recording stays off the critical path, but the GPU starts every frame -- game or in-between -- at the frame start, as it did before PS2_PERF_RECORD_EARLY, so the runtime's frame timing sees frames of one shape. The user's 10-23 headset sessions with RECORD_EARLY=1 (game frames: GPU work before the wait, ~0.1 ms after it; in-between frames: 1.1 ms of recording + the GPU after it): the predicted display time skipped a refresh on 52 % of the waits in 5-s windows with GPU < 3 ms, 26 % at 3.5-4, 5.6 % at 5-6 ms (M2 29.5 %, M3 11.1 %, M4 3.8 %), and the Link stats lost app frames 1:1 with the skips beyond the 10-22 baseline (2583-3483 a minute in M2 vs ~1200 on 10-22). Identical images (same commands). Cost: it gives the latency back -- simulator (exclusive 4x, M2 take-off, 2 runs, in-run A/B): game frames' latency (wait's end -> GPU done) 0.39-0.40 ms -> 4.21-4.35 (RECORD_EARLY=0: 4.48-4.55), in-between frames 4.4-4.6 either way, GPU unchanged; with RECORD_EARLY=2 both kinds 0.39-0.42 ms without it, 4.26-4.45 with it. Headset A/B: PS2_REC_AB=600 PS2_REC_AB_FLIP=p2,+df,++p3 (xr-pace lines per configuration) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32139` |

## Profiling and diagnostics

### Timing and profiling

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PS2_SPEED_LOG` | off | non-0 | CPU/GPU timing windows every 5 s (`gpu-speed: dt=...`), per-frame Vulkan call counts, pipeline compile times; in VR the `xr-pace:` lines (headset frame pacing: display-time steps by frame kind, cadence, in-between alpha, motion evenness, the runtime's dropped frames; per configuration every 30 s in an in-run A/B; PS2_XR_PACE_CSV); since perf6_threads also `speed: EE -> stage 1:` (stage 1's real wake-ups per field, the EE's ms in the signal path, the doorbell), `threads-cpu:` (CPU ms per field of every EE guest thread, stage 1, stage 2, the renderer, the pool helpers and the process, QueryThreadCycleTime) and `kernel: vblank -> the polling EE` (the vblank wake latency: mean, p50, p99, max; EE idle waits and their 1 ms timeouts) | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:114`, `runtime/src/ps2_hle_iop.c:993`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:417` |
| `PS2_GPU_CLASSES` | off | any (flat and multiview); `2` = pass level | GPU time per draw class (native meshes, GS 3D, DATE, cockpit, ...); with PS2_GPU_TRACE also per frame. A timestamp at every class change (~2-3.5 us each, ~1,600 a frame in the M2 intro): never compare GPU totals with it on (nx_census). `2` (fq_opt 2026-10-26): pass level only -- every game draw counts as one class ("GS 3D opaque" = the game's draws), so a frame takes a stamp only where one of our passes starts or ends (~20-40 a frame; M2 flight +0.03-0.1 ms): the split of our own passes, with extra classes "cockpit glass", "cloud far update (early)", "frame setup before the draws", "frame setup: masks and targets", "frame setup: the draw list" | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:6175`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:19733` |
| `PS2_GPU_DRAWS` | off | any (flat only) | Every 300th frame, time each draw and log the 20 most expensive | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:6175`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32484` |
| `PS2_DEBUG_PIPE_STATS` | off | `1` | [diagnostic] (fq_opt 2026-10-26) VK_KHR_pipeline_executable_properties: every graphics and compute pipeline made from then on logs its shaders' driver statistics (`pipe-stats:` lines: shader files, stage, register count, binary size, stack, ...): what limits a shader's occupancy (65536 registers an SM: 96 registers = 21 warps, 72 = 28, 56 = 36, <= 40 = 48). Pipelines are made with VK_PIPELINE_CREATE_CAPTURE_STATISTICS_BIT_KHR then, so the pipeline cache misses for them | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:3112` |
| `PS2_LOG_FPS` | off | any | Log the game's frame rate (fields with a new scene) every 600 fields | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:5944` |
| `PS2_PROFILE_PHASES` | off | non-0 | Per-phase host time report at exit; with PS2_SPEED_LOG one report per 5 s window (the exit report then covers the last window) | `runtime/src/ps2_core.c:647` |
| `PS2_THREAD_PROF` | off | path | [diagnostic] Timeline of the frame pipeline, one CSV line per event (type,t_ns,a,b,c; CLOCK_MONOTONIC ns): vblank, EE queues a field / starts waiting, graphics worker first record / field done / published (busy, E-core and CPU time per field), list published / taken by the renderer, xrWaitFrame returned, final submission, GPU done, A/B blocks; written by a background thread every 0.25 s. `tools/perf/thread_timeline.py` rebuilds each field's path EE -> worker -> renderer -> GPU (stage times, lag, slack, attempts on screen, A/B by block). Since perf4_timeline (2026-11-03) also event 22 per renderer attempt (its stages in microseconds: fence, collect + window acquire, before the wait, xrWaitFrame + xrBeginFrame, recording, vkQueueSubmit, xrEndFrame, the rest), 23 per frame (the GPU's first timestamp on the host clock and its busy time) and 24 (PS2_VK_CENSUS_AB blocks): `tools/perf/frame_timeline.py PREFIX` joins PREFIX.tl.csv with PS2_GPU_TRACE (PREFIX.gpu.csv) and PS2_XR_PACE_CSV (PREFIX.pace.csv) into one row per game / in-between frame (PREFIX.frames.csv) and names the stage that blew the worst 1 % of the frames per scene (latency, cadence, field-to-GPU). With PS2_SPEED_LOG also a `threads:` line every 5 s: per hot thread the share of its run time on E-cores, on the favoured cores, and on a physical core shared with another hot thread | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:464`, `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:645` |
| `PS2_VK_CENSUS` | off | `1` (a window every 30 s), N >= 5 (seconds) | [diagnostic] (perf4_timeline 2026-11-03; runtime/include/ps2_vkcen.h, runtime/src/ps2_vkcen.c) The Vulkan command census of every recorded frame, split by the part of the frame that recorded it, and the pass census. The renderer's files (ps2_video.c, ps2_clouds*.c, ps2_xr.c) redirect their vkCmd* calls through a counter: per part (the GPU classes of PS2_GPU_CLASSES: the game's draws by class, the HUD (xr class 2), the cockpit model / glass / composite / sun shadow / mirrors, our clouds, edge AA, the eye copies, the frame setup and uploads; set at the same pass stamps whether the class timer runs or not) and frame kind (game / in-between / other): begin-rendering, barrier calls and images, pipeline binds, descriptor sets, push constants, draws, dispatches, copies, clears, dynamic state sets; and every begin-rendering with the end-rendering before it (why the pass starts: a target switch, a snapshot copy, an early-submission split, one of our passes), every barrier (layout change, ALL_COMMANDS, BY_REGION, inside a pass), copy and clear with its call site and the caller of its function. Logged every window as `vk-census` lines (per counted frame, game / in-between); per frame totals into PS2_GPU_TRACE's `cen_*` columns. `tools/perf/vk_census.py LOG --exe build/zero/ac5.exe` pools the windows, groups the parts and names the reasons. PS2_BENCH_REPEAT's extra drawings are not counted. Cost (exclusive 4x M3, the scene with the most calls, ~12,600 counted calls a frame; in-run A/B PS2_VK_CENSUS_AB=60, 71 block pairs): recording +0.029 +- 0.009 ms per frame, the renderer's own stages incl. the fold +0.032 +- 0.013. Off: one predictable branch per redirected call | `runtime/src/ps2_vkcen.c:166` |
| `PS2_VK_CENSUS_EVERY` | `1` | K | [diagnostic] With PS2_VK_CENSUS: only every K-th frame is counted (the census' cost / K; an odd K at 120 Hz, where game and in-between frames alternate, or one kind is never counted) | `runtime/src/ps2_vkcen.c:171` |
| `PS2_MERGE_CENSUS` | off | `1` (a window every 10 s), N >= 5 (seconds) | [diagnostic] (perf5_merge 2026-11-03) Which of the game's draws could be merged: for the draws of each frame's own pass (not the PS2_BENCH_REPEAT drawings or re-draws; our passes and the cockpit model are not in the draw list), by population (native meshes of the world = xr class 1, other native meshes, GS draws), what separates each draw from the one recorded just before it -- a target / pass start, a barrier or snapshot, a pipeline bind, a descriptor-set bind, dynamic state, the scissor, other state (viewport, blend constants, vertex / index buffers), a change of kind, the push constants (and which fields), only the push constants' mesh block index (the two 480-byte blocks equal or not), or nothing. `merge-census:` lines per frame kind: draws per frame, how many would be left if every candidate merged (X1 one draw as it is; X2 with re-based indices; X3 one multi-draw with a per-draw block index) and ABAB (the draw's state equals the one two before, not the one before: interleaved passes a pass-major reorder could join); and the native terrain's passes 1-2 (detail textures: how many are wholly beyond their fade distance, i.e. draw colour 0) and pass 3 (the world-XZ layer) with their states. Game frames, light runs, the user's settings, layered default: M3 J35J 2,246 draws (native world 2,068, mean 22.8 indices) -> X1 2,246 / X2 2,151 / X3 2,104, ABAB 985 (91 % of the native world draws follow a descriptor-set bind: each terrain patch draws its atlas pass and its world-XZ layer pass alternately, ~1,000 patches); M1 overcast 1,441 -> 1,441 / 1,288 / 1,275 (push constants: the same atlas, another clamp rectangle); M18 630 -> 629 / 482 / 470; M2 take-off 1,570 -> 1,570 / 1,173 / 1,119. M3 terrain: passes 1-2 50-64 draws a frame (21-37 wholly faded), pass 3 970-1,030; all blend 0x162 (Cd - Cs x FIX), FIX 128, depth key 0x00A (no z or alpha write); M1 at altitude 45 detail draws, 44 wholly faded. Class timers off (their stamps sit between the draws). `python -I tools/perf/merge_census.py LOG` pools the windows | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:26766` |
| `PS2_TERRAIN_ZCENSUS` | off | N (seconds between log lines) | [diagnostic] (perf5_terrain 2026-11-04) The native terrain's draws take the fragment shaders' census variants (mesh_zcen*, gs_zcen*: terrain_zcensus.glsl; needs fragmentStoresAndAtomics), which count each pass's fragments atomically into two reserved mesh blocks (the frame's arena stops below them): `terrain-zc:` lines per pass (0 the atlas -- only patches drawn by the plain mesh.frag --, 1-2 the detail textures, 3 the world-XZ layer): fragments a frame (both eyes; helpers not counted), the share that reached the output (DATE / alpha test), and of those the vertex colour rgb 0, the texel rgb 0, the exact no-ops (output rgb 0: the blend leaves the target as it is), the texel 0 under a non-zero colour, the no-ops by the blend's rounding only (output under half a level); and the overlay draws marked for PS2_PERF_TERRAIN_ZCULL. Light runs at 2x, the user's settings, cull off, median (range) of the 10-s windows: M3 pass 3 779 k fragments a frame, texel 0 83 % (25-98), colour 0 1.5 % (1.1-1.8), no-op by rounding only 0.7 % (0.2-2.8); pass 1 827 k, colour 0 7 % (6-11); M4 pass 3 777 k, texel 0 96 % (38-99.8); M2 no pass 3, pass 1 1,058 k, colour 0 6 % (2-11); M18 no pass 3, pass 1 378 k, colour 0 99 % (91-100); M1 no pass 3, pass 1 360 k, colour 0 97 % (43-100). Pass 2's no-ops count rgb only: it writes alpha. Slow (atomics): measurement only; with PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=K a frame counts K drawings | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:1413` |
| `PS2_VK_CENSUS_AB` | off | N frames | [measurement] With PS2_VK_CENSUS: frames counted only in odd blocks of N frames (PS2_THREAD_PROF event 24 marks the blocks): `tools/perf/frame_timeline.py PREFIX --census-ab` gives the census' own recording cost as the paired block difference +- its standard error | `runtime/src/ps2_vkcen.c:172` |
| `PS2_LOG_STAMP` | off | `1` | [diagnostic] Every log line starts with `[unix seconds fFIELD] ` (CLOCK_REALTIME to 0.1 ms, the clock of PS2_GPU_TRACE's t_unix; the GS field counter); the first line gives `realtime - monotonic` for PS2_THREAD_PROF's clock. For placing log lines against single frames: tools/perf/hitch_scan.py (ov_hitch) | `runtime/src/ps2_core.c:43` |
| `ACZ_UPDATE_CHECK` | unset: `[update] check` of the settings file, default 1 | `0`, `1` | (setup_1105; `docs/SETTINGS.md` "Updates") `ac0vr.exe` asks the latest release at start (WinHTTP, 3 s; offline or rate-limited: silent). `0`: no question at start; the window's button still asks | (`tools/setup/ac0vr_update.c`) |
| `ACZ_UPDATE_URL` | the public repository's `releases/latest` | a URL; `file:///` works | [test] (setup_1105) `ac0vr.exe`: another JSON of that shape, for a mock release | (`tools/setup/ac0vr_update.c`) |
| `ACZ_CRASH_REPORT` | `1` | `0`/`1` | On a crash (an unhandled exception, abort()): a minidump (dbghelp, without heaps and module paths) and a text report with the last 200 log lines in `crash/` next to the exe (`%TEMP%\ac5_crash` when that folder cannot be written); the next start logs where the newest report is and keeps the 8 newest. Nothing is written without a crash and nothing is ever sent. `ps2_fatal_report()` writes the same text report without a dump for fatal conditions that are not exceptions (ps2_fatal calls it; the stall exit and a lost GPU device are the next, stab_audit U5, U8). `0`: only the log lines as before. Costs one sleeping thread and a memcpy per log line (night_setup 2026-11-04) | `runtime/src/ps2_crash.c:430` |
| `PS2_LOG_IMAGES` | off | `1` | [diagnostic] Log every image the renderer makes (size, format, layers, samples, MB, field) and which render target / snapshot it is for (ov_hitch: what a hitch frame created) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:2145`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:4512`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:7027` |
| `PS2_CPU_PROF` | off | `1` | [diagnostic] CPU zones on the host threads (ov_cpu 2026-10-25, runtime/include/ps2_cpuprof.h): every instance of a zone is timed by TSC -- renderer: pump events (SDL, pad, rumble, Touch) and its Touch sync, fence + GPU times, prefill, pre-upload, xrWaitFrame + xrBeginFrame, recording after the wait and its render_list, early recording, final submit + xrEndFrame, window present, pipeline warm-up step / cache save, idle, the whole attempt; graphics worker (and stage 2) busy per field and the display-list hand-off; the EE's work per field; the audio callback and its game / immersion / cockpit-system layers; the cracks worker; and every guest hook as `ee hook OWNER before/after/replace ADDR` (trails sampler, props, syssnd, gaze, VR camera, ...). With PS2_SPEED_LOG every 5 s one `cpu:` line per zone (calls and ms per field, mean, p50, p99, max, instances over the spike threshold; the 16 costliest hooks) and `cpu: spike` lines; with PS2_THREAD_PROF every instance of at least PS2_CPU_PROF_TL_US as timeline event 20 (a = zone, b = ns, c = thread id; names in `<path>.zones`). `tools/perf/cpu_timeline.py zones LOG` / `ab TP.csv --ab 12\|15\|16` / `svg TP.csv OUT.svg --at S --span MS` (thread lanes). Off: one predictable branch per zone | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:531` |
| `PS2_CPU_PROF_SPIKE_US` | `1000` | microseconds | [diagnostic] With PS2_CPU_PROF: an instance longer than this counts as a spike (`over`; per-field zones: over 16.7 ms; waits never) | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:544` |
| `PS2_CPU_PROF_TL_US` | `20` | microseconds | [diagnostic] With PS2_CPU_PROF and PS2_THREAD_PROF: zone instances at least this long go into the timeline | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:546` |
| `PS2_CPU_AB` | off | seconds | [test] In-run A/B of ov_cpu's switches named in PS2_CPU_AB_FLIP: their new behaviour only in the odd ("B") blocks of that many seconds (PS2_THREAD_PROF event 16; `cpu_timeline.py ab --ab 16`) | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:667` |
| `PS2_CPU_AB_FLIP` | none | comma list: `pause`, `vif`, `policy`, `idle`, `pool`, `bell`, `prio` | [test] Which switches PS2_CPU_AB alternates: `pause` = PS2_PERF_EE_PAUSE (8 when unset); `vif` = PS2_PERF_VIF_FAST (the word path in the A blocks; checked every 64 VIF chunks); `policy` = the spin-free thread policy (PS2_THREAD_POLICY) whatever P is: `idle` the EE idle wait, `pool` the pool's helpers and spin, `bell` PS2_GFXQ_DOORBELL, `prio` PS2_THREAD_POLICY_PRIO -- the policy's values in the B blocks, the old ones in the A blocks, switched at fields, with a `cpu-ab:` log line per block (CPU per field of every thread role and of the process, EE idle waits, vblank wake latency, stage 1 wake-ups, the EE's signals; `tools/perf/tpol_ab.py LOG TP.csv --from-field F` pairs the blocks and prints the vblank -> list chain) | `runtime/src/ps2_gfxq.c:669` |

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |

| `PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP` | `mono,dry` | comma list of `mono`, `dry`, `pmask`, `sparse`, `reuse`, `cpidx`, `terrain`, `gshadow`, `optics`, `hudglass`, `depthcut`, `markersharp`, `markershape`, `markerlines`, `cpinner`, `unify`, `eaa`, `cloudfx`, `cloudedge`, `props`, `vrs`, `dyn`, `water`, `msaa2`, `tmat`, `glass`, `flash`, `expo`, `cracks`, `behindz`, `texpack`, `wet`, `gov1`..`gov8`, `govseq`, `mirrors`, `mirclouds`, `mirlive`, `mirreal`, `mirtone`, `datequad`, `datebatch`, `datehf`, `scenelight`, `glassspec`, `nreuse`, `offrt`, `cutpre`, `freshhead`, `fogr`, `compunroll`, `cpspec`, `gssplit`, `wxonce`, `waterexit`, `dust`, `noglass`, `soot`, `nightpanel`, `drops`, `cloudwake`, `fxsoft`, `layers`, `rime`, `cloudsplit`, `shafts`, `compearly`, `cloudsoft`, `citylook`, `burst`, `tunnel`, `dynstate`, `cpfold`, `cvrs`, `cptex`, `cvrsdry`, `dsplit`, `lwcpit`, `gxv`, `gwsnap`, `cplod1`, `cplod2`, `cptexb`, `cdmed`, `cdlow`, `nearspec`, `clouddetail`, `terrzc`, `cqmed`, `cqlow`, `cqa`..`cqd` | [test] Which switches PS2_PERF_AB=1 flips: PS2_PERF_MONO_TARGETS, PS2_PERF_CANOPY_DRY, ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_INDEXED (`cpidx` keeps the old vertex buffer too), the realistic terrain relief (`terrain`: an exact image A/B of the same frame), cloud shadows on the ground (`gshadow`: the same, except the tree sprites, whose colour the game frame carries); `optics`: the optical phenomena (ACZ_VR_OPTICS) off in the re-draw -- since 2026-10-21 the re-draws keep their own cloud pass slots, so our clouds are re-drawn faithfully (before, every re-draw read the last re-draw's cloud uniforms); `hudglass`: the HUD's combiner mark left out (ACZ_VR_HUD_GLASS), `depthcut`: the cockpit depth without the alpha cut (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_DEPTH_CUT); `markersharp`: target boxes from the smooth replacement (ACZ_VR_MARKER_SHARP); `markershape`: target boxes flat (ACZ_VR_MARKER_SHARP=1); `markerlines`: target boxes as shapes (ACZ_VR_MARKER_SHARP=2 instead of 3); `cpinner`: the canopy weather on every glass over 0.12 m as before ACZ_VR_CANOPY_INNER (water, mist, film, ice, grime, flecks; not fx_glass's wear); `unify`: in-between frames with unmatched native meshes on the old screen-space path (PS2_XR_REPROJ_UNIFY); `eaa`: edge AA (PS2_EDGE_AA) off in the re-draw (same-frame A/B of its softening); `cloudfx`: ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FX flipped (the composite's place before the effect sprites and the effect depth stamp); `cloudedge`: ACZ_VR_CLOUD_EDGE_DEPTH flipped; `vrs`: the re-draw without the foveated shading attachment (PS2_VRS_FOV; with PS2_VRS_FOV_DUMP_CROP=1 for the crop); `dyn`: the re-draw with static blend pipelines (PS2_PERF_DYN_BLEND); `water`: the shader water (ACZ_TERRAIN_WATER) left out of the re-draw (the same frame and wave time); `msaa2`: the cockpit model at the other sample count (4x <-> the governor's 2x level 3; since nx_cpit also with the governor off, and 2x -> 4x for a model of ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MSAA_AUTO); `cutpre`: the re-draw with the cut parts drawn the other way (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_CUT_PREPASS); `fogr`: the re-draw with ACZ_VR_FOG_RADIAL flipped (the world meshes' fog and the horizon haze ring's layers) (hz_m2 2026-10-26); `tmat`: the near-ground materials (ACZ_TERRAIN_MATERIALS) left out of the re-draw; `glass`: the canopy glass effects (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_SCRATCH / _GLARE / _REFLECT) left out of the re-draw; `flash`: the flash lights (ACZ_VR_FLASH_LIGHT) off in the re-draw (the cockpit by the light block index's 0.5 flag, the clouds by their own pass slots); `expo`: the eye adaptation's exposure pass left out (ACZ_VR_EYE_ADAPT); `cracks`: the canopy cracks (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_CRACKS) left out of the re-draw; `behindz`: PS2_MESH_BEHIND_Z flipped in the re-draw (native mesh depth behind the eye); `texpack`: the T5 terrain pack (ACZ_TERRAIN_TEXPACK) left out of the re-draw (the 2006 texture); `wet`: the canopy weather (water, mist, film, ice, flecks: ACZ_VR_CANOPY) left out of the re-draw (ov_visual 2026-10-25); `gov1`..`gov8`: the re-draw sheds the frame-time governor's levels 1..N fully (the look of a level; the materials' shedding and the terrain detail ring are made on the game side, so the re-draw shows them only fully off resp. not at all); `govseq`: the 1st dumped frame's re-draw sheds level 1, the 2nd levels 1-2, ... in turn (logged `perf-ab: dump k`) (ov_gov 2026-10-25); `nightpanel`: the night instrument lighting (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_NIGHT_LIGHTS, bug #197) left out of the re-draw; `pmask`: PS2_PASS_MASK off in the re-draw (with PS2_PASS_MASK_DUMP=1); `sparse`: ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_FOV_DEG off in the re-draw; `reuse`: PS2_XR_INBETWEEN_REUSE off in the re-draw (an in-between dump frame: PS2_XR_DUMP_RERENDER=1); `mirrors`: the cockpit's rear-view mirrors as before (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS) in the re-draw; `mirclouds`: the rear-view mirrors without our clouds (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_CLOUDS) in the re-draw; `datequad`: alpha-writing flat native DATE draws back on their snapshot copies (PS2_DATE_LOCAL_QUAD); `datebatch`: the re-draw with PS2_DATE_BATCH=0 (the game's draw order, one feedback barrier per tile); `datehf`: alpha-writing heightfield DATE meshes back on their snapshot copies (PS2_DATE_LOCAL_HF) (nx_date 2026-10-25); `nreuse`: an in-between frame's near clouds marched in full in the re-draw (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_REUSE; dump in-between frames with PS2_XR_DUMP_RERENDER=1) (nx_cloudreuse 2026-10-25); `scenelight`: the 3D cockpit lit by the game's model lights as before (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_SCENE_LIGHT off in the re-draw); `glassspec`: the canopy glass's sun highlight as the material's broad lobe (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_GLASS_SHINE=0) (ov_light 2026-10-25); `offrt`: the re-draw with the governor's offrt lever shed (the qualifying off-screen targets at ACZ_VR_GOV_OFFRT_SCALE) (nx_offscreen 2026-10-25); `freshhead`: the 3D cockpit drawn the other way of ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_FRESH_HEAD (the game frame's eye position instead of the newest, or back) (lb_f15 2026-10-26); `compunroll`, `cpspec`, `gssplit`: the re-draw with ACZ_VR_CLOUD_COMP_UNROLL, ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_SHADER_SPLIT, PS2_PERF_GS_SPLIT the other way (fq_opt 2026-10-26; the other pipelines are made when the flip names them); `wxonce`: the canopy weather on every shell of a multi-shell canopy as before ACZ_VR_CANOPY_WX_ONCE; `waterexit`: the canopy water state of the other model (ACZ_IMM_WATER_EXIT); `dust`: the sunlit dust (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_DUST) left out; `noglass`: the 3D cockpit's glass pass left out (how much the canopy shows at all); `soot`: the soot film (ACZ_IMM_SOOT) left out (cv_canopy 2026-10-27); `drops`: the canopy drops in the other look of ACZ_VR_CANOPY_DROPS (cv_drops 2026-10-29); `cloudwake`: the cloud wake's strips left out (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_WAKE; cl_wake 2026-10-29); `fxsoft`: the effects' soft depth edges left out (ACZ_VR_FX_SOFT; fx_boom 2026-10-29); `mirtone`: with LIVE or SKY the mirrors as TONE in the re-draw (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_TONE_AB); `mirreal`: the mirrors' parts behind the pilot by the cube march instead of the real view (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_MIRRORS_REAL; mir_sharp 2026-10-29); `layers`: the canopy's outside weather on the glass mesh as before ACZ_VR_CANOPY_LAYERS; `rime`: the frost in the other look of ACZ_VR_CANOPY_RIME (rain_layers 2026-10-30); `cloudsplit`: our far cloud composite by the cloud's mean distance as before (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FAR_SPLIT; cl_split 2026-10-30); `shafts`: the light shafts in the haze the other way (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_SHAFTS); `compearly`: the far composite without its early exit (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_COMP_EARLY); `cloudsoft`: the effects' cloud veil the other way (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FX_SOFT: off = the binary stamp; fx_mpbm 2026-10-31); `citylook`: the city buildings in the plain look as before (ACZ_TERRAIN_CITY_LOOK; houses 2026-10-31); `tunnel`: the re-draw as under an open sky (ACZ_VR_TUNNEL_LIGHT; exact for the cockpit's solid parts, which take it by push constant; the glass, the mirrors and the scene light's bounce read the frame's shared blocks, so compare ACZ_VR_TUNNEL_LIGHT=0 runs for them) (tunnel_light 2026-11-02); `burst`: the MPBM burst's volume left out (ACZ_VR_FX_BURST; the game's glow stays hidden: ACZ_VR_FX_BURST_HIDE; fx_burst 2026-11-02); `layered`: an in-between frame re-drawn the other way of ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_MODE (layered <-> rerender; the game frames' re-draws keep the copy; PS2_PERF_AB=1 with PS2_XR_DUMP_SEQ / _PAIR=1: the plain image of an in-between frame is one mode, its _nc image the other; perf4_smooth 2026-11-03); `dynstate`: the re-draw without PS2_PERF_DYN_STATE (PS2_PERF_DYN_BLEND alone); `cpfold`: the re-draw with the cockpit's multisampled pass in place (PS2_PERF_COCKPIT_FOLD) (perf4_passes 2026-11-03); `dsplit`: the re-draw issues every native-mesh triangle draw as two draws of its halves (the exact check that a draw boundary changes no pixel; `perf-ab: dsplit re-draw:` counts them: M3 2x, 2,032-2,181 draws split, game frames 6 of 6 eye images byte-identical; perf5_merge 2026-11-03); `lwcpit`: an in-between frame's re-draw draws the 3D cockpit when the frame kept the game frame's cockpit layer, or keeps it when the frame drew it (ACZ_VR_SMOOTH_LAYER_COCKPIT; dump in-between frames with PS2_XR_DUMP_RERENDER=1) (perf5_reproj 2026-11-03); `gxv`: the re-draw with the plain glass pipeline (PS2_PERF_GLASS_VARIANTS), `gwsnap`: with the canopy's world snapshot copy (PS2_PERF_CANOPY_INPLACE); the canopy's water, ice, film and flecks hold still between a dumped frame's re-draws, so the A/B is exact with them on (perf5_glassexact 2026-11-03); `cplod1` / `cplod2`: the re-draw draws the 3D cockpit at mesh LOD level 1 / 2 (ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_LOD), `cptexb`: through the texture twins of ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_AB_TEX_BUDGET, `cdmed` / `cdlow`: at that level of ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_DETAIL whole (perf6_cockpitladder 2026-11-04); `cvrsdry`: the re-draw with ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_VRS_DRY's gate the other way (the canopy's state and the gate are the frame's own in both re-draws; perf5_glasszones 2026-11-03); `nearspec`: the re-draw with the near cloud march's generic pipeline (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_SPEC 0, as before; perf6_clouds 2026-11-03); `clouddetail`: the same frame re-drawn at another CLOUD DETAIL level: with ACZ_VR_CLOUD_DETAIL_AB_SET set the k-th dumped game frame's _nc images at its k-th entry in turn, else at HIGH (the plain images at the configured ACZ_VR_CLOUD_DETAIL; the rungs' look, same frame); `terrzc`: the re-draw as before PS2_PERF_TERRAIN_ZCULL (mesh.vert itself, no marks; perf5_terrain 2026-11-04); `cqmed` / `cqlow` / `cqa`..`cqd`: the same frame's glass with that CANOPY QUALITY set (ACZ_VR_CANOPY_QUALITY; the rungs' look, same frame; perf6_glassladder 2026-11-04) | `runtime/src/ps2_clouds.c:655`, `runtime/src/ps2_clouds.c:678`, `runtime/src/ps2_clouds.c:705` (+8 more) |
| `PS2_GPU_TRACE` | off | file path | [diagnostic] One CSV line per completed frame: frame, GS field, kind, in-between, GPU ms (without early-submission gaps), our cloud passes' ms, texture uploads / KB / mipmapped, render targets made, new pipelines, governor level, parts, wall ms, frame latency (recording start after xrWaitFrame to GPU done), then the frame's ms per draw class (with PS2_GPU_CLASSES), then (2026-10-23) `t_unix` (wall clock at the end of the recording, for joining an nvidia-smi log), the far cloud map's tile lists `far_must` / `far_vis` / `far_out` and `far_tiles` marched, `far_flags` (1 weather splat, 2 its soft pass, 4 a new generation, 8 the march, bits 4-5 the far map's governor state), `boost` (PS2_PERF_GPU_BOOST in effect) and `p2` (PS2_PERF_RECORD_EARLY: 1 a game frame, 2 an in-between frame recorded and submitted before its wait; its latency still counts from the wait's end); with PS2_VK_CENSUS (2026-11-03) also `cen_begin,cen_barc,cen_bari,cen_pipe,cen_desc,cen_dyn,cen_draw,cen_copy` (the frame's begin-rendering, barrier calls and images, pipeline binds, descriptor sets, dynamic state sets, draws, copies; empty for a frame not counted); tools/perf/gpu_*.py, tools/perf/frame_timeline.py | `runtime/src/ps2_clouds_far.c:50`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:30868` |

| `PS2_REC_PROF` | off | N frames | [diagnostic] Every N frames of each kind (game / in-between): host ms before xrWaitFrame (fence and GPU times, acquire, prefill), xrWaitFrame and its CPU time, marks after the wait (render_list, copies, cockpit scan, mfd_plan, uploads, ov_scan, clouds, gsh, draw loop = first early submission, 1/3, 2/3, render_list done, eye copies, present, end, submit, frame_end), the thread's CPU time over the recording (QueryThreadCycleTime), time inside early submissions / cockpit / clouds / uploads / snapshots / target switches / other passes, and per-frame counts of draws and binds (`rec-prof:` lines; tools/perf/rec_prof.py); marks of a frame recorded before the wait (PS2_PERF_RECORD_EARLY) are negative; every 5 s `rec-prof: display times` (xrWaitFrame steps that are not one display period: the predicted display time minus the previous one minus the period, |error| > 0.5 ms; all waits, the cinema screen's one-a-field waits included; finer: the xr-pace lines) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32581` |

| `PS2_XR_DEPTH` | off | `on`, `auto` (`1`, `2`) | Frame depth to the headset (xr_depth, 2026-10-23; the user's idea #1): XR_KHR_composition_layer_depth on the stereo projection layer, so the compositor's reprojection (positional timewarp; ASW 2.0 on the Meta PC runtime) moves near things with the head's translation, not only its rotation -- the 3D cockpit is 0.24-1.15 m from the eye, and every in-between frame (smooth mode) and every late or repeated frame is shown with a head pose 8-25 ms old. Per eye a D32 depth swapchain the size of the colour chain; each synchronous stereo frame (game and in-between frames) copies the frame depth's visible region (both eyes' crops) right before the game's last full-screen depth-writing pass (it leaves 1.0 everywhere by the eye copies) and converts it in a depth-only pass into one reversed mapping -- the 3D cockpit's 1 - 0.0002 per metre, the HUD combiner's mark, the sky and saturated 2D overlays at infinity, the world through the frame's own projection -- over the same pixels and crop as the colour (edges within half a pixel). No work and no depth for the head-locked image (stick head look outside the cockpit); no depth while the quit prompt / settings quad is up (Link depth-tests quads against it) or when an eye's colour went out without its depth. `on`: whenever the runtime offers the extension (the OpenXR Simulator does, and ignores it); `auto`: only on the Meta / Oculus PC runtime. GPU +0.10..+0.13 ms per stereo frame at 4x (simulator, user config, in-run PS2_XR_DEPTH_AB=60, 2 runs each: M3 J35J +0.099 / +0.129 and +0.101 / +0.127, M4 F-1 +0.102 / +0.107 and +0.101 / +0.108 per 40-block window; memory bandwidth of the copy and the pass over each eye's crop; ~0.2 ms expected on the headset path); memory at 4x 2 x 3 runtime images 2048x2048 D32 (~100 MB) + a 28 MB snapshot (2x: ~34 + 7 MB). Headset untested: see NOTES 2026-10-23 xr_depth for the test plan | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:559` |
| `PS2_XR_DEPTH_NEAR` | `0.05` | metres | With PS2_XR_DEPTH: the layer's farZ (reversed: depth 1 there; anything nearer is clamped to it). The nearest cockpit pixels measured 0.24 m (J35J) / 0.35 m (F-15C) | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:561` |
| `PS2_XR_DEPTH_FAR` | infinite | metres (> 2 x near) | With PS2_XR_DEPTH: the layer's nearZ (depth 0); unset = an infinite far plane, submitted as FLT_MAX as UEVR does (some runtimes mishandle +INFINITY) | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:563` |
| `PS2_XR_DEPTH_FORMAT` | `d32` | `d24`, `d16`, `d32s8` | With PS2_XR_DEPTH: the depth swapchain format when the runtime offers it (D32_SFLOAT is Meta's recommendation; a 2024 report had DEVICE_LOST with Meta's Vulkan depth swapchains: try another format if that happens) | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:565` |

| `PS2_XR_DEPTH_DUMP` | off | `1` | [debug] With PS2_XR_DUMP_EYES: each dumped stereo frame also writes out/eye_<field>_<L|R>_zraw.pfm (the frame depth the headset depth is made from) and _zxr.pfm (the converted headset depth, drawn again by the same pipeline into a private image); works without PS2_XR_DEPTH or a runtime that takes depth. tools/verify/xd_check.py checks them against the colour dump (class shares, distances, edge alignment, false-colour images; numpy + PIL: the pyav venv) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:24429`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:24457` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_XR_DEPTH` | off | N | [debug] Every N depth passes a line with the eye's region, the source target and the world mapping (A, B, zmax); in dump frames the frame's depth-writing draws into slot 0 after the world | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:24430` |
| `PS2_REC_PROF_CSV` | off | file path | [diagnostic] With PS2_REC_PROF: one CSV line per multiview frame (frame number as in PS2_GPU_TRACE, in-between flag, A/B block, every rec-prof value; 2026-10-23: `dynb` dynamic blend calls, `p2` recorded early, `p2_sub` the scene's submission before the wait (ms, negative), `rc_dynb_ms`, `ab2` the 2N block) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32069` |
| `PS2_REC_PROF_CALLS` | off | `1` | [diagnostic] With PS2_REC_PROF: TSC time and count of each kind of draw-loop call (pipeline lookup, bind, line width, descriptor sets, push constants, draw, viewport/scissor/blend constants, vertex/index buffers, dynamic blend state) in a fourth rec-prof line (~0.01 us added per call) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32704` |
| `PS2_REC_AB` | off | N frames | [test] The recording switches named in PS2_REC_AB_FLIP flipped in alternate blocks of N frames; with PS2_REC_PROF the sums and lines are kept per block kind (`game A` = as configured, `game B` = flipped); paired analysis: tools/perf/rec_ab.py, tools/perf/p2_ab.py (latency / GPU joined from PS2_GPU_TRACE; `--factor ab2` for the 2N switches) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:32583` |

| `PS2_PERF_GPU_BOOST_AB` | off | N frames | [test] PS2_PERF_GPU_BOOST on / off in alternate blocks of N frames (the GPU trace's `boost` column; the driver needs ~0.5 s to follow a switch, so blocks of ~900 frames); analysis: tools/perf/gpu_boost_ab.py with an nvidia-smi log (tools/perf/gpu_trace_run.sh) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:4205` |

| `PS2_PASS_AB` | off | N frames | [test] In-run GPU A/B of one of ov_passes' savings (PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT): blocks of N recorded frames with it on and off (the first 4 of a block and eye-dump frames not counted); every 40 N frames `pass-ab:` the mean GPU time per stereo frame of each state, split into game frames and in-between frames, the frame latency and the frames over 4.7 / 6.3 ms | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:17134` |
| `PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT` | (none) | `mask`, `sparse`, `reuse`, `date`, `lrbar`, `datebatch`, `datehf`, `offrt`, `rtskip`, `compunroll`, `cpspec`, `gssplit`, `cloudsplit`, `cloudshafts`, `compearly`, `dynstate`, `upbatch`, `cpfold`, `cloudasync`, `splitsetup`, `eyepair`, `dsplit`, `dsplitpc`, `dsplitpc4`, `dsplitds`, `meshtiny`, `meshscis`, `meshnull`, `meshskip`, `meshdetail`, `meshterrain`, `terrzc` | [test] What PS2_PASS_AB toggles: PS2_PASS_MASK, ACZ_VR_CLOUD_NEAR_FOV_DEG, PS2_XR_INBETWEEN_REUSE, PS2_DATE_LOCAL_QUAD (each must be on for the "on" blocks); `lrbar` (nx_census 2026-10-25, measurement only, wrong images): the "on" blocks leave out the DATE local-read feedback barriers (counted as skipped in the `vk-date` line), so the delta is the most that batching them can save; `datebatch`: PS2_DATE_BATCH (the "off" blocks at 0), `datehf`: PS2_DATE_LOCAL_HF (nx_date); `offrt` (nx_offscreen 2026-10-25): the governor's offrt lever forced on in the "on" blocks (with or without the governor; ACZ_VR_GOV_OFFRT, ACZ_VR_GOV_OFFRT_SCALE); `rtskip` (measurement only, wrong images): the "on" blocks leave out every draw into the off-screen targets of PS2_DEBUG_RT_SKIP (default all but the scene, the displayed target and the cockpit screens' target) -- what they cost at all; `compunroll`, `cpspec`, `gssplit` (fq_opt 2026-10-26): the "off" blocks with ACZ_VR_CLOUD_COMP_UNROLL, ACZ_VR_COCKPIT_SHADER_SPLIT, PS2_PERF_GS_SPLIT the other way (the near composite's loop as before, the cockpit's unsplit pipelines, every GS draw with the full gs.frag; the other pipelines are made when PS2_PASS_AB_WHAT names them); `cloudsplit`, `cloudshafts` (cl_split 2026-10-30): the "off" blocks without ACZ_VR_CLOUD_FAR_SPLIT (the far composite by the mean distance) / without ACZ_VR_CLOUD_SHAFTS (no shaft pass, no use); `compearly`: the far composite without its early exit (ACZ_VR_CLOUD_COMP_EARLY); `dynstate`, `upbatch`, `cpfold`, `cloudasync`, `splitsetup`, `eyepair` (perf4_passes 2026-11-03): the "off" blocks without PS2_PERF_DYN_STATE / PS2_PERF_UPLOAD_BATCH / PS2_PERF_COCKPIT_FOLD / PS2_PERF_CLOUD_ASYNC (the "on" blocks need it set) / PS2_PERF_SPLIT_SETUP / PS2_PERF_EYE_COPY_PAIR; `datebatch` with PS2_DATE_BATCH_AB_BASE compares two batch modes; perf5_merge 2026-11-03 (measurement: what the native-mesh draws cost the GPU and what for; every drawing of an "on" frame, so with PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=K the delta is K drawings' worth; `pass-ab: <what>:` lines count what was done): `dsplit` every native-mesh triangle draw of two or more triangles issued as two draws of its halves (exact images: PS2_PERF_AB_FLIP=dsplit), `dsplitpc` the same with the whole 224-byte push-constant block pushed again between them, `dsplitpc4` with only its 4-byte mesh block index, `dsplitds` with descriptor sets 0-1 bound again; wrong images: `meshtiny` each native-mesh draw only its first triangle, `meshscis` with a 1 x 1 scissor (its vertex work and state, no pixels), `meshnull` its first triangle with that scissor (the draws and their state alone), `meshskip` none, `meshdetail` the native terrain's passes of PS2_PASS_AB_TERRAIN_PASSES (default 1-3) left out, `meshterrain` all its draws. Exclusive 4x, the user's settings, governor off, PS2_BENCH_REPEAT=2, layered default, 60-frame blocks, game frames, both drawings: M3 J35J dsplit (~4,100 draws more a frame) -0.027 +- 0.034 ms, dsplitpc -0.017 +- 0.033, dsplitpc4 +0.011 +- 0.029, dsplitds +0.006 +- 0.028 (frame latency +0.08..+0.10: the recording); M1 overcast dsplitpc +0.029 +- 0.029, M18 X-02S +0.039 +- 0.023, M2 take-off +0.017 +- 0.023: a draw, a push or a descriptor bind costs the GPU ~0-40 ns. M3 meshskip -1.835 +- 0.032, meshscis -1.756 +- 0.038 (the native meshes' pixels: 0.88 ms a drawing of their 0.92), meshnull -1.708 +- 0.030 (the draws with their real state changes 0.064 +- 0.022 ms a drawing), meshtiny -1.225 +- 0.031, meshterrain -1.818 +- 0.041 (the native terrain 0.91 of the 0.92), meshdetail -1.334 +- 0.034 (its passes 1-3: 0.67 ms a drawing), passes 1-2 alone -0.189 +- 0.028 (PS2_PASS_AB_TERRAIN_PASSES=6: 0.094 ms a drawing; so pass 3, the world-XZ layer, ~0.57); M1 meshskip -0.427 +- 0.030, meshnull -0.388 +- 0.027, meshtiny -0.213 +- 0.031; M18 meshskip -0.420 +- 0.033, meshtiny -0.123 +- 0.023; M2 meshskip -1.091 +- 0.026; perf5_terrain 2026-11-04: `terrzc` the "off" blocks as before PS2_PERF_TERRAIN_ZCULL (mesh.vert itself, no marks) | `runtime/src/ps2_clouds.c:1041`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:9023`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:17137` |

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ACZ_SETUP_SHOT` | unset | a path prefix | [test] (setup_1105) `ac0vr.exe`: the window's own image (PrintWindow, never the screen) into `<prefix>_<n>.bmp` at each change of the stage | (`tools/setup/ac0vr_launcher.c`) |
| `ACZ_LINK_VERBOSE` | unset | `1` | [release] (setup_1105) `player_build.py`: the link with `-Wl,--verbose`; `make_release.sh`'s rehearsal gives its output to `prune_bundle.py` | (`tools/setup/player_build.py`) |
| `ACZ_CRASH_TEST` | off | `av`, `so`, `abort`, `fatal` | [test only] From field ACZ_CRASH_TEST_FIELD a new thread writes to a null pointer, overflows its stack, calls abort(), or asks for a fatal report and the game goes on. Exercises ACZ_CRASH_REPORT; do not set it otherwise | `runtime/src/ps2_crash.c:441` |

| `PS2_PAD_JOY` | off | `"dev[@F1][-F2]:<vid>:<pid>:<name>:<axes>:<buttons>:<hats>[:rumble]; F:devN:axisK:V; F1-F2:devN:axisK:A..B; F:devN:buttonK:0\|1; F:devN:hatK:up\|right\|down\|left\|center; F:capture:<action>[:slot]; F:f4:<action>; F:neutral; F:mode:<mode>; noreal"` | [test] (ctl_hotas) SDL virtual joysticks with real VID/PIDs (hex), so SDL's type detection and the profile match run on them, attached at start or at field F1 and detached at F2, in the order declared (dev0, dev1 ...); entries separated by `;`: an axis value (raw -32768..32767) or a ramp, a button, a hat; `capture`: the F4 popup's capture of that action without the window (log `joy: capture:`), `f4`: the window's popup; `neutral`: SET LEVER NEUTRAL; `mode`: the throttle lever's mode. `:rumble` gives the device SDL's rumble capability and counts commands (ACZ_FFB). `noreal`: real joysticks stay out. `@file`: the script is in that file (the environment cuts a value at about 8 KB). tools/agents/hotas_script.py writes them from the profiles | `runtime/src/ps2_hotas.c:494` |
| `PS2_XR_DUMP_PAIR` | off | `1`, `2` | [test] With PS2_XR_DUMP_EYES: `1` dumps a game frame and its smooth-mode in-between frame back to back (eye_<field>_L.ppm + _L_b.ppm), `2` two consecutive game frames (_n); dumps are written by a thread | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:1683` |
| `PS2_XR_DUMP_SEQ` | off | `2`..`16` | [test] With PS2_XR_DUMP_EYES: each dump is K consecutive presented frames (game frames and frames drawn between them), captured without a pause and written afterwards as `eye_<field>_L_s<i>.ppm` / `_R_s<i>.ppm`; the log line of each image gives its game frame, field, in-between alpha and the cockpit model's samples (0 = not drawn). PS2_XR_DUMP_COUNT / _EVERY count sequences | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:1685` |
| `PS2_XR_DUMP_NOCLOUD` | off | kind bits: `1` cloud sprites/bands, `2` cloud deck/ceiling planes, `4` sky dome, `8` horizon haze, `16` horizon band (`3` = clouds) | [test] Multiview: every eye dump also draws the same frame again without those draw kinds and writes it as `..._nc.ppm` (cloud pixels = where the two differ; `tools/verify/cloudcheck.sh`). The headset's frame is unchanged (the normal pass is copied out first, the no-cloud pass runs as a stereo second pass, then the normal pass again); dump frames cost up to 3 scene passes | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:1670` |
| `PS2_XR_TEST_HEAD_SCRIPT` | off | `"field:yaw,pitch[,roll[,x,y,z]] field~yaw,pitch[,roll[,x,y,z]] ..."` degrees (+ = left / up / right ear down), metres | [test] Head poses by vblank field on top of PS2_XR_TEST_HEAD: `:` holds the pose from that field on (a step), `~` ramps linearly from the previous entry's pose to this one; logged when an entry starts. x, y, z (lb_f15 2026-10-26): the head moved that far in the LOCAL frame (+ right, up, back; on top of PS2_XR_TEST_HEAD_POS), ramped like the angles: the simulator's head turns about the point between the eyes, a real one about the neck (checking six over the left shoulder: about `150,8,0,-0.10,0,0.17`) | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:1286` |
| `PS2_XR_TEST_HEAD_JITTER` | off | `deg[,mm[,from_field]]` | [test] An unsteady head on top of the other test poses (rec_cine 2026-10-30): per axis a deterministic sum of sines by the field clock (1.3 / 3.7 / 6.1 / 11.3 Hz, weights 0.45 / 0.30 / 0.17 / 0.08; roll at half the amplitude) peaking near deg degrees, and position noise of about mm millimetres, from that field on: the cinematic view's smoothing (ACZ_REC_CINE_SMOOTH) is judged against it | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:1313` |
| `PS2_XR_TEST_HEAD_POS` | off | `x,y,z` metres in the LOCAL frame (+ = right, up, back) | [test] Move the tracked head by that much (the simulator's head does not move): the line of sight through the HUD's combiner (ACZ_VR_HUD_GLASS) | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:1394` |
| `PS2_XR_TEST_HZ` | off | `R[,S]`: Hz `20`..`89`, seconds | [test] An emulated slower display on the OpenXR Simulator (fixed at 90 Hz; perf6_cadence): from S s after the first frame (default 0), after each xrWaitFrame the renderer waits for its own R Hz grid (the display time minus the runtime's lead at the start) and the frame reports the grid's display time and period (xrEndFrame keeps the runtime's own display time). A grid slot already past when the wait returns is skipped (display step 2), counted `app` (the renderer called the wait after that slot: a late frame) or `runtime` (the simulator's wait itself returned late: an emulation artefact); a `xr: TEST: emulated R Hz` line every 10 s. `72` = the Quest's lowest rate; `60,30` switches to an even 1:1 display at 30 s (the automatic smooth clock turns off). Needs XR_KHR_win32_convert_performance_counter_time | `runtime/src/ps2_xr.c:247` |

| `ACZ_DEBUG_LOWFLY_LOG` | off | N | [test] With ACZ_DEBUG_LOWFLY: log height above ground, attitude and stick every N fields | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3401` |
| `PS2_DEBUG_CANOPY` | off | N | [debug] Log the canopy state every N frames (`canopy: field F water mist grime smear cloud precip gain drop type dens humid film ice frost cold alt frz kt`: drop intensity, C1 cloud type, in-cloud density, humidity memory, condensation film, frozen share, frost, the glass's degrees below 0 C, altitude and freezing level in m, airspeed; then `c1 near body data wet run view`: C1 in_cloud, ps2_clouds_in_cloud (max of C1 and the near probe), the body value, the data input used, the water supply, flight stamp fresh, cockpit view); then `exit model drawn / not drawn: water (beads fine) mist film drop`: the ACZ_IMM_WATER_EXIT state | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:14153` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_IMM_SOUND` | off | N | [debug] Every N s of audio: the immersion sound's inputs, smoothed gains and counts (`imm-sound: ... N drops, whoosh in / out, debris, debris zips / hits / bed clicks (bed max)`) | `runtime/src/ps2_audio.c:734` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_IMM_WAV` | off | path | [test] Write the immersion sound layer alone (before it is added to the mix) to this WAV (48 kHz stereo; the header is filled at a normal exit) | `runtime/src/ps2_audio.c:41` |

## Other

### Zero game patches

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ACZ_RUMBLE` | on | `0` | Forward the game's vibration calls (sceVibSetActParam) to the gamepad | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3955` |
| `ACZ_RUMBLE_LOG` | `1` | `0` / `1` / `2` | Rumble log: the game's own VIBRATION option at each mission start (save slot +0x5F70, 0 = on); the pad's rumble capability, connection and SDL path when it is opened; every SDL_RumbleGamepad failure with its error; every 30 s with any rumble a summary (the game's vibration calls and pattern requests, refused ones by its per-player gate, peak per source: the game, weather / debris, buffet, cracks, the own gun (ACZ_IMM_GUN_RUMBLE); the active controller, the gain (ACZ_RUMBLE_GAIN), commands sent to the pad, failed ones, updates to Touch, the test pads' commands (PS2_PAD_SDL devN), the registry scan's cost (avg / max microseconds per poll), the adaptive triggers' updates and refusals); every pad's rumble capability, connection and path when it connects (`pad: ... connected`); the game's pattern requests (the first 24 and each gate change; `2` = all, and every change of the adaptive triggers); `3` = also every change of the game's motor values and every change of what goes to the pad, by source (fx_debris 2026-10-31), and each effect packet a test pad receives; `0` = off | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:33627`, `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3283` |
| `ACZ_RUMBLE_TEST` | off | seconds | [debug] That many seconds after a controller becomes the active one (ACZ_PAD_ACTIVE): three 0.4-s pulses (large motor, small motor, both) on it, each logged with SDL's answer, on a DualSense then 4 s of L2 / R2's flight effects (ACZ_PAD_TRIGGERS) -- checks the pad, its driver and Steam Input without the game; on demand: ENHANCEMENTS > CONTROLS > TEST VIBRATION (not while PS2_PAD_RECORD records) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:34245` |
| `ACZ_RUMBLE_GAIN` | `auto` (ENHANCEMENTS > CONTROLS > RUMBLE STRENGTH, live) | `auto`, `0`..`2` (or `50`..`200` as percent) | (ctl_priority) A gain on the game's own motor values before the merge with ours, clamped to 1.0: auto = 1.5 on an Xbox-type pad (the game's large motor at 0.39-0.59 is faint on its low-frequency motor; a DualShock 2 renders it strongly), 1.0 on a PlayStation-type pad, another pad and Touch (whose own level is ACZ_TOUCH_HAPTICS). The vibration goes to the active controller only (ACZ_PAD_ACTIVE); weather / debris / buffet / cracks / gun keep ACZ_IMM_RUMBLE | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:33991` |
| `ACZ_MC_FIFO` | on | `0` | Save manager completion queue (fixes the "files corrupted" dialog at boot) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:3971` |
| `ACZ_PACKET_MB` | `8` | MB, `2`..`32` | Size of each relocated per-frame DMA packet buffer (the wide VR view overflowed the original 1 MB) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:1462` |
| `ACZ_NO_PACKET_RELOC` | off | any | Keep the game's original 1 MB packet buffers (overflows corrupt the game with a wide FOV) | `runtime/src/zero_vr.c:7066` |

### Input, audio and emulation core

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PS2_PAD_DEADZONE` | F4 setting | `0`..`32000` | Analog dead zone override | `runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp:1062`, `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:33292` |
| `PS2_PAD_TRIGGER_REST` | off | any | Subtract the triggers' resting value read at start (calibration for drifting triggers) | `runtime/src/ps2_video.c:33302` |
| `PS2_PAD_PATH` | `libpad2` | `libpad2`, `dbc`, `both` | Which pad driver path reports input to the game | `runtime/src/ps2_hle_pad.c:34` |
| `PS2_DMA_IPU_TAG_KEEP` | on | `0` | DMA channel 4 (to the IPU) remembers whether the tag its QWC belongs to ends the chain (refe / end / IRQ) across a stop and restart, as the DMAC keeps it in CHCR.TAG (fmv_fix 2026-10-29). Zero's movie player stops the bitstream chain around every picture's CSC transfer and restarts it with the QWC left; the restart forgot the refe, so when a movie's last chunk -- fed by ONE refe tag when it is under ~1 MB: MOVIEUS 0-4, 7-13, 17-20, 25, 28, 29, 34, BRIEF all but 5, 6, 7, 14 -- ran out, the DMAC read the next tag slot, a stale tag of the previous chunk (its last ~0.4 MB in the other buffer), and the player decoded it: 17-41 broken and replayed pictures (0.6-1.4 s of the movie's last ~2 s again) after the real end (bug: "the video repeats at the end", 2026-10-28 22:55). `0` = the old behaviour | `runtime/src/ps2_hw.c:1036` |
| `PS2_NO_AUDIO` | off | any | Do not open the audio device | `runtime/src/ps2_audio.c:1203` |
| `PS2_AUDIO_WAV` | off | file path | Also write the audio mix to a WAV file (the header is written at a clean exit; a killed run leaves 44 zero bytes, then the s16 stereo 48 kHz data) | `runtime/src/ps2_audio.c:39` |
| `PS2_AUDIO_DEVICE` | headset when the XR runtime names it, else the system default | `default`, or text in a device name (case-insensitive) | Playback device. Overrides F4 > Enhancements > Doppler effect | `runtime/src/ps2_spatial.c:46` |

Overrides F4 > Enhancements > Granular engine | `runtime/src/ps2_spatial.c:48` |
| `ACZ_SND_SYSTEMS` | `1` | `0`..`2` (level) | Cockpit system sounds (bug #88), synthesised and placed at their spot in the cockpit relative to the head (the spatial mixer's head model; binaural in VR), outside parts muffled in the cockpit view: `switch` -- clicks of the pilot's controls where the game has an event (the weapon switch on the throttle, player+0x1ACB; the target button on the stick, press and release, +0x1AB6; the big map button on the panel, press and release, action 0x10; the master arm once per flight 1.2 s after the pilot gets control); `gear` -- the landing gear (the game has none: a flight whose pilot gets control on the ground (FM+0x236 bit 0) retracts it after lift-off, climbing 15 m above the runway at > 150 kt: door / uplock clunks, the pump, the lock thumps nose then mains, the doors; extends back over that runway slow and descending), the airflow with it down, the wheels on the ground (a roll rising with speed, slab joints under the nose wheel then the mains); `flaps` -- the flap motor and end-stop thuds following the aircraft model's own speed-driven flap channel (player+0x804: down below ~186 kt, up by ~250 kt), a little more airflow with them down; `brake` -- the air brake (L1, flight model +0x408): an actuator stroke, a stop thump at full travel, the airflow with the dynamic pressure. The game has none of these sounds (it beeps the weapon switch, which stays). The continuous layers duck under a loud game and 4 dB under radio speech (ACZ_IMM_DUCK; the take-off balance: ACZ_SND_SYSTEMS_MIX). `0` = off | `runtime/src/zero_syssnd.c:103` |
| `ACZ_SND_SYSTEMS_MIX` | `2` | `2`, `1` | The take-off mix of the cockpit system sounds: `2` = fewer layers on the take-off roll and at lift-off (under a helmet the runway and the hydraulics are felt more than heard): the wheels' roll 8 dB lower and fading to a quarter between 45 and 80 m/s (the slab joints' knocks keep the rhythm), the flaps' airflow only in the air and 6 dB lower, the gear's airflow 6 dB lower with a gentler wobble (depth 1.2 instead of 2.5), the gear pump 6 dB, the flap motor 4 dB and the air brake actuator 2.5 dB lower; `1` = the first mix (2026-10-23) | `runtime/src/zero_syssnd.c:124` |

Only passes: it fades in with the speed relative to the listener (20 -> 80 m/s), so a wingman keeping station adds nothing. | `ACZ_DEBUG_SYSSND_DUMP` | off | file path | [debug] Every ACZ_DEBUG_SYSSND_DUMP_EVERY (15) fields write the player record (0x2000 bytes) and the player's unit record (0x4A0) to a binary file (state hunting by diff) | `runtime/src/zero_syssnd.c:1170` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SYSSND_DUMP_EVERY` | `15` | fields | [debug] Interval of ACZ_DEBUG_SYSSND_DUMP | `runtime/src/zero_syssnd.c:1173` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SYSSND_THREAT` | off | `1` | [test] The missile-alert bearing cue takes any homing missile in the game's list (also the player's own), to check the list's layout in runs without enemy fire | `runtime/src/zero_syssnd.c:1169` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SYSSND_FLAPFORCE` | off | `f0,f1` | [test] Hold the aircraft model's speed-driven flap channels (player+0x804 / +0x828 / +0x82C) at their low-speed values (0, 50, 100) between those fields -- what they animate (the inboard trailing-edge flaps) | `runtime/src/zero_syssnd.c:619` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SYSSND_CHASE` | off | field | [test] From that field a crude autopilot pursues the current target on the left stick (lock tones, missile alerts, kills in scripted runs) | `runtime/src/zero_syssnd.c:1176` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SYSSND_WAV` | off | file path | [test] A WAV tap of the cockpit system sound layer alone (from its first mix; for a tap aligned with PS2_AUDIO_WAV use ACZ_DEBUG_IMM_WAV with ACZ_IMM_SOUND=0) | `runtime/src/zero_syssnd.c:922` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_AUDIO3D` | off | fields | [debug] Every N fields: per positional voice the distance, head azimuth/elevation (ours and the game's), relative speed, Doppler weight, the game's factor and ours, pitch, volumes, waveform; cockpit flag and engine state | `runtime/src/zero_audio3d.c:264` |
| `ACZ_AUDIO3D_TEST` | off | `az,el[,dist_m]` or `flyby[,speed_mps[,miss_m[,period_s]]]` | [test] A synthetic positional source rendered by the spatial mixer: fixed in the aircraft's axes (degrees, + = right/up), or passing from ahead to behind on the right at that relative speed and miss distance every period | `runtime/src/zero_audio3d.c:266` |
| `ACZ_AUDIO3D_TEST_SIG` | noise bursts | `tone` | [test] The test source's signal: 250 ms noise bursts (ITD/ILD/spectra measurable) or a 220 Hz harmonic tone (pitch measurable) | `runtime/src/ps2_spatial.c:339` |
| `ACZ_AUDIO3D_TEST_SOLO` | off | `1` | [test] Only the test source is heard (main and reference taps) | `runtime/src/ps2_spatial.c:323` |
| `ACZ_DEBUG_SPATIAL` | off | voice number (48 = test source) | [debug] About once a second that voice's renderer state (direction target/smoothed, delays, head-shadow gains, rear shelf, muffling, Doppler multiplier) | `runtime/src/ps2_spatial.c:213` |
| `PS2_AUDIO_WAV_REF` | off | file path | [test] With `PS2_AUDIO_WAV`: a second, sample-aligned tap of the same audio with the spatial stages off (the game's pan, no muffling, no granular engine; the Doppler pitch is in both) for in-process A/B | `runtime/src/ps2_audio.c:40` |
| `PS2_AUDIO_WAV_LOG` | off | `1` | [test] Log the byte counts of the main and reference WAV taps every ~10 s of audio, and any short write | `runtime/src/ps2_audio.c:56` |
| `PS2_AUDIO_TAP_2D` | off | `1` | [test] Leave the voices on the spatial path out of both taps: the main and reference WAVs must then be bit-identical (2D voices untouched) | `runtime/src/ps2_spu2.c:380` |
| `PS2_AUDIO_SOLO` | off | voice list, e.g. `2,5` | [test] Only those SPU2 voices are heard (the others still play silently) | `runtime/src/ps2_spu2.c:367` |
| `PS2_AUDIO_PROF` | off (summary at exit) | seconds | [debug] Every N seconds of audio: the SPU2 mixer's CPU time per second of audio and its longest chunk | `runtime/src/ps2_spu2.c:379` |
| `PS2_VU_RECOMP` | on | `0` | Recompiled VU1 microprograms; `0` interprets them | `runtime/src/ps2_vu.c:1166` |
| `PS2_VU_QLAT` | on | `0` | Emulate Q/P register latency (DIV, SQRT, RSQRT, E-ops); `0` publishes results immediately | `runtime/src/ps2_vu.c:733` |
| `PS2_VU_LIMIT` | 1048576 (VU1), 20000 (VU0) | instructions, `>= 1000` | Instruction limit for one VU microprogram run (sets both) | `runtime/src/ps2_vu.c:307` |
| `PS2_VU0_ADV_INLINE` | `OFF` | CMake `-DPS2_VU0_ADV_INLINE=ON` / `OFF` | Build time (a CMake option, not read by the game): the VU0 flag pipeline step `ps2_vu0_advance` (runtime/include/ps2_vu0.h) that the generated code calls at 209,608 places. `OFF`: only its "flags pending?" test is inline and the slot loop runs in one cold function, `ps2_vu0_advance_slow` (runtime/src/ps2_vu0.c); same behaviour (3.6 M whole-context comparisons against the old body, 0 differ; GS signatures of replays identical), generated .text 65.6 -> 34.1 MB (ac5.exe text 72.6 -> 41.1 MB), the 62 generated objects build in 85 s instead of 175 s (-j 8; 1,300 -> 510 CPU-s; ps2_code_0013 157 -> 79 s), EE work per field -0.43 ms (-11 %, M3) / -0.28 ms (-7 %, M4). `ON`: the whole loop inlined at every call site, the build before 2026-10-24 (byte-identical generated objects) | `CMakeLists.txt:174` |
| `PS2_GEN_CMODEL_SMALL` | `OFF` | CMake `-DPS2_GEN_CMODEL_SMALL=ON` / `OFF` | Build time (a CMake option): `ON` compiles the generated code (only) with `-mcmodel=small`, so its five extern data symbols (`ps2_pt`, `ps2_trace_ring`, `ps2_trace_pos`, `ps2_loop_ctr`, `ps2_zero_q`, all defined in ac5.exe) are reached RIP-relative instead of through `.refptr` pointers (21,231 pointer loads fewer in ps2_code_0013 alone). On top of the default it measured: generated .text -0.4 %, EE work per field -0.15 ms (M3) / -0.06 ms (M4), under 2 standard errors, build time the same; off until a clear win. Only for data inside the exe: generated code that referenced data in a DLL would fail to link or to load (32-bit pseudo relocation) | `CMakeLists.txt:177` |

### GS capture (F6, gsreplay) and state snapshots (F8)

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PS2_CAP_MAX_MB` | `16384` | MB | Size limit of a GS capture file | `runtime/src/ps2_capture.c:718` |
| `PS2_CAP_DICT_SLOTS` | `8192` | count (rounded to a power of two) | Capture dedup dictionary slots | `runtime/src/ps2_capture.c:714` |
| `PS2_CAP_DICT_MIN` | `2048` | bytes | Smallest payload the capture dictionary dedups | `runtime/src/ps2_capture.c:715` |
| `PS2_CAP_DICT_MAX` | 2 MB | bytes | Largest payload the capture dictionary dedups | `runtime/src/ps2_capture.c:716` |
| `PS2_CAP_DICT_MB` | `256` | MB | Memory budget of the capture dictionary | `runtime/src/ps2_capture.c:717` |
| `PS2_CAP_NORAM` | off | any | Leave the 32 MB RAM image out of the capture | `runtime/src/ps2_capture.c:741` |
| `PS2_REPLAY_ASYNC` | off | `1` | [test] gsreplay sends the capture's records through the graphics queue (ps2_gfxq.c) as the game's EE does -- two stages, or one worker with PS2_GFX_SPLIT=0 -- with a drain after every field, so statistics and PS2_GS_SIG signatures stay deterministic (`replay_ab.sh` in a private script compares the three ways). Only this way do VIF chunks go through ps2_vif_transfer as in the game (the synchronous replay feeds VIF data a quadword at a time through ps2_vif_fifo). Each pass then prints `thread CPU per field` of the worker (stage 1), stage 2 and the replay thread (ov_vif 2026-10-25); the replay thread is profiled as `ee` (PS2_PROFILE_HOST=gfx = the worker, gfx2 = stage 2) | `runtime/src/ps2_replay.c:556` |
| `PS2_REPLAY_VIF_AB` | off | `1` | [test] gsreplay: PS2_PERF_VIF_FAST on in the odd passes and off in the even ones (with `--loop 2N`): the same fields both ways in one process, alternating; compare the passes' `thread CPU per field`, the phase profile (`--bench`, PS2_PROFILE_PHASES=1: the VIF walker row is stage 1's VIF work with PS2_GFX_SPLIT) and the PS2_GS_SIG signatures (a private script rpab.sh, rpabsum.py) | `runtime/src/ps2_replay.c:573` |
| `PS2_REPLAY_TEX_AB` | off | `1` | [test] gsreplay: the texture switches named in PS2_PERF_TEX_AB_FLIP as configured in the odd passes (block A) and flipped in the even ones (B), with `--loop 2N`: the same fields both ways in one process; with PS2_TEX_STREAM_LOG (any value) each pass writes its tex-stream lines (stage 2's thread cycles per field, the zones; analysis tools/perf/tsl_pairs.py LOG --from 0, or a private script rpab.sh / tools/rpab_sum.py). Use PS2_REPLAY_ASYNC=1, and no PS2_GS_SIG in timing passes (it adds ~8 ms a field) | `runtime/src/ps2_replay.c:584` |
| `PS2_REPLAY_HIDDEN` | off | `1` | [test] gsreplay without a visible window | `runtime/src/ps2_replay.c:536` |
| `PS2_GS_SIG` | off | `1` | [test] A `gs-sig:` line per field: hashes of every GS register write, of GS memory at the field's end and of every VU1 activation's inputs (start, TOP, ITOP, registers, data memory), so two replays of one capture can be compared field by field | `runtime/src/ps2_gs.c:440` |
| `PS2_SNAP_NORAM` | off | any | State snapshot without the RAM dump | `runtime/src/ps2_statecap.c:219` |
| `PS2_SNAP_NOCHUNK` | off | any | State snapshot without `machine.bin` | `runtime/src/ps2_statecap.c:237` |
| `PS2_SNAP_NOKEY` | off | any | Do not watch for a `snap.on` file as a snapshot trigger | `runtime/src/ps2_statecap.c:319` |

### External variables

Not read by the runtime's own code, but set by it or by the launcher, or read by the Python tools.

| Variable | Default | Values | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ACZ_DISC` | none | path to an ISO image or to a folder with the disc's files | The disc for `play_zero_vr.sh`, `bench_flat.sh`, `run_zero_mission.sh`, `run_zero_soak.sh` and `sweep_missions.sh`; without either they stop with a message (night_repo 2026-11-04) | `play_zero_vr.sh:105` |
| `ACZ_UCRT64_BIN` | `/c/msys64/ucrt64/bin` | folder | Build time: the folder of MSYS2's compilers that the cockpit build scripts (for `cpit_bake`) and `tools/textures/build_xbrz.sh` put first on PATH (night_repo 2026-11-04) | `tools/cockpit/build_f15c.sh:80` |
| `SDL_JOYSTICK_ALLOW_BACKGROUND_EVENTS` | SDL default (off) | `1` | SDL keeps reading the gamepad while the window is unfocused; `play_zero_vr.sh` sets 1 | `play_zero_vr.sh:114` |
| `ACZ_PAD_LAYOUT` | F4 / ENHANCEMENTS setting (`[input] pad_layout`; classic, modern with ACZ_MENU_SETTINGS=1 while the file has none) | `modern`, `classic` | Controller layout for the session: `modern` = the modern layout (RT/LT = R1/L1 analog throttle up/down, RB/LB = R2/L2 yaw, both = autopilot; the rest as classic), `classic` = the F4 bindings; not saved unless changed in F4 (launcher words `modern` / `classic`; the desktop shortcuts use modern) | `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:500`, `runtime/src/ps2_settings.c:1682`, `runtime/src/ps2_texrep.c:434` (+1 more) |

The F4 settings menu also reads `PS2_ASPECT`, `PS2_PRESENT_MODE`, `PS2_FPS_CAP`, `PS2_UPSCALE`, `PS2_NO_MIP` and `PS2_PAD_DEADZONE`, only to warn that an environment variable overrides the setting shown (`runtime/src/ps2_ui.cpp`).
